The Yogacintāmaṇi of Śivānandasarasvatī
Typed from "Yogacintāmaṇi," ed. Haridas Śarma. Calcutta: Calcutta Oriental Press (no date).
Transcribed by Ramya for the Hatha Yoga Project
Not Proofread!!!

P. 1
yogacintāmaṇiḥ
prathamo dhyāyaḥ|
namo gurubhyo devībhyo yogibhyo haraye namaḥ | 

nityaṃ sukṣmatamaṃ cidekarasakaṃ muktaṃ viśuddhaṃ śivam,
nirdhūtākhilakalpanaṃ śrutiśirogamyaṃ pramāṇīrjitam| 
pūrṇaṃ dhāmavaśīkṛtāḥ sumanasaḥ paśyanti yad yoginaḥ, 
sāndrānandam upāsmahe tad aniśaṃ svātmakāśaṃ mahaḥ|| 1||
māyāgāḍha(ra)mahāduratyayatamo mārtaṇḍakoṭiprabhaḥ, 
saṃsārākhyayugāntabhāskarakaravyātaptapūrṇendakaḥ|
duḥkhāmbhodayugāntacaṇḍapavanakāruṇyalāvaṇyabhūḥ,
bhūyān me nikhilārthabodhanakaraḥ śrīdakṣiṇāmūrtikaḥ || 2||
-----------------------
P. 2
vighnaughatulānalasannikāśaṃ cintārthacintāmaṇicārumūrtim|
brahmācyuteśādisamarcitāṅghriṃ namāmi lambodaram ugravīryam ||3||
śrīvyāsaṃ yatiśaṅkaraṃ bhavaguruṃ śrīrāmacandraṃ gurum,
sāndrānandapadāmbujañ ca nikhilān natvā hi yogīśvarān |
nānāgranthapayodhimadhyapatitaṃ śrīyogacintāmaṇim,
niśśeṣārthasamarthakaṃ yatiśivānandaḥ karoti sphuṭam || 4||
yogas tu nirvikalpakasamādhir eva| tathā cāha – patañjaliḥ
`yogaścittavṛttinirodha’ iti | manovṛttīnāṃ bāhyārthābhimukhānāṃ nirodhapūrvakaṃ cittasya yad brahmākāratayāvasthānaṃ sa nirodhākhyo nirvikalpakasamādhir yoga ity ucyate| vyākaraṇe 'yujsamādhāv' iti| vāsiṣṭhe 'pi —
dvau kramau cittanāśāya  yogo jñānañ ca rāghava |
yogas tadvṛttirodhaḥ syāj jñānaṃ samyakparīkṣaṇam || iti| 
kūrmapurāṇe īśvaroktir yathā –
"yathaikacittasya yogo vṛttyantaranirodhataḥ|
śūnyaṃ sarvaṃ nirābhāsaṃ svarūpaṃ yatra cintyate||
abhāvayogaḥ prokto yenātmānaṃ prapaśyati|
yatra paśyati cātmānaṃ nityānandaṃ nirañjanam||
mām ekaṃ tu paro yogo bhāṣitaḥ parameśvaraḥ" | iti|
---------------------------------------------------------
P. 3
śrādityapurāṇe 'pi—
yogāt sañjāyate jñānaṃ yogo yathaikacittatā | iti|

yogabīje devy uvāca –
yogaḥ ka ucyate deva! yogābhyāso 'pi kīdṛśaḥ?|
yogena vā bhavet kiñ ca tat sarvaṃ vada śaṅkara! ||
īśvara uvāca—
"yo 'pānaprāṇayor yogaḥ sarajomanasas tathā | 
sūryācandramasor yogo jīvātmaparamātmanoḥ|
evan tu dvandvajālasya saṃyogo yoga ucyate"| iti|
atra jīvātmaparamātmanor eva nirbījasamādhilakṣaṇaḥ saṃyogo yoga ity ucyate | 
anye tu  tadaṅgabhūtā eveti draṣṭavyam|
vistareṇa vakṣyate| skandapurāṇe—
"vṛttihīnaṃ manaḥ kṛtvā kṣetrajñaṃ paramātmani|
ekīkṛtya vimucyeta yogayuktaḥ sa ucyate||
bahirmukhāni sarvāṇi kṛtvā khāny antarāṇi ca|
manasaivendriyagrāmaṃ manaś cātmani yojayet||
sarvabhāvavinirmuktaṃ kṣetrajñaṃ paramātmani|
etajjñānañ ca yogaś ca śeṣo 'nyagranthavistaraḥ||
P. 4
saṃyogas tv ātmamanasor yoga ity ucyate budhaiḥ|
prāṇā'pānasamāyogo yoga ity api kaiś cana||
viṣayendriyasaṃyogo yoga ity atha paṇḍitaiḥ"| iti|
prāṇāyāmasya sarvayogaśāstreṣu yogāṅgatvena siddhatvān na tasya yogatvam| ata eva ity api kaiś caneti matāntaropanyāso 'sya rahasyodbhāvanārtham eva| viṣayendriyasaṃyogena nādānusandhānaṃ  bhrūprāṇādinirīkṣaṇañ cābhipretam| etair api samādhisambhavasya patañjalyādiyogibhir uktatvād vistareṇa ca vakṣyate| 
viṣṇupurāṇe—
"yogasvarūpaṃ khāṇḍikya śrūyatāṃ gadato mama|
yatra sthitvā na cyavate prāptayogalayo muniḥ||
ātmaprayatnasāpekṣā viśiṣṭā yā manogatiḥ|
tasyā brahmaṇi saṃyogo yoga ity abhidhīyate||
evamatyantavaiśiṣṭyayuktadharmopalakṣaṇaḥ|
yasya yogaḥ sa vai yogī mumukṣur abhidhīyate" ||
parākpravaṇasya viṣayābhimukhapravṛttiśīlasyāntaḥkaraṇasya viṣayebhyo nivartya pratyaṅmukhīkaraṇātmaprayatnasāpekṣā viśiṣṭā jīvarūpeṇa vyāvṛttā manasā kāraṇabhūtenotpāditā yā gatir jñānaṃ tasyā gater brahmaṇi viṣaye yasya saṃyogo jīvasya brahmabhedacintanāt sa samprajñātalakṣaṇasamādhir ukta iti dvayor arthaḥ|
---------------------------------------------------
P. 5
tṛtīyārthas tu evambhūtābhyāsaśālino viṣṇvākhye pare brahmaṇi manaḥ pralayarūpātyantavaiśiṣṭyayukto yo dharmopalakṣaṇaś ca | 
yasya yogino yogo bhavati sa sākṣānmokṣasādhanayogābhyāsaśālitvāc chāstreṣu mumukṣur ity abhidhīyate ||
anenāsamprajñātalakṣaṇaḥ samādhir uktaḥ | 
padmapurāṇe—
yujir yoge tv ayaṃ dhātur gataḥ kāṣṭhāṃ parāṃ sthitim |
sā tasya yujyate yasmāt tasmād yogī sa ucyate ||

parāṃ sthitiṃ jīvabrahmaṇor advaitasākṣātkārākhyāṃ gataḥ prāpto dhātur na tu tayor yogamātram | sā sthitis tasyādvaitābhyāsaśālino yujyate | yataḥ sā sthitir yujyate iti vyutpattyā yoga ucyate| anenāsamprajñātasamādhir uktaḥ| tathā tatraiva; -- 
nirodho manaso yas tu sa yogo yogināṃ mataḥ|
anena nirvikalpa uktaḥ | yājñavalkyaḥ, -
jñānaṃ yogātmakaṃ viddhi yogaś cāṣṭāṅgasaṃyutaḥ 
saṃyogo yoga ity ukto jīvātmaparāmātmanor iti"||
anena manaḥpralayāpralayalakṣaṇau saṅkṣepataḥ samprajñātāsamprajñātāv uktau| smṛtau viṣṇudharmottare,--
"pareṇa brahmaṇā sārddham ekatvaṃ yan nṛpātmaja!|
sa eva yogo vikhyātaḥ kim anyad yogalakṣaṇam ||
---------------------------------------------------
P. 6
khadehārambhakasyāpi karmaṇaḥ saṃkṣayāvahaḥ |
yo yogaḥ pṛthivīpāla! śṛṇu tasyāpi yatphalam || [lakṣaṇam]
yat tad brahma paraṃ proktaṃ viṣṇvākhyaṃ śuddham avyayam|
cetasaḥ pralayas tatra yoga ity abhidhīyate|
yogas tenāvabodhena pralīne tatra cetasi|
puruṣaḥ kāraṇād bhedaṃ tatra naivānupaśyati||
parātmanor manuṣyendra! vibhāgo 'jñānakalpitaḥ|
kṣaye tasyātmaparayor vibhāgābhāga eva hi||
paramātmātmanor yo 'yam avibhāgaḥ parantapa!|
sa eva tu paro yogaḥ samāsāt kathitas tava"||
atra `pareṇa brahmaṇā’ ityādināsamprajñātasamādhir uktaḥ | 
nanu yamaniyamāsanaprāṇāyāmapratyāhāradhāraṇādhyānasamādhayo 'ṣṭāvaṅgāni yogasyeti, sarvayogaśāstreṣu samādher yogāṅgatvena gaṇitatvād yogaḥ kaś cid anya eva pratīyata iti cet satyam| samādhir hi dvividho 'sti samprajñātāsamprajñātabhedāt | tatrāsamprajñātasamādher samprajñātasamādhir aṅgam | tatrāṅgāṅgibhāvavyavasthākṛtā pātañjale | dhāraṇādīnāṃ trayāṇāṃ sākṣādupakārakatvenāntaraṅgatvam uktam |
yamādīnān tu pañcānām api pratipakṣabhūtahiṃsādivitarkonmūlanadvāreṇa samādhyapakārakatvād bahiraṅgatvam uktam | āsanā-
P. 7
dīnān tu tatrāpi uttarottaram upakārakatvam| "saty āsanajaye prāṇasthairyam" ityādi |
tathā dhāraṇādhyānayoḥ samprajñātasamādhir niṣpādakatayā itarāṅgāpekṣayāntaraṅgatve 'pi asamprajñātākhyanirbījasamādhiṃ prati pāramparyeṇopakārakatvād bahiraṅgam|
"trayam antaraṅgaṃ pūrvebhyaḥ" "tad api bahir aṅgaṃ nirbījasya" iti sūtrābhyām | vasiṣṭhayājñavalkyau tu yamādīnāñ caturṇām apy antaraṅgatvam āhatuḥ |
yājñavalkyaḥ, --
"ādau catvāri bāhyāni tadantyāny antarāṇi vai|
uktāny etāni catvāri yogaṅgāni varānane"||
vasiṣṭhaḥ, --
"pratyāhārādicatvāri śrṛṇuṣv ābhyantarāṇi ca " |iti| atredaṃ bodhyam—yamādīni dhyānāntarāṇi uttarottaropakārakatvena samprajñātasamādhisampādakatvena ca bahiraṅgāni, nirbījasamādheḥ samprajñātasamādhir evāntaraṅgam iti|
samyak prakarṣeṇa jñāyate saṃśayaviparyayānadhyavasāyarāhityena bhāvasya svarūpaṃ yena sa samprajñātasamādhir bhāvanāviśeṣaḥ | bhāvanāviśeṣo yasyeti samprajñātasamādhir uktaḥ | sa ca vitarkādibhedāc caturvidhaḥ | savitarkaḥ savicāraḥ sānandaḥ sāsmitaś ceti| tathā ca bhagavān patañjaliḥ—
"vitarkavicārānandāsmitā rūpānugamāt samprajñātaḥ" iti | 
-----------------------------------------
P. 8
bhāvanā ca viṣayāntaraparihāre bhāvyasya cetasi punaḥ punar niveśanam | bhāvyan tu dvividham īśas tattvāni ca | tattvāni punardvividhāni jaḍa़o'jaḍa़āni ca | jaḍ़āni, prakṛti-mahadahaṅkāra-ekādaśendriya-pañcatanmātra-pañcabhūtabhedāc caturviṃśatiḥ | saṃkṣepatastu prakṛtireva | yā īśvaraśaktiriti māyeti ya vyavahtriyate (vyapadiśyate)| "devātmaśaktiṃ svaguṇairnigūḍha़ām" "māyāntu prakṛtiṃ vighān māyinantu maheśvaram"  ityādiśrutiṣu| ajaḍa़ puruṣaḥ| jaḍa़ānyapi dvividhāni sūkṣmāṇi sthūlāni ca| tatra yadā prathamaṃ sthalāni mahābhūtendriyāṇi viṣayatvenādāya bhāvanākriyate pūrvāparānusandhānaśabdollekhasambhedena tadā savitarkasaṃjñitaḥ| tathā sūkṣmāntaḥkaraṇaviṣayamavalambaya deśakāladharmāvacchinnā bhāvanā cettadā savicārasaṃjñitaḥ|
tadanavacchinnā dharmimātraviṣayā cettadā nirvicārākhyasamādhirayaṃ grāhya samādhigrāhyasamāpattiriticābhidhauyate|
yadā rajastamo leśānuviddhamantaḥkaraṇasattvaṃ bhāvyate tadā guṇābhāvāccicchakteḥ sukhaprakāśamayasattvasyodrekāt sānandākhyaḥ samādhirbhavati| etanmātradhṛtayaśca videhā ityucyante |
----------------------------------
P. 9
ataḥ paraṃ ratastamoleśānabhibhūtaṃ śuddhasattvamālambanīkṛtya yā pravartate bhāvanāṃ tasyāṃ grāhyasya sattvasya nyagbhāvāccitiśakterudrekātsattāmātrāvaśeṣatvena samādhiḥ sāsmita ityucyate| na cāhaṃkārāsmitayorabhedaḥ śaṅkanīyaḥ| yato yatrāntaḥ karaṇamahamityullekhena viṣayānvedayate so'haṅkāraḥ| yatra tvantarmukhatayā pratiloma pariṇāme prakṛtilīne cetasi sattāmātramavabhāti sā'smitā iti etānmātraratayastu prakṛtisayaśabdavācyāḥ| ye tu paraṃ puruṣaṃ jñātvā bhāvanāyāṃ pravartante teṣāmiyaṃ vivekakhyātirgrahītṛsamāpattirityucyate|
caturavastho'yaṃ saṃprajñātasamādhiḥ –savikalpa iti sabīja iti nididhyāsanamityādiśabdairvyapadiśyate yogaśāstreṣu| asaṃprajñātasamādhelakṣaṇamāha, bhagavān patañjaliḥ—"virāmapratyayābhyāsapūrvaḥ saṃskāraśeṣo'nya" iti | asyārthaḥ, "viramyate aneneti virāmo vitarkādicintātyāgaḥ, virāmaścāsau pratyayaśceti virāmapratyayaḥ tasyābhyāsaḥ paunaḥ punyena cetasi niveśanam| tatra yā kāciccittavṛttirullasati tasyā neti netīti nairantaryaṇa paryudasanam tatpūrvakaḥ samādhiḥ saṃskāraśeṣo'nyastadavilakṣaṇo'saṃprajñātasamādhirityarthaḥ
na tatra kiñcidvedyaṃ saṃprajñāyata  ityasaṃprajñātaḥ samādhiḥ| ayaṃ nirvīja iti nirvikalpa iti nirālamba iti rājayoga- iti cocyate| rājayoge vāmadeva uvāca, --"
----------------------------------------------
P.10
"bhagavan devadeveśa paramānandasundara!|
tvatprasādānmayā labdhaḥ pūrvayogaḥ savistaraḥ||
aparaḥ kastamākhyāhi bhavatā ya udīritaḥ"|
śrīmahādeva uvāca—
"vahirmudrānvitaḥ pūrvo vahiryogasya tanmayaḥ| 
antarmudrānvitastvanyo'ntaryogaḥ sa eva hi||
rājayogaśca kathitaḥ sa eva munipuṅgavaiḥ|
rājate sarvayoge ca rājayoga iti smṛtaḥ||
rājānaṃ dīpyamānaṃ taṃ parabrahmāṇamavyayam|
dehinaṃ prāpayedyastu rājayogaḥ sa ucyate"||
samādhidvayasādhāraṇanāmāni|
haṭapradīpikāyām—
"rājayogaḥ samādhiśca unmanī ca manonmanī|
amaro'sau sa cāndrīva nirālambaṃ niṃrañjanam||
amanasko layaścaiva śūnyāśūnyaṃ parāparam|
jīvanmuktiṃ sahajañca turyaṃ cetyekavācakam ||" iti|
darśitaṃ codāhṛtaṃ purāṇādivākyeṣu samādhidvaividhyaṃ pūrvameva nanu -- "mantrayogo layaścaiva rājayogo haṭhastathā|
yogaścaturvidhaḥ prokto yogibhistattvadarśibhiḥ||"
iti yogaścaturvidhaḥ prokto yogibhistattvadarśibhiḥ||"
iti yogasya caturvidhatvaśravaṇād brahma-viṣṇu-īśvara-śivādigītāsu karmayogo bhaktiyogo vairāgyayogaḥ sāṃkhyayoga ityādiṣu yogaśabdaprayogadarśanena ca yogaśabdasyānekārthatvapratīteḥ kathaṃ nirṇayo'yamevayoga iti cetsatyaṃ jīvanasādhane jīvanaśabdaprayogavat|

P.11
sākṣātparamparayo yogasādhaneṣu mantrayogādiṣu yogaśabdasya prayogopapatteḥ| tathāhi, --
mantrajapahomapūjādiṣu samārādhiteśvara-prasādādeva yogoyogasiddhiśca bhavati nānyathā| ādityapurāṇe śivavākyam, --
"sarvajñaḥ paripūrṇaśca jarābharaṇavarjitaḥ|
matprasādādbhaveda yogī nānyathā krauñcasūdana! ||" iti| 
mānasollāse, --
"guruprasādāllabhate yogamaṣṭāṅgalakṣaṇam|
śiva prasādāllabhate yogasiddhiñca śāśvatīm"|| iti|
śaive, -- "brahma-viṣṇu-śivādīnāṃ mantrajāpyaviśāradaiḥ|
sādhyate mantrayogaḥ sa vatsarājādibhiryathā"|| iti |
kūrmapurāṇe, --
"jñānārthī jñānamāpnoti mokṣārthī ca viśeṣataḥ|
saṃsma(sa)reddhi virūpākṣaṃ sarvakāma-phalapradam"|| iti |
kiñceśvaramantrajapapūjādīnāṃ sarvayogaśāstreṣu yogāṅgatvaṃ ṣpaṣṭameva dṛśyate| tathāca vakṣyate, --
rājayogastu nirbījasamādhireva, layaḥ samprajñātaḥ| haṭhastu prāṇāyāma eva |
tathāca yogavīje, --
"mantro haṭho layorāja-yogaste bhūmikā kramāt|
eka eva caturddhāyaṃ 'yaṃ mahāyogo 'bhidhīyate "||
----------------------------------
P.12
devyuvāca, --
"kathayedaṃ mahādeva ! yogatattvaṃ caturvidham|
bhūmikāṃśānusāreṇa yathābhūtaṃ kramānmama"||
īśvara uvāca—
"sakāreṇa vahiryāti hakāreṇa viśenmaruta|
haṃsahaṃsetyamuṃ mantraṃ jīvo japati sarvadā||
guruvākyātsuṣumnāyāṃ viparīto bhavejjapaḥ|
so 'haṃ so 'hamiti prokto mantrayogastaducyate||
pratītirmantrayogocca jāyate paścime pathi|
hakāreṇaiva sūryo 'sau ṭhakāreṇendurucyate||
sūryācandramasoścaikyaṃ haṭha ityabhidhīyate|
haṭhena prāpyate jāḍyaṃ sarvadoṣasamudbhavam ||
kṣetrajñaḥ paramātmā ca tayoraikyaṃ yadā bhaveta|
tadaikye sādhite devi! cittaṃ yāti vilīnatām ||
pavanaḥ sthairyamāyāti layayogodaye sati|
layātsamprāpyate siddhiḥ sānandaṃ paramaṃ padam ||
aṇimādipadaṃ prāpto rājate rājayogataḥ|
prāṇāyāma-samāyoge jñeyaṃ yogacatuṣṭayam ||"
pūrvaṃ vāhyo mantrayogaḥ prokto 'yantvāntara iti viśeṣaḥ| sūryācandrākhyayoḥ prāṇā 'pānayoraikyalakṣaṇaḥ prāṇāyāmo haṭhayoga-ityucyate|
vāsiṣṭhe, --
apānaścandramā dehamāpyāyayati vāhyataḥ|
prāṇaḥ sūryo 'thavā vahkiḥ pacatyantamidaṃ vapuḥ|"
--------------------------------------
P.13
matāntare tu –
viparītāsaṃjñā 'nayoḥ|
yathā amṛtasiddhau, --
"prāṇaścandramayo jñeyo 'pānaḥ sūryamayastathā|
ubhī śarīra-netārau yātāyātaṃ prakurvataḥ||
anayoryatnato yogaḥ sadābhyasyo mumukṣubhiḥ|
etadudvāreṇa dhīrāṇāṃ sarvasiddhiḥ prajāyate|| "
"prāṇāpānagatīruḍghā prāṇāyāmaparāyaṇāḥ" iti gītāvacanāt  "prāṇāpānasamāyogaḥ prāṇāyāma iti smṛta" iti yājñavalkyavacanācca prāṇāyāmapadaṃ haṭhaśabdenocyate|
yasyāṃ bhāvanāyāṃ paramātmani līnasya yogino brahmānandāvirbhāvojāyate| sa bhāvanāviśeṣaḥ samprajñātalakṣaṇaḥ samādhirlayayoga ityucyate|
layayoge'pi navaṣveva cakreṣu marunmanasorlayo layayoga ityucyate| yenāṇimādiparamaiśvarya-sampannoyogī tribhūvane rājate| sa ca nirbījamādhirūpo  rājayoga ityucyate|
skandapurāṇe, --
"prāṇāpānavaśojīva ūrḍghādhaḥ paridhāvati|
vāmadakṣiṇamārgeṇa cañcalo na sthitiṃ labheta||
guṇabaddho yathāpakṣī gato'pyākṛṣyate punaḥ|
guṇairbarddhastathā jīvaḥ prāṇāpānena kṛṣyate||
-------------------------------------
P.14
apānaḥ karṣati prāṇaṃ prāṇo'pānañca karṣati|
ūrḍghādhaḥ saṃsthitāvetau saṃyojayati tattvavida||
hakāreṇa vahiryāti sakāreṇa viśetpunaḥ|
haṃsa haṃsetyamuṃ mantraṃ jīvo japati sarvadā||
ṣaṭśatāni divārātrī sahasrāṇyekaviṃśati|
etatsaṃkhyānvitaṃ mantraṃ jīvo japati sarvadā||
ajapā nāma gāyatrī yogināṃ yogadāyinī|
asyā saṅkalpamātreṇa sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate||
anayā sadṛśī vidyā nā'nayā sadṛśo japaḥ|
anayā sadṛśaṃ jñānaṃ na bhūtaṃ na bhaviṣyati||"
trayo'pi rājayogāṅgameva |
haṭhapradīpikāyām, --
pīṭhādikumbhakāścitrā divyāni karaṇāni ca |
sarve laya haṭhābhyāsā rājayogasya siddhaye||
rājayogasamārūḍha़ḥ puruṣaḥ kālavañcakaḥ| " iti|
atrādipadena ṣaṭkarmāṇi gṛhyante| pīṭhapadaṃ yamaniyamayorapyupalakṣaṇam| tayoḥ sarvayogaśāstreṣu yogāṅgatvenotvāt|
ata yadyapi śloke yamaniyamādīnāṃ sarveṣāmeva sākṣādeva rājayogopakārakatvaṃ pratīyate,tathāpi pūrvāparagranthaparyālocanayānaivaṃ siddhāntaḥ| yamādīnāmaṣṭānāṃ sākṣātparamparayā samādhyupayogatvaṃ pūrvamevoktam| tatra ṣaṭkarmamudrā upariṣṭhādvakṣyante|
tatra ṣaṭkarmaṇāṃ śleṣmādidoṣanivārakatvadvārā| doṣāmāve nāstyeva|
-------------------------------------------------
P.15
anye tu –satyapi doṣe'nekāpāyasaṅkulatvāt ṣaṭkarmaṇāmanuṣṭhānamanicchintaḥ prāṇāyāmaireva sarvakāryasiddhiḥ|
ato haṭhasyāniyatāṅgatvamityāhuḥ|
tathācātmārāmaḥ—
ṣaṭkarmanirgataḥ sthaulyakapha-prado gadādikaḥ?|
prāṇāyāmaṃ tataḥ kuryādanāyāsena siddhayati||
medaśleṣmanivṛttyarthaṃ ṣaṭkāryāṇi samācaret|
anyathā nācarettāni doṣāṇāṃ samatā yataḥ||"
`prāṇāyāmaireva praśuṣyanti malā’ iti keṣāñcidāryāṇāmanyat karma nasanmatamiti| mudrāṇāntu mūlabandha-jālabandhakoḍaḍīyānamahāmudrāmahāvedha-mahābandhaśakticālanānāṃ sākṣādeva haṭhopakārakatvam| viparītakaraṇīkhecaryostu śarīra śu(si) ddhidvāreti vivekaḥ|
vāsiṣṭhe tu, --khecaryā api sākṣādevopayogitvamuktam| tathāhi--"tālumūlagatāṃ yatnājjihvayā''kramya ghaṇṭikām|
ūrddharandhrāgate prāṇe prāṇaspando nirudhyata  || " iti|
mānasollāse, --
"ākañcanamapānasya prāṇasya ca niroghanam|
lambikopari jihvāyāḥ sthāpanaṃ yogasādhana" miti ||
nandikeśvara-tārāvalyām, --
"uḍaḍīna-jālandharamūlabandhi-
runnidritāyāmbhujagāṅganāyām|
pratyagvimuñcan praviśan suṣumnām,
gamāgamau muñcati gandhavāhaḥ"|| iti|
-------------------------------------------
P.16
haṭhapradīpikāyām, --
"vividhairāsanaiḥ kumbhi-vicitra-karaṇairapi|
prabuddhāyāmādiśaktau prāṇaḥśūnye vilīyate||
utpannaśaktibodhasya tyaktaniḥśeṣakarṣaṇaḥ|
yoginaḥ sahajāvasthāsvayameva prajāyate||
suṣumnāvāhinī prāṇe śūnye viśati mārute|
tadā samastakaraṇāni nirmūlayati yogavit||"
vāsiṣṭhai, --
"gudādisthānasaṃkocāt sthānakādikriyākramaiḥ|
bhajanāsana śuddhyā ca sādhuśāstrārtha bhāvanāt||
ācārāttu janāsaṅgātsarvatyāgātsukhāsanāt|
prāṇāyāmasya hyabhyāsādrāma| kālena kenacit||
kopalobhaparityāgāda bhogatyāgaśca subrata|
tyāgādāna nirodheṣu bhṛśaṃ yānti vidheyatām||
prāṇāḥ prabhutvāttajjñasya puṃso bhṛtyā ivākhilāḥ|" iti | nanu mudrā haṭha yogādīnāṃ bahuphalaśravaṇātprādhānyaṃ kiṃ na syāditi cenna tasyārthabādhakatvāt| tathāca pūrvamīmāṃsāyām, "aṃśeṣuphalaśrutirarthavādaḥ, phalavatsannidhāvaphalaṃ tadaṅgamiti" | karmamayogastvīśvarārādhanameva| tacca yogāṅgabhūtaniyama madhye gaṇitamasti sarvayogaśāstreṣu| tathāca pātañjalasūtradvayam, --
"tapaḥ khādhyāyeśvarapraṇidhānāni kriyāyogaḥ" |
"śaucasantoṣatapaḥ svādhyāyeśvarapraṇidhānāni niyamāḥ  iti|"
------------------------------------------
P. 17
tapaḥ kṛcchracāndrāyaṇādi| svādhyāyaḥ praṇavādijapaḥ| praṇidhānaṃ parameśvare phalanirapekṣatayā sarvakarmārpaṇam| ete kriyāyogā ityucyante| etadanuṣṭhānena samādhirbhavati| tatra kriyāyogasya samādhyupakārakatve dvāramāha, -- patañjatiḥ "samādhibhāvanārthaḥ kleśatanūkaraṇārthaśce"ti| kleśā "avidyāsmitārāgadveṣābhiniveśāḥ| tatrāvidyā atasmiṃstadbuddhirūpābhrānti- "ranityāśuciduḥkhānātmasu nityaśucisukhātmakhyāti" rūpā'smitādīnāṃ catūrṇāṃ kleśānāṃ prasavabhūmiḥ| asmiṃstadbuddhirūpābhrānti - "ranityāśuciduḥkhānātmasu nityaśucisukhātmakhyāti"rūpā'smitādīnāṃ catūrṇāṃ kleśānāṃ prasavabhūmiḥ| asmitā-satvapuruṣayoraikyaviṣayo manovṛttiviśeṣo'haṅkāraḥ| rāgaḥ sukhameva sadāstītīcchārūpaḥ| dveṣo duḥkhahetuṣu nindā| abhinivaśo durāgrahaḥ| eteṣāṃ pañcānāṃ duḥkhajanakatvena kleśaśabdavācyānāṃ saṃsārākhyamahānarthānukalānāṃ samādhipratibandhakānāṃ mūlabhūtapāpanirāśena śithilīkaraṇadvāreṇa samādhihetutvaṃ kriyāyogasyeti| kiñca ye sthūlā āravdhakāryayāḥ kleśāste cittaikāgrayarūpeṇa dhyānena nivartante| tathāca sūtradvayam -- "dhyānaheyāstadvṛttaya" | "te pratiprasavaheyāḥ sūkṣmāḥ" iti | ete kleśā avidyāmūlāḥ| eṣāñca phalaṃ śūbhāśubhalakṣaṇam| karmaṇaścoktarūpasya phalaṃ sukhaduḥkhānubhavaḥ| karmānurūpajā āyurviṣayendriyaprāptiśca| tattad dvārā "sati mūle tadvipāko jātyāyurbhogā" ityatra jātyāyuṣorapi karmaphalatvābhi-
--------------------------------------
P.18
dhānaṃ bhoge janayitavye dvāratvena sukhamapi pariṇāmavirasasvādidarśanādvivekinaṃ prati duḥkhameti mantabyam|
ete kleśāḥ śubhāśubhakarmavāsanākhya-karmāśayajātyāyurbhogākhyavipākaphalā avidyāmūlā vivekinā tājyā eva|
tattyāgasya mūlabhūtavighnocchedakasamyagjñānasādhyaḥ| samyagjñānañca śāstrasādhyam| śāstrañcedaṃ heyam........tatkāraṇākhya parvacatuṣṭayatayā caturvyūhastadayathā, "duḥkhabahulaḥ saṃsāro heyaḥ, pradhānapuruṣayoḥ saṃyogo heyahetuḥ, tasyāpi heturavidyā, pradhānapuruṣasaṃyogātyantanivṛttirhānam"  tadeva kaivalyam| tatra heturvivekakhyātiriti| tathāca sūtrāṇi "heyaṃ duḥkhamanāgatam draṣṭṛ dṛśyayoḥ saṃyogo heyahetuḥ, tasya heturavidyā, tadabhāvāt saṃyogābhāvo hānam, tadṛśeḥ kaivalyam, vivekakhyātiraviplavā hānopāyaḥ" ityādīni| draṣṭṛ dṛśya tatsaṃyogaparāṇi ca sūtrāṇi draṣṭā dṛśimātraḥ śuddho'pi pratyayānupaśyaḥ" "prakāśa-kriyā-sthiti śīlaṃ bhūtendriyātmakaṃ bhogāpavargārthaṃ dṛśyam" "svasvāmiśaktyoḥ svarūpopalabdhihetuḥ saṃyoga" iti| etadarthastu citsvarūpo'pariṇāmī sannidhānamātreṇa viṣayo'paravṛttiḥ puruṣo draṣṭā| prakāśapravṛttiniyamarūpasattvarajastamoguṇātmakaṃ grāhyarūpapṛthivyādi tanmātrādilakṣaṇa-sthūlasūkṣmapariṇāmakaṃ buddhīndriya-karmendiyāntaḥ karaṇatrividhendriyapariṇāmabhogāpavargaprayojanakaṃ dṛśyam| svaśakti svāmiśaktiśabditayo dṛśyadraṣṭṛsvarūpayornityayorvyāpakayorasaṃvedyaparvaṇi sthitayo ryatsvarūpopalabdhihetuḥ sa saṃyoga(hetuḥ)|
----------------------------------
P.19
anādi bhoktṛtvasvabhāvaḥ sa eva draṣṭṛdṛśya saṃyoga iti| atredamālocanīyam| draṣṭā dṛpimātretyatra mātrapadaṃ naiyāyikābhimatadharma-dharmi bhāva nirākaraṇaparam| na tu ānanda sūvarūpatva nivārakam| yathā cetanamātra paramārtha nija svabhāva iti sarvajñātmamunibhiruktam| mātrapadaṃ "tadetat preyaḥ prattrāditi"  śruteḥ| nirūpādhipremāspadatvena liṅgena "sukhamahamasvāptam"  ityanubhavena vā| ātmana ānandarūpatvasya siddhatvāt| ataevāśarīraṃ vāvasantaṃ priyāpriye na spṛśata iti śrutiḥ vaiṣayika sukhaduḥkha niṣedhaparā| anyathaitasyaivānandasyānyāni bhūtāni mātrā upajīvanti  iti| yo vai bhūmā tatmukhaṃ nālpesukhamastīti | "ko'nyāt kaḥ prāṇyād yadeṣa ākāśa ānando na syāt"  iti| "eṣa hyevaṃ nidayatīti| "ānandaṃ brahmaṇovidvān"  iti| "vijñānamānandraṃ brahma"  ityādyanantaśrutivirodhaḥ syāt| nacaiṣā brahmaṇa ānandatvabodhanaparā na jīvasyeti vācyam ? "tattvamasi"  "ayamātmā"  "yaḥ sākṣādaparokṣādbrahma" (10) ya ātmā sarvāntaraḥ" (11) śrutibhirjivabrahmaṇo saikyabodhanāt| tasmānmātra padaṃ nānandarūpatāvyavacchedakam|
----------------------------------
P.20
kintu dharma dharmibhāvavyavacchedakamityeva yuktam| "vivekakhyātiraviplavā hānopāyaḥ"  iti sūprasyāyamarthaḥ| anye guṇā anyaḥ puruṣaḥ ityevambidha vivekasya yā khyātiḥ prajñā sā hānasya dṛśya parityāgasyopāyaḥ| āgamānumānābhyāṃ vivekakhyātikasyaṃ puṃso vyutthāna tatsaṃskārānuvṛtternāsyā-upāyatvam,-- ityetaduktam| aviplaveti| mithyājñānarahitasyetyarthaḥ "etaduktaṃ bhavati, -- śrutimayena jñānena vivekaṃ gṛhītvā yuktimayena vyavasthāpya dīrghakālanairattarya" satkāra sevitāyābhāvanāyāḥ| prakarṣaparyantasamadhigatā sākṣātkāravatī vivekakhyātirnivartita savāsana mithyājñānā-nirviplavā hānopāya"  iti vācaspatimiśrāḥ| bhojarājavyākhyānantu, -- "na vidyate viplavo vicchedo vyutthānarūpoyasyā anyeguṇā anyaḥ puruṣa iti pratipakṣabhāvanābalādavidyāpravilaye nivṛttajñātṛtvakartatvābhimānāyā rajastamomalānabhi bhūtāyā buddherantarmukhāyāyā cicchaktisaṃkrāntiḥ sā vivekakhyāti rityucyate|
tasyāñca santatatvena pravṛttāyāṃ satyāṃ dṛśyasyādhikāranivṛtterbhavatyeva kaivalyamiti"| idamatrālocanīyam| prakṛtipuruṣavivekadhīmātraṃ na hānasādhanībhūtavivekakhyātisvarūpam|
kintu vivekakhyātiḥ pratha(sa)ma(va)bhūmiḥ| etadanantarantu rajastamo leśahīne mirmale sattvaprakāśe'ntakaraṇe yāvṛttisvārūpyalakṣaṇā| "tadā draṣṭuḥ svarūpeṇāvasthānam"  iti sūtrāhitā
-----------------------------------
P.21
pūrṇacaitanyaikarasa brahmapratibhāsalakṣaṇā "ahaṃ brahmāsmī"  tyevaṃrūpaprāptilakṣaṇasya kaivalyasya hānaśabdābhidheyasya paramaupāya iti|
anye guṇā anyaḥ puruṣa iti jñānamiti vivekakhyātirvyākhyānantvanādipravṛttasakalānarthamūlabhūta-jaḍa़dehādyabhimānavṛttyasādhāraṇakāraṇatvena jaḍa़ājaḍa़vivekamātropayogitayā ataevāsminnaye mokṣo'pi hānakaivalyādiśabdairevoktaḥ|
yattuḥ – bhokṣodaśāyāṃ prakāśamānaṃ nijātmasvarūpaṃ yacca mokṣasādhanasyāsādhāraṇaviṣayāvacchinnaṃ svarūpaṃ tatsākṣāt śrutyaiva vyaktam| .........tamiti na prādhānyena viśeṣato vyutpāditam bhagavatā patañjalinā, tathāca vṛhadāraṇyake "brahma vā idamagra āsīt"  "tadātmānamevaṃ vedāhaṃ brahmāsmī" "tasmāt tatsarvamabhavati"ti  jñānaṃ sādhanamityupakrāntamupasaṃharati| tadidamapyetattarhi "ya evaṃ vedāhaṃ brahmāsmīti sa idaṃ sarvaṃ bhavati" | tathācānyathājñānamavidyā ityetadapi pradarśitaṃ tatraivāvidyā sūtre "atha yo'hanyāsāvanyo'hamiti na sa veda" ityādinā, ityalamati prasaṅgena| etaduktaṃ bhavatikāmyaniṣiddhavarjiteśvarārthakṛtasarvakarmabhirnitāntanirmala-svāntasya
----------------------------------
P.22 
viṣayadoṣadarśanasañjātaverāgyasya "tarali śokamātmaviditi" śrutivākyāvagatasamūlasarva-duḥkhocchedakātmajñānārthitayā sadagurumatyā śravaṇamananopavṛṃhitanididhyāsanābhyāsena prāptanirbījasamādheḥ puṃso buddhiśuddhidvāraiva samādhirūpakṛtakāryāṇi karmāṇi svat eva nivartante| tathāca naiṣkarmasiddhau "pratyak pravaṇatāṃ buddheḥ samyaksampādya śuddhitaḥ| karmāṇi vinivartante prāvṛḍa़nte ghanā iveti " | rājayoge, --
"na karmāṇi tyajedaḥ yogī karmabhistyajyate tvasau|
karmaṇo mūlabhūtasya saṅkalpasyaiva nāśanāt||
yathā yathā sadabhyāsātsaṅkalpavilayo bhavet|
yogino bhavati śreyān karmatyāgo tathā tathā||
uttuṅgasahajānandaḥ sadābhyāsarataḥ svayam|
sarvasaṅkalpasantyaktaḥ sa vidvān karma santyajeta||
sāṃsārikaṃ kriyāyuktaṃ brahmajñasyeti vādinam|
karma brahmobhayabhraṣṭaṃ tantyajedantyajaṃ yathā||
kālikāpurāṇe,--
"dharmaḥ prayatnataḥ kāryo yoginā tu viśeṣataḥ|
nāsti dharmādṛte yoga iti yogavido viduḥ||
yājñavalkyagītāsu gārgyuvāca, --
"yogayukto naraḥ svāmin sandhyayorvāthavā niśi|
vaidhaṃ karma kathaṃ kuryāntriṣkṛtiḥ kā tvakurvataḥ||"
---------------------------------
P.23
yājñavalkya uvāca, —
"yogayuktamanuṣyasya sandhyayorvāthavā niśi|
yatkartavyaṃ varārohe! kṛtaṃ yogena tat khalu||
svātmāgnihotravahnau ca prāṇāyāmairvivarddhite
viśuddhacittahaviṣā viśuddhaṃ karma kurvataḥ||
niṣkṛtistasya kā bāle! kṛtakṛtyastadā khalu|
viyoge sati samprāpte jīvātmā paramātmanoḥ||
vidhyuktaṃ karma kartavyaṃ brahmavidbhiśca nityaśaḥ|
viyogakāle yogī tu duḥkhamityeva na tyajet||
karmāṇi tasya nilayo nirayaḥ parikīrtitaḥ|
na yogiṇā yataḥ śakyaṃ tyuktaṃ karmāṇyaśeṣataḥ||
tasmādāmaraṇādvaidhaṃ kartavyaṃ yoginā sadā||""
bhagavadagītāyām  –
"ārurukṣormuneryogaṃ karma kāraṇamucyate|
yogārūḍha़sya tasyaiva śamaḥ kāraṇamucyate||"
kūrmapurāṇe –
"tasmātsarvaprayatnena varṇāśramavidhau sthitaḥ|
samāśrito'ntimaṃ bhāvaṃ māyāśaktiṃ bhavedbudhaḥ||
dharmaḥ sa paramo nityamīśvarārpitamānasaḥ|
prāpnoti parame sthānaṃ yaduktaṃ vedavādibhiḥ ||
P.24
brāhmaṇakṣatriyaviśāṃ strīśūdrāṇāñca sattamāḥ|
svajāti vihitaṃ karmarāhādveṣavivarjitam||
jñānakāraṇabhāvaṃ hi tadeva pratipādyate||" iti|
yogavīje,--
"na karmaṇā vinā devi yogasiddhiḥ prajāyate|
jñānaṃ vā svargabhogo vā puṇyahīnairna labhyate||
tasmātkāryantadevāśu yad yasyāsti hi sādhanam|
te naiva prāpyate siddhirnānyathā viśabhāṣitam||"iti|
kiñca īśvarārādhanamameva yogasya mukhyopāyosti| tathāca pātañjale, yogopāyanirūpaṇe -- "īśvara praṇidhānādveti"  vā śabde'tra eva kārārthaḥ| kāyika vācika mānasika bhaktiviśeṣeṇārādhitasya īśvararasya prasādādeva yogo yogaphalañca bhavati iti sūtrārthaḥ| vedānte vyāsasūtraṃ -- "phalamata upapatteriti"  anena bhaktitogasyāpi rājayogāṅgatvena patañjalinoktatvānna mukhyaṃ yogam| tathāhi -- "abhyāsavairāgyābhyāṃ tannirodha" iti  asyārthaḥ – vakṣyamāṇābhyāsa vairāgyābhyāṃ prakāśa pravṛtti niyama rūpāṇāṃ cittavṛttīnāṃ nivṛttavāhyābhiniveśānāṃ antarmukhatayā svakāraṇe citte śaktirūpatayā'vasthānam, atra viṣayadoṣadarśanajena vairāgyeṇa tadvaimukhyamupapādyate| abhyāsena sukhajanakaśānta pravāhapradarśana dvāreṇa dṛḍhaṃ sthairyamutpādyate| atastābhyāṃ bhavati citta-
P.25
vṛttinirodhākhyaḥ samādhiḥ| abhyāsasya sāmānyaviśeṣalakṣaṇamāha sūtrābhyām|
"tatra sthitau yatno'bhyāsaḥ|
"sa tu dīrghakālanairantarya satkārāsevito dṛḍha़bhūmi" riti | "vṛttirahitasya cittasya svarūpaniṣṭhaḥ pariṇāmaḥ sthitistasyāṃ yatna-utsāhaḥ punaḥ punaḥ tathātvena cetasi niveśanamabhyāsa ityucyate"| sa evābhyāso bahukālaṃ nārantaryeṇa yogādatiśayena ca sevyamānodṛḍha़bhūmiḥ sthirobhavatītyarthaḥ"| skandapurāṇe, --
"ātmajñānenaṃ muktiḥ syāttacca yohagādṛte na hi|
na ca yogaściraṃ kālaṃ abhyāsādeva sidhyati||
nāraṇyasaṃśrayād yogo nānāgrantha vicintanāt|
na dānairnavratairvāpi na tapobhirnavāmakhaiḥ||
na ca padmāsanād yogo na ca prāṇāgravīkṣaṇāt|
na śaucena na maunena na mantrārādhanairapi||
abhiyogātsadābhyāsāttatraiva ca viniścayāt|
punaḥ punaranirvedāt sidhyed yogo nacānyathā||
ātmakrīḍa़sya satataṃ tathātmamithunasya  ca |"
ātmanyeva ca tṛptasya yogasiddhirnadūrataḥ||
athātmavyatirekeṇa dvitīyaṃ yo na paśyati|
ātmārāmaḥsa yogondro brahmībhūto bhavediha||
svasaṃvedyaṃ hi tad brahma kumārīstrīmukhaṃ yathā|
ayogī na hi tadvetti jātyandha iva varṇikām ||
P.26
nityābhyasana śīlasya svasaṃvedyaṃ hi tad bhavet|
tatsūkṣmatvādanirddeśyaṃ paraṃbrahmasanātanam|| " iti|
āgame pārameśvaratantre, --
"tasmādabhyasanaṃ nityaṃ kartavyaṃ mokṣakāṅghibhiḥ|
abhyāsājjāyate siddhirabhyāsānmokṣamāpnuyāt||
saṃvittiṃ labhate'bhyāsād yogo'bhyāsāt pravartate|
mātrāṇāṃ siddhi rabhyāsādabhyāsād vāyu sādhanam||
kālavañcanamabhyāsāttathā mṛtyujayobhavet|
vāksiddhiḥ kāmacāratvaṃ bhavedabhyāsato dhruvam||" iti| 
haṭhapradīpikāyām, --
"yuvābālo'tivṛddhovā vyādhitodurbalo'pivā|
abhyāsātsiddhimāpnoti sarvayogeṣvatantritaḥ||"
amṛtasiddhau, -- mudrāprāṇāyāpanirūpanānantaramuktam|
"evaṃ budhaiḥ sadābhyāsaḥ kartavyomokṣakāṅkṣibhiḥ|
abhyāsāj jāyate yogo yogātsarvaṃ prasidhyati||
anenābhyāsa yogena vāyurabhyasito bhavet|
vāyorabhyāsato vahniḥ pratyahaṃ varddhate bhṛśam||
vahnau vivarddhamāne hi mukhamannaṃ hi pacyate|
annasya paripākena rasavṛddhiḥ prajāyate||
rase vṛddhiṃ gate nityaṃ varddhante dhātavastadā|
dhātoḥ saṃvarddhanādeva pradhāno varddhate rasaḥ||
pradhāna rasa saṃpattau dṛḍha़kāyo mahābalaḥ|
mahābandho mahābhyāso balādeva prajāyate||
P.27
mahābandhānmahābhyāsādrasasya jāraṇobhavet|
doṣāḥ sarve'pi śuṣyanti mūlamūtrakaṣāyakāḥ||
dahyante sarva pāpāni janmakoṭikṛtāni ca|
ihaivābhyāsa yogena tṛṇāni vahninā yathā||
tapāṃsi yāni divyāni hyayutānyayutāni ca? |
koṭikoṭiguṇaistāni bhavantyabhyāsayogataḥ||
prakṛtiśca guṇā doṣā vyādhayo vividhāstanau|
naśyantyabhyāsayogena khe meghā vāyunāyathā||" iti|
tathā vairārgyasāmānyaviśevalakṣaṇaparaṃ sūtradvayam –
"dṛṣṭānuśravikaviṣayavitṛṣṇyamiti", -- etadarthastu -- "dṛṣṭo viṣaya ihaivopalabhyamānaḥ śabdādiḥ| ānuśraviko viṣayo devalokādau| anuśrūyate gurumukhādityanuśravo vedastata āgata ānuśravikaḥ | tayordvayorviṣayayorapi pariṇāmavirasatvadarśanena vigatagarddhasya yā vaśīkārasaṃjñā mamaivaite vaśyā nāhanevesāṃ vaśya iti yo'yaṃ vimarśasvadvairāgyamucyate" | prathamaṃvairāgyaṃ viṣaya viṣayam| dvitīyaṃ guṇaviṣayam| tatra guṇaviṣayaṃ vairāgyaṃ paramucyate| utpannaguṇapuruṣavivekakhyāterevaitadvairāgyaṃ bhavati| nirodhasamādheratyantānukūlatvāt"|| anena vivekakhyātivācyasāṃkhyayogasyāpi yogoyogitvamuktam| evañcādvitīye vastuni vedāntavākyatātparyāvadhāraṇātmakasya śravaṇasya  śrutārthasya yuktibhiścintanātmakasya mananasya ca niyame vijātīyapratyayatiraskāreṇa
P.28
sajātīyapratyayapravāhīkaraṇātmakanididhyāsanasya dhyānarūpasamprajñāta samādhau vā (cā)ntarbhāvo draṣṭavyaḥ|
tathāca niyamavivaraṇe yājñavalkyaḥ, --
"siddhārntaṃśravaṇaṃ proktaṃ vedāntaśravaṇaṃ vudhaiḥ|
vedāntaśravaṇaṃ kurvan yogaṃ tasmin samabhyaset||" iti|
tasmānnāṣṭāṅgayogādvihirmūtaṃ  kimapi yogaśabdavācyamastītyavadheyam| nanu vāsiṣṭhe, --
"saṃsārottaraṇe yuktiryogaśabdena kathyate|
ātmajñānaprakāraśca prāṇānāṃ saṃyamastathā||
prakārau dvāvapi proktau yogaśabdena yadyapi|
tathāpi rūḍha़imāyātaḥ prāṇayuktā tvasau bhṛśam|"
ityuktatvātkthaṃ mano nirodha eva yoga iti cet satyam, manonirodhasya prāṇanirodhātmakatvāśayenoktatvāt| ata eva prāṇāyāma evābhyāskrameṇa pratyāhāradhāraṇā-dhyāna-samādhiśabdenocyate|
tathāca dattātreyaḥ,--
"prāṇānāmupasaṃrodhātprāṇāyāma iti smṛtaḥ|
śabdādibhyaḥ pravṛttāni yadakṣāṇi yatātmabhiḥ||
pratyāhriyante yogena pratyāhārastataḥ smṛtaḥ|
dhāraṇetyucyate yeyaṃ dhāryate yatmanīṣiṇā||
prāṇāyāmādiśabdaśca dhāraṇā parikīrtitā|
he dhāraṇe smṛto yogo yogibhistattvadarśibhiḥ"||
skandapurāṇe, --
"prāṇāyāmadviṣaṭkena pratyāhāra udāhṛtaḥ|
pratyāhārairdvādaśabhirdhāraṇā parikīrtitā||
P.29
bhavedīśvarasaṅgatyai dhyānaṃ dvādaśadhāraṇam|
dhyāna-dvādaśakenaiva samādhiramidhīyate||
samāgheḥ parato jyotiranantaṃ svaprakāśakam|
tasmin dṛṣṭe kriyākāṇḍaṃ yātāyātaṃ nivartate" || iti|
tathā tatraiva dhāraṇādi-parimāṇañcoktam, --
"dhāraṇā pañcanāḍa़ikā dhyānaṃ sāt ṣaṣṭhī nāḍa़ikam|
dinadvādaśakena syātsamādhiḥ prāṇasaṃyamāt" || iti|
ghaṭikāmānamāha vaśiṣṭhaḥ, --
"ṣaṣṭhiśvāso bhavetprāṇāḥ ṣaṭprāṇā ghaṭikā bhavet|
ghaṭikā pañcarāśiḥ syādrāśi-dvādaśakaṃ dinam"|| iti| evañca rāśidvādaśāṃśaḥ sārddhaprāṇadvayasya  dvādaśāṃśaḥ sārddhadvādaśaśvāsāstāvatkālaṃ dhṛto vāyuḥ prathamaḥ prāṇāyāmo bhavati| caturviṃśatiśvāsānto madhyamaḥ| ṣaṭtriṃśacchāsāntauttama iti rāddhāntaḥ|
skandapurāṇe—
"ekaśvāsamayī mātrā prāṇāyāme nirūcyate|" iti|
nidrāvaśaṅgatasya puṃso yāvatkālenaikaśvāso gacchatyāgacchatica tāvatkālaṃ prāṇāyāmasya mātretyucyate|
visvareṇa mātrālakṣaṇamupariṣṭhādvakṣyate | kiñca-manaḥprāṇayoḥ spando'pyeka evāsti|
vāśiṣṭhe, --
"yaḥ prāṇapavanaspandaḥ cittaspandaḥ  sa eva hi|
prāṇāspandajaye yatnaḥ kartavyo dhīmatoccakaiḥ" || iti |
ataevobhayapralayātmaka eva samādhiḥ sarvayogaśāstreṣu prasiddhaḥ|
P.30
skandapurāṇe, --
"yadā saṃkṣīyate prāṇo mānasañca pralīyate|
tadā samarasatvaṃ yatma samādhirihocyate"  iti|
ataevobhayanāśe satyeva jñānaṃ mokṣaśca bhavatīti śivavākyam|
"jñānaṃ kuto manasi jīvati devi! tāvat,
prāṇo'pi jīvati mano mriyate na yāvat|
prāṇe manodvayamidaṃ vilayannayed yo-
mokṣaṃ sa gacchati naro na kathañcidanyaḥ||"
vāśiṣṭhe, --
"ekatattvaghanābhyāsaprāṇānāṃ vilayastathā|
mano vinigrahaśceti mokṣaśabdārthasaṃgrahaḥ"|| iti|
nanu, asya saṃsārarogasya sarvopadravakāriṇaḥ|
upāya eka evāsti manasaḥ svasya nigrahaḥ||
cittameva hi saṃsāraḥ kāmakopādibhiryutam||"
ityādisampūrṇam| vāsiṣṭhe,--
"mana eva manuṣyāṇāṃ kāraṇaṃ bandhamokṣayoḥ|
bandhāya viṣayāsaktaṃ muktyai nirviṣayanmatam|"
ityādi, śāstrāntarepvapi saṃsārasya manomūlakatvaśravaṇāttattvajñānamokṣayorapi manojayādhīnatvaśravaṇācca sarvānarthahetusavilāsaviśvonmūlanapūrvakamokṣarūpapumarthasya mano nigraheṇaiva sambhavāt kiṃ duḥsādhyenānekarogavyādhiduḥkhadeva prāṇa-nigraheṇeti cet naḥ prāṇajayaṃ vinā cittajayasyāsādhyatvāt|
tathāca skandapurāṇe, --
"cale vāte calaṃ cittaṃ niścalanniścale tathā|
yogī sthāṇutvamāpnoti tato vāyunnirodhayet||
kṣaṇabhapyekamudakaṃ yathā na sthiratāmiyāt|
vātāhatantathā cittaṃ tasmāttasminna viśvaset||
ato'nilannirundhīta cittasya sthairyahetave|
marunnirodhanārthāya ṣaḍa़ṅgaṃ yogamabhyaset||
yāvaddehe marudvaddhastāvaccittaṃ nirāmayam|
yāvadṛṣṭibhruvormadhye tāvatkālabhayaṃ kutaḥ?||
mārkaṇḍeya purāṇe, --
"iti te kathitaṃ bhūpa ! yogañcemannibodha me|
samprāpya brāhmaṇo yogī śāśvatānnānyatāmbrajet||
prāgevātmā''tmanājeyo yoginā sati durjaye|
kurvīta tajjaye yatnaṃ tasyopāyaṃ śrṛṇuṣva me||
yathā parvatadhātūnāṃ mūlā dahyanti dhamyatām|
tathendriya-kṛtā doṣā dahyante prāṇanigrahāt||
grāṇāyāmairdaheddoṣān dhāraṇābhiśca kilviṣam|
pratyāhāreṇa saṃsargān dhyānenānīśvarān guṇān" || iti|
haṭhapradīpikāyām, --
"vividhaiḥ prāṇasaṃyāmairnāḍa़īcakre viśodhite|
suṣumnāvadanaṃ bhitvā sukhādviśatu mārutaḥ||
mārute madhyasañcāre manaḥ sthairyaṃ prajāyate|
yo manaḥ susthiro bhāvaḥ saivo'vasthā manonmanī" || iti|
P.32
vāsiṣṭhe, --
"yoginaścittaśārtyarthaṃ kurvanti prāṇanigraham|
prāṇāyāmaistathā dhyānaiḥ prayogairyuktikalpitaiḥ||
cinto(tto) paśāntiphaladaṃ paramaṃ śāntikāraṇam|
śubhagaṃ sambidaḥ svāsthyaṃ prāṇasaṃrodhanaṃ viduḥ||
dve vīje cittavṛtta(kṣa)sya pravṛttivratadhāriṇaḥ|
ekaṃ prāṇaparispando dvitīyaṃ dṛḍha़vāsanā|
cittaṃ prāṇaparispandanāṅgarāgavibhūṣaṇāḥ||
tasmin saṃrodhite nūnamupaśāntaṃ bhavenmanaḥ|
manaḥspandopaśāntyā'yaṃ saṃsāraḥ pravilīyate||
sūryālokaparispandaśānto vyavahṛtiryathā|
abhyāsena parimpandaṃ prāṇānāṃ kṣayamāgate||
manaḥ praśamamāyāti nirvāṇamavaśiṣyata" iti|
śāstrāntare—
"prāṇāyāmair vinā yogaṃ sādhayed yas tu mandadhīḥ|
na sa siddhagatiṃ yāti paṅgarvāji-gatiṃ yatheti"||
yogavīje, --
"cittaṃ prāṇena sambaddhaṃ sa ca jīveṣu saṃsthitaḥ|
rajjavā yadvatsamābaddho vṛṣastadvadidaṃ manaḥ||
nānāvidhairvicāraistu na sādhyaṃ jāyate manaḥ|
tasmāttasya jayopāyaḥ prāṇasya jaya eva hi||
tarkairjalpaiḥ śāstrajālairyuktibhirmantrabheṣajaiḥ|
na vaśo jāyate prāṇaḥ siddhopāyaṃ vinā priye! ||
-------------------------------------
P.33
upāyantamavajñāya yāgamārge pravartate|
khaṇḍajñānena tenaiva jāyate kleśabhāṅ naraḥ||
yojitvā pavanaṃ mohāda yogamicchati yogikaḥ|
so'pakkaṃ kumbhamāruhya sāgaraṃ tartumicchati||
yasya prāṇā pravilīnāḥ sādhake jīvati sati|
piṇḍo na patitastasya cittaṃ doṣaiḥ pramucyate||
śuddhe cetasi tasyaiva svātmajñānaṃ prakāśate|
tasmāj jñānaṃ bhaveda yogājjanmanaikena pārvati! |
tasmādayogantamevāhaḥ sādhako nityamabhya set|
mumukṣubhiḥ prāṇajayaḥ kartavyo mokṣahetavaḥ" iti||
patañjalisūtrañca,--
"praccharddanavidhāraṇābhyāṃ vā prāṇasye"ti  cittanirodhaḥ syādityarthaḥ| evaṃ manojaye satyeva prāṇajayo bhavati| tayoḥ svavyāpāre parasparasyāpekṣatvāt|
tathā viśiṣṭhe, --
"avinābhāvini nityaṃ jantūnāṃ prāṇacetasi|
kusumāmodavanmiśre tile tailamiva sthiteḥ||
"ādhārādheyavaccaite ekābhāve vinaśyataḥ|
kurutaścavināśena kāryaṃ mokṣākhyamuttamam|| "
rājayoge, --
"dugdhāmbuvatsammilitau sadaiva, tulyakriyau mānasamārutau ca|
yāvanmanastatramarut pravṛttiryāvanmaruttatra manaḥ pravṛttiḥ||
----------------------------------
P.34
tatraikanāśādaparasyanāśaḥ, eka pravṛtterapara pravṛttiḥ|
adhvastayoḥ khendriyavargavṛddhi, rvidhvastayormīkṣapadasya siddhiḥ"||
"tatrāpisādhyaḥ pavanasyanāśaḥ,
ṣaḍa़ṅgayogādi niṣevanena|
mano vināśastu guruprasādāt,
nimeṣa mātreṇa susādhya eva||
yathā yathā sadābhyāsānmanasaḥ sthiratā bhaveta|
vāyu vākkāya dṛṣṭīnāṃ sthiratā hi tathā tathā||
tasmānmanonāśa vidhau yateta,
yanmāsato naśyati vāyurugraḥ|
tasmāt svadehendriya buddhināśāda-
advaitabuddhiḥ sahaja sthitaiva iti||"
sureśvarācāryāḥ, --
"citte niścalatāṃ yāte prāṇo bhavati niścalaḥ|
cittasya niścalatvāya yogamaṣṭāgamabhyasediti||"
haṭhapradīpikāyām, --
manaḥ sthairyāt sitharovāyu statobinduḥ sthirobhavet|
bindusthairyāda yathā yatnaṃ piṇjasthairyaṃ prajāyata" iti||
amṛtasiddhau, --
"maraṇaṃ bindupātena jīvanaṃ bindadhāraṇāt|
bindau mahārasesiddhe sarvasiddhiḥ prajāyate|| "
"bindurvedhā vindurviṣṇu rvindurdeva sadāśivaḥ|
binduḥ sarvagatodevo bindustrailokyadarpaṇaḥ||
-----------------------------
P.35
binduḥ sarvagato devo bhavarūpo nirañjanaḥ|
ayañca bindurūpeṇa vasate sarvajantuṣu||
sabindu rdvividhojñeyaḥ pīruṣo'vajisambhavaḥ|
bījaṃ ca pīrūṣaṃ proktaṃ rajaśca strīsamudbhavam||
anayorvāhyayogena sṛṣṭiḥ saṃjāyate nṛṇāṃ|
yadābhyantarato yogastadā yogītigīyate||
bindu sūryamayaḥ prokto rajaścandramayantathā|
anayoḥ saṅgamādeva jāyate paramaṃ padam||
svargado mokṣadobindurdharmado'dharmada stathā|
tanmadhye devatāḥ sarvāstiṣṭanti sūkṣmarūpataḥ||
idaṃ tattvamidaṃ bījaṃ sarbvasāro'yamucyate|
vāyunā patate bindurna cānyadvindusādhanam||
yāmavasthāṃ vrajedvāyurvindastāmavagacchati|
yathāhi sādhyate vāyu stathābindu prasādanam||
mūrchito harate vyādhyiṃ baddhaḥ khecaratāṃ nayet|
sarvasiddhikarolīno niścalo muktidāyakaḥ||
yathāvasthābhavedvindo ścittāvasthā tathā tathā|
calatyeṣa yadā vāyu stadābinduścalaḥ smṛtaḥ||
binduścalati yasyāṅge cittaṃ tasyaiva cañcalam|
cale bindī cale citte vāyauca sarvadā||
jāyate mtriyate lokaḥ satyaṃ satya midaṃ vacaḥ|
yo vai nādaḥ savaibindu taddhi cittaṃ prakīttitam||
nādo binduśca cittañca trayāṇāmaikya sādhanam|
yadyapyekatayā vāhye śarīre saṃvyavasthitāḥ||
------------------------------------
P.36
tathāpi sādhite vāyau sarvai sidhyanti niścitam|
yadā'saumtriyate vāyuḥ madhyamā madhyayogataḥ||
tadā binduśca cittañca mtriyate vāyunāsaha|
sarvāṇi bindu tattvāni śarīre nivasanti ca||
caranti vāyusañcāre mtriyante vāyu māraṇāditi|"
vāśiṣṭhe śrīrāmauvāca, --
"aniśaṃ caratāṃ dehe gehe gahaṇagāminām|
prāṇādīnāṃ parispando vāyūnāṃ rodhyate katham||"
śrīvasiṣṭha uvāca, --
śāstrasajjanasamparka vairāgyābhyāsa yogataḥ|
anāsthāyāṃ kṛtāsthāyāṃ pūrvasaṃsāra vṛttiṣu||
yathābhivāñchitadhyānāccira mekatayoditān|
ekattattvaghanābhyāsāt prāṇaspando nirudhyate||
pūrakādyanilāyāmādṛḍha़ābhyāsādakhedajāt|
ekāntadhyānasaṃyogāt prāṇaspando nirudhyate||
oṅkāroccāraṇa protaśabdatattvānubhāvanāt|
avāsanamanodhyānāt prāṇaspando nirudhyate||
dvādaśāṅgula paryante nāsāgre vimale'mbare|
saṃvidṛśoḥ praśāmyanto prāṇaspandonirudhyate||
prāṇāyāmai[r] dṛḍh़ābhyāsair yuktyā ca gurudattayā|
āsanāśanayogena prāṇaspando nirudhyate||
abhyāsena nirāvādha metāstā yogayuktayaḥ|
upāyatā mupayanti bhavyasya bhavamedane||
--------------------------------
P.37
abhyāsāddaḍha़tāṃ yāto vairāgya parilācchitaḥ|
yathāvāsana māyāmaḥ prāṇānāṃ saphalaḥ smṛtaḥ||
yato vāco nivartante vikalpa kalanānvitāḥ|
vikalpaṃ saṃkṣayājjantoḥ padaṃ tadavaśiṣyate"|| iti|
satyanye'pi prāṇajayopāyā vistarabhayānna likhyate|
tasmāddhaṭarājayogau sabhucchitāvevābhyasanīyau||
haṭhapradīpikāyām, --
"haṭhaṃ vinā rājayogo rājayogaṃ vinā haṭhaḥ|
na siddhyati tato yugmaṃ manīṣṭe tausamabhyaset||
haṭhaṃ vinā rājayogo rājayogaṃ vināhaṭham|
ye vicaranti tānmanye prayāsa phalavarjitā niti||"
visiṣṭhe'pi—
"yāvadvilīnaṃ namanonatāvadvāsanākṣayaḥ|
nakṣīṇā vāsanā yāvattāvacittaṃ naśāmyati||
nayāvatatvavijñānaṃ na tāvaccittasaṃkṣayaḥ|
yāvannacittopaśamo na tāvattatvavedanam||
yāvannatattvasamprāptirnatāvadvāsanākṣyaḥ||
tattvajñānaṃ manonāśo vāsanākṣaya eva ca|
mithaḥ kāraṇatāṃ gatvā duḥsādhyāni sthitānyataḥ||
tasmādrāghava! yatnena pauruṣeṇa vivekataḥ|
bhogāsthāṃ dūrataḥ kṛtvā trayametatsamāśrayet||
traya ete samaṃyāvannasvabhyastā () muhurmuhuḥ|
tāvannatattvasaṃprāpti rbhavatyapi samāśataiḥ||
-------------------------
P.38
vānākṣayavijñāna manonāśā mahāmate!|
samakālaṃ samābhyastā bhavanti phalanāmune |
ekaikaśo niṣevyante yadayate !  ciramapyalam|
tannasiddhiṃ payacchanti mantrāḥ saṃkīrtitādava||
tribhiretaiścirānyastai hṛdayagranathayodṛḍha़āḥ|
niḥśaṅkamevatrudyanti viśacchadā guṇā iva||
janmāntaraśatābhyastā sāmasaṃsāra saṃsthitiḥ|
sā cirābhyastayogena vinā na kṣīyate kṛcit||
vāsanāsamprarityāga samaṃ prāṇanirodhanam|
vindustatvidastasmāttadapetaṃ samāharet||
vāsanā samparityāgāccittaṃgacchatyacittatām|
prāṇaspandanirodhāccha yathecchasi tathākuru||
asaṅga vyavahāritvād bhavabhāvanavarjanāt|
śarīranāśadarśitvādvāsanā na pravartate||
vāsanā vibhave naṣṭe na cittaṃ sampravartate||
dve bīje rāma! cittasya prāṇaspandanavāsane|
ekasmiṃ ścittayornaṣṭe kṣipraṃ dve opinaśyataḥ|| " iti||
atha yogopāyanirūpaṇam|
tatra patañcali proktāḥ kecit prasaṅgāt pūrvamevoktāḥ| ayamihopāyakramaḥ| prathamamāgamānumānācāryopadeśasamadhigatatattva viṣayacetoviratistatsādhanabhūte yoge śraddhāṃ prasūte tadanantara-
---------------------------------
P.39
mutsāhaḥ, sotsāhasya pāścātyāsu bhūmikāsu smṛtiḥ| tat smaraṇādanukūlaṃ cittaṃ yamaniyamādi niviṣṭaṃ sadayogāṅga samādhimadbhavati| tataścākhila-yogāṅga sampannasya prajñā prakarṣādbhavati samprajñātaḥ samādhiḥ| tadabhyāsāttadviṣayācca paravairāgyaṃ bhavatyasamprajñātaḥ samādhiriti| sa ca kaivalyahetuḥ sattvapuruṣānyathā khyātipūrvohi nirodhaḥ, cittamakhilakāryakaraṇena caritārthamadhikārādavasāda panitam| tatra prathamaṃ maitrayādibhiryoginā citta prasādhanaṃ kāryam| irṣyādiyuktasya manaso jetumaśakyatvāt| tathāca gītāyām, --
asaṃyatātmanāyogo duṣprāpa iti me matiḥ|
vaśyātmanā tu yatatāśakyo'vāptumupāyataḥ ||" iti|
skandapurāṇe, --
"yāvat prayatna-leśosti yāvat rukalpa kalpanā|
yāvacca manasānāpti stāvattattvasya kākathā ?" || iti |
haṭhapradīpikāyāṃ, --
"yāvannaiva praviśati gatirmāruto madhyamārge,
yāvadvindurna bhavati dṛḍha़uprāṇavāta prabaddhaḥ|
yāvadvayomnaḥ sahaja sadṛśaṃ jñāyate naiva tattvam;
tāvatsarvaṃ vadati yadidaṃ dambhamithyāpralāpaḥ" || iti |
amṛtasiddhau,--
"tāvadvaddho'pyasiddho'sau naraḥ sāṃsārikomataḥ|
yāvadbhavatirasendro'sau dehago brahma rūpakaḥ||
asiddhaṃ tadvijānīyāt kāyamabrahmacāriṇām|
jarāmaraṇasaṅkīrṇaṃ sarvakleśa samāśrayaḥ"||
--------------------------------
P.40
tathaiva, -- "yāvadvimārgagovayu rniścalo naiva madhyagaḥ|
asiddhaṃ tadvijānīyā dvāyuṃ karmaguṇānvitam||
yāvat praspandate cittaṃ vāhvābhyāntara vastuṣu|
asiddhantadvijānīyā ccittaṃ karma vaśānugam"||
tathāca patañjali sūtram, --
"maitrīkaruṇāmuditīpekṣāṇāṃ sukhaduḥkha puṇyāpuṇyaviṣayāṇāṃ bhāvanātaścitta prasādana miti"| 
atra sukhādi śabdāstadṛtparāḥ rāgadveṣakaluṣīkṛtasya puṃsaḥ| sukhini dṛṣṭe īrṣyā, puṇyavatidveṣaḥ, duḥkhinicopekṣā, pāpini cānumodanaṃ jāyate| yogītu sukhini maitrīm, duḥkhini kariṇām, puṇyavati muditām, pāpinyupekṣām, bhāvayan sakalakhedamūlarāgadveṣau samunmūlayati|
tataścittaṃ prasīdati prasannacittasya sulabho yoga iti| abhimata dhyānādapi cittaṃ sthiraṃ bhavatītyetaduktam| "yathābhimatadhyānādveti" sūtreṇa| 
vāsiddhe, --
"cittaṃ kāraṇamarthānāṃ tasmin sati jagattrayam|
tasmin kṣīṇe jagatkṣīṇaṃ taccikitsyaṃ prayatnataḥ||
hastaṃ hastena niṣpiṣya dantāndantaivicūrṇya ca|
aṅgānyaṅgaiḥ samākramya jayedādī svakammanaḥ|
viṣayān pratibhoḥ putra! sarvānevahi sarvadā (thā)||
anāsthā paramā hayeṣā sā yukti rmanasojaye|
eṣaiva paramāyukti ranayaiva mahāmadaḥ|
svamanomattamātaṅgo drāgatyeva vimadyate||
-------------------------------------
P.41
citasyabhogairdvaubhāgau śastreṇakamprapūrayet|
guruśuśrūṣayābhāga mavyutpannasya satkṛtaḥ||
kiñcihyutpattiyuktasya bhāgaṃ bhogaiḥ prapūrayet|
guruśuśrūṣayābhāga mavyutpannasya satkṛtaḥ||
kiñcihyutpattiyuktasya bhāgaṃ bhogaiḥ prapūrayet|
guruśuśrūṣayā bhāgau bhāgaṃ śāstrārthacintayā||
vyutpattimanujātasya pūrayeccetaso'nvaham|
dvau bhāgau śāstravairāgyairdvauradhyānagurupūjayā||
prajñāvicāravaśataḥ saṃyateva sadā suta.
na śakyate manojetuṃ vinā yuktimaninditām||
aṅkuśena vinā matto yathā duṣṭamataṅgajaḥ|
adhyātmavidyādhigamaḥ sādhusaṅgama eva ca||
vāsanā samaparityāgaḥ samaṃ prāṇaniroghanam|
etāstā yuktayaḥ śreṣṭhāḥ santi prāṇajaye kila||
satīṣu yuktiṣvetāsu haṭhānniyamayanti ye|
cetaste dīpamutsṛjya nāśayanti tamojanaiḥ|
vimūḍha़āḥ kartumadyuktā ye haṭhāccetaso jayam|
tena nivadhnanti nāgendramummukta viṣatantubhiriti"||
atha sugaṃmānāmanusandhānādayogopāyaḥ nirūpyate|
haṭhapradīpikāyām, --
"indriyāṇāṃ mano nātho mano nāthaśca mārutaḥ|
mārutasya layo nātha sa layo nādamāśritaḥ||
ayameva tu mokṣākhyohyastu vāpi matāmbare! 
manaḥ prāṇo layo nādo na ca kaścidvibhidyate||
mano mattagajendrasya viṣayodyānacāriṇaḥ|
niyāmanasamartho'yaṃ ninādo niśitāṅkuśaḥ||
-------------------------------------------
P.42
makarandaṃ pivadabhṛṅgī gandhānnāpekṣate yathā|
nādasaktaṃ tathā cittaṃ viṣayānna hi kāṅkṣati||
kāṣṭhe pravartito vahniḥ koṣṭhena saha śāmyati|
nāde pravartitaṃ cittaṃ nādena saha līyate||
vismūtya sakalaṃ vāhyaṃ nāde dugdhāmbuvannaraḥ|
ekībhūyātha sahasā cidākāśe vilīyate||
"śrīādināthena  sapādakoṭilayaprakārāḥ kathitā jayanati|
nādānusandhānakameva kāryaṃ manyāmahe nānyatamaṃ layānām|
audāsīnya paro bhuktvā sadābhyāsena saṃyamī|
unmanīkaraṇaṃ sadyo nādavevāvadhārayet||"
kīdṛśañcīdāsīnyam,--
"śīte kāle kāpaṭī vā paṭī vā, 
pathyāhāre gopayo vā payo vā|
bhakṣye bhikṣā vṛkṣamāraṇyakaṃ vā;
pā(yā)ne droṇikā parāmbhojapātrī||
unmanyavāptaye śīghraṃ hī bhāgau mama sammatau|
tattvaṃ paramasaukhyaṃ vā nādopāsanameva vā||
saukṣmayaṃ praviṣṭacittānāṃ mūḍha़ānāmapi sammatam|
śīghramānandasandhāyī jāyate nādajo jayaḥ||
muktāsanaṃ sthito yogī mudrāṃ sandhāya śāmbhavīm|
śrṛṇuyāddakṣiṇe karṇe nādamantargataṃ sadā||
sarvacintāṃ parityajya sāvadhānena cetasā|
nādamevānusandhatte yogasāmrājyamicchatā||
karṇaupidhāya hastena yaḥ śrṛṇoti dhvaniṃ muniḥ|
P.43
tāvaccittaṃ sthiraṃ kuryāda yāvat sthirapadaṃ vrajet||
abhyasyamāno nādo'yaṃ vāhyamāvartayed dhvanim|
paścādvikṣepamakhilaṃ jitvā yogī sukhī bhavet||
śrūyate prathamābhyāse nādo nānāvidho mahān|
vartamāne tato'bhyāse śrūyate sūkṣma-sūkṣmataḥ||
ādau jaladajīmutabherīrūrurasammavaḥ|
madhye mārddalaśaṅkho'tha ghaṇṭākāhalakastathā||
anye tu kiṅkiṇīvṛndavīṇābhramaranisvanāḥ|
iti nānāvidho nādaḥ śrūyate dehamadhyagaḥ|
mahati śrūyamāṇe('pi) tu meghabheryādike dhvanau|
tataḥ sūkṣmātsūkṣmataraṃ nādameva parāmṛśet||
ghanamutsṛjya vā sūkṣmaṃ sūkṣmamutmṛjya vā ghanam|
paraṃ tatraiva niḥkṣipya mano nānyatra cālayet||
yatra kutrāpi vā nāde prathamaṃ viśate manaḥ|
tatraiva susthiraṃ kuryāttena sārddhaṃ vilīyate||
nādo bījaṃ haṭhaḥ kṣetramaudāsīnyaṃ jalaṃ smṛtam|
unmanī kalpalatikā sadya eva bhaviṣyati"||
rājayoge, --
"yatra dṛṣṭirlayastatra bhūtendriyasanātanī|
syācchaktiḥ sarvabhūtānāṃ dṛṣṭirlakṣeṇa saṅgatā||
ūrddhadṛṣṭiradhovedha ūrddhaṃ vedhastvadhomukhaḥ|
ādhāyātra vidhānena jīvanmukto bhaviṣyati||
sarvato vitatādṛṣṭiḥ saṃkṣiptā ca śanaiḥ śanaiḥ|
paratattvamanādarśaṃ paśyatyātmānamātmanā||
P.44
prathamaṃ niḥsṛtā dṛṣṭiḥ saṅgato yatra kutracit|
sthirobhūtā ca tatraiva vinaśyati śanaiḥ śanaiḥ"||
prasahya saṅkalpaparaṃ parāṇaṃ, niṣū(sū)dane santatasāvadhānāḥ|
ālambanāśādapacīyamānā, śanaiḥ śanaiḥ śāntimupaiti dṛṣṭiḥ||
vedaśāstrapurāṇāni sāmānyagaṇikā iva|
ekaiva śāmbhavīmudrā guptā kulabadhūriva|
antarlakṣyaṃ vahirdṛṣṭirnimeṣonmeṣavarjitā|
eṣā tu śāmbhavī mudrā sarvaśāstreṣu gopitā"||
haṭhapradīpikāyām, --
antarlakṣya vilīnā cet tapovano yogī yadā vartate,
dṛṣṭyā niścalatārayā vahirasau paśyanna paśyatyapi|
mudreyaṃ khalu śāmbhavī bhavati sā yuṣmat prasādādguro!, 
śūnyāśūnyavivarjitā sphurati yat tattvaṃ padaṃ śāmbhavam|
nādānusandhānasamādhibhājāṃ, yogīśarāṇāṃ hṛdaye prarūḍha़m|
ānandamekaṃ vacasāmavācyaṃ, jānāti tattvaṃ gurujāma eva|
arddhodghāṭitalocanaḥ sthiramanā nāsāgradattekṣaṇam,
candrārkāvapi līnatāmupanayennispandabhāvottare|
jyotīrūpamaśeṣavāhyarahitaṃ dedīpyamānaṃ paraṃ,
tattvaṃ tatpadameti vastuparamaṃ vācyaṃ kimevādhikam? ||
śrīśaāmbhavyāśca khecaryā śravasthāñcale bhedataḥ|
tāre jyotiṣi saṃyojya kiñciduccālayet bhruvau||
pūrvayogasya mārgo'yamunmanīkaraṇaḥ kṣaṇāt|
kecidāgamajālena kecinniyamasaṅkulāḥ||
kecit tarkeṇa muhyanti naiva jānanti tārakam"|
P.45
caturvidhayogāvasthāsu nānābhedo śrūyate śravasthāśca vakṣyante—
"brahmagranthirbhavedbhinna ānandaśūnyasambhavaḥ|
vicitrakṣaṇiko deho'nāhataḥ śrūyate dhvaniḥ||
viṣṇugranthiryadā bhinnaḥ paramānandasūcakaḥ|
atiśūnyavibhedaśca bhrerīśabdastadā bhavet||
tṛtīyāyāṃ tato bhitvā vimāyo maṅgalaḥ dhvaniḥ|
mahāśūnyaṃ tathā yāti sarvasiddhisamāśrayam||
cittānandaṃ tato bhitvā sahajānandasambhavaḥ|
rudragranthiṃ tato bhitvā sarvapīṭhagato'nilaḥ||
niṣṭhūyato vai navaḥ śabda kkaṇadvīṇā kvaṇo bhavet|
ekībhūtaṃ tathā cittaṃ rājayogabhidhāyakam"||
sureśvarācāryāḥ,--
"prāṇe suṣumnāṃ saptāgre nodāntaḥ śrūyate'ṣṭadhā|
ghaṇṭādundubhiśaṅkhā hi vīṇāveṇvādinādavat||
tanūnapāt tarittārā tāreśa tapanopamaḥ|
vrahmanāḍa़īṃ gate prāṇe kiñca rūpaṃ prakāśayet"||
tripurāsārasamuccaye nādotpattividhiḥ, --
atha pārṣṇiniruddhakambuni...nādadhvajamūlāpitasavyapādapārṣṇiḥ|
ṛjukāyaśirodharo yatātmā viṣayebhyo vinivartatendriyaśca||
kākīcañcvā kṛṣṭemuhurudadhiṃ purayitvā samīraiḥ,
aṅgaṣṭhābhyāmubhābhyāṃ nararipuvivare tarjanībhyāñca netre|
nāsārandhre nirudhya sthiravimalamatirmadhyamābhyāṃ yathāsyam,
tvanyābhirnātigāḍha़ kamalajanilaye sthāpayenmānasaṃ svam||
P.46
yaghāśaktyā bhagnaṃ pratidivasamevaṃ vidadhataḥ,
suṣumnāntaścāyaṃ praviśati śanairddehapavanaḥ|
tadā nādonaijo bhavati sahajānanādajanakaḥ,
kramādvīṇāvāde prahatakalavīṇāravasamaḥ||
ādau mattālisālā janitaravasamastārahūṅkārahālī,
nādo'sau vāṃśikasyānilabharitalasadvaṃśaniḥsvānatulyaḥ|
ghaṇṭhānādānukārī tadanu ca jalaghirdhvānadhīro gabhīraḥ,
garjatparjanyaghoṣapura iha kuhare vartate brahmanāḍyāḥ||
vijīto bhavatīha tena vāyuḥ sahajo yasya samutthitaḥ prāṇādaḥ|
animādiguṇā bhavanti tasyāmitapuṇyasya mahāguṇāspadasya||
surarājatanujavairirandhre vinirudhya svakāraṅgulidvayena|
jaladheriva dhīranādamataḥ prasarantaṃ sahasā śrṛṇoti martyaḥ||
paribhāvayato'pi nādamenaṃ tridaśādhīśatanūvairijātam|
satataṃ bhavatīha tasya jantorguṇasamprāptirapetakalmapasya||
śrṛṅgānyaṅgulibhirnirudhyavidhivat kodaṇḍayoḥ paṇḍitaḥ,
madhye rudramarunirudhyavṛṣabhaśreṣṭhañca ṣaṣṭhendriyam|
sākṣāttatkṣaṇameva paśyati śanaiḥ srāvyantargītaṃ param,
jyotistraipuramaṅkamuktaḥśaśvallakṣāvalakṣaprabham|| iti|
yājñavalkyaḥ –
evaṃ vāyujayopāyaḥ prāṇasya tu varānane! |
śakyamāsanamāsthāya samāhitamanāstathā ||
karaṇāni vaśīkṛtya viṣayebhyo balātsudhīḥ|
apānamūrddhamākṛṣya praṇavena samāhitaḥ|
P.47
hastābhyāṃ bandhayet samyak karṇādikaraṇāni ca|
aṅguṣṭhābhyāmubhe śrotre tarjanībhyāñca cakṣuṣī||
nāsāpuṭau tathā nābhyāṃ pracchādya karaṇāni vai|
ānandānubhavo yāvattāvanmūrddhi nidhārayet ||
prāṇaḥ prayātyanenaiva tatastvāyurvighātakṛta|
brahmarandhre suṣumnāyāṃ mṛṇālāntarasūtravat||
nādotpattistvananaiva śuddhā sphaṭikasannibhā|
āmūrdhno vartate nādo vīṇānādavadatthitaḥ||
śaṅkhaghvaninibhastvādau madhye meghadhvaniryathā|
vyomavartmagate nāde giriprasravaṇaṃ yathā||
vyomarandhraṃ gate vāyau yaścitte cātmani sthite|
sadānandī bhavedayogī vāyustena jito bhavet"|| iti|
iṭhapradīpikāyām, --
"śravaṇayuganayananāsāpuṭanirodhanaṃ kāryam|
śrīśuddhasuṣumnā saraṇau suphuṭamamalaḥ śrūyate nādaḥ||
kāṣṭhakauṣṭhī prasaṅgena nādamantargataṃ śrṛṇu|
purā matsyendrabodhāya ādināthoditaṃ vacaḥ||
manasaḥ pākamāpnoti nirālambākhyaghoṭanam|
nādopastirato nityamavadheyā hi yoginā||
nādaśravaṇataścittamantaraṅgaturaṅgamaḥ|
viśūnyaṃ sarvathaikāgraṃ kutracinna hi dhāvati||
nādo'ntaraṅgasāraṅgabandhamevāgurāyate|
antaraṅgaturaṅgasya bandhane līyate'pi ca||
P.48
ghaṇṭāninādasaktasya śabdaniṣkaraṇasya ca|
anāhatasya śabdasya tasya śabdasya yo dhvaniḥ||
dhvanerantargataṃ jyatirjyotirantargataṃ manaḥ|
tanmanī vilayaṃ yānti tadviṣṇoḥ paramaṃ padam||
tāvadākāṣṭhasaṅkalpya yāvacchabdaḥ pravartate|
niḥśabdaṃ tatparaṃ brahma paramātmā samīryate||
yatkiñcannāmarūpeṇa śrūyate śaktireva sā|
yastacchrotā nirākāraḥ sa eva parameśvaraḥ||
nādakoṭisahasrāṇi vindukoṭiśatāni ca|
sarve tatra layaṃ yānti yatra devo nirañjanaḥ"|| iti |
rājayoge, --
"citte calati saṃsāro'cale layaḥ prajāyate|
tasmāccittaṃ sthiraṃ kuryādaudāsīnyaparāyaṇaḥ||
yadā bhavedudāsīnastadā tattvaṃ prakāśate|
svayaṃ prakāśite tattve svānandastatkṣaṇādbhavet||
ānandena ca santuṣṭaḥ sadābhyāsaparo bhavet|
sadābhyāsaparībhūte na vidhirnaiva ca kramaḥ||
na kiñciccintayedayogī hyaudāsīnyaparo bhavet|
na kiñca......cchayaṇāttasya svayaṃ tattvaprakāśate||
svayaṃ prakāśite tattve lakṣaṇā tanmayo bhavet|
mano dṛśyamidaṃ sarvaṃ yatkiñcitsacarācaram||
manasastūnmanībhāvo dvaitābhāvaṃ pracakṣate|
jāyamāno'manaskasya udāsīnasya tiṣṭhataḥ||
P.49
mṛdatvañca paratvañca śarīrasyāpi jāyate|
śramanaskakṣanitreṇa samūlonmūlīte kṛte||
antaḥkaraṇaśalye tu sukhī sañjāyate muniḥ|
śvāsasūtrasamopetamindriyālayasaṅkulam||
troṭayitvā manojālaṃ mīnavajjāyate sukhī|
praśāntendriyavargo'yaṃ buddhiśaktisamanvitaḥ||
vāyanālayataṃ jitvā manaḥśatraṃ sukhī bhavet|
guṇatrayamayī rajjuḥ sudṛḍha़ātmātmabandhinī||
amanaskakṣureṇaiva kṣiptvā mokṣamavāpnuyāt|
yathā saṃlīyate sarvamastaṃ gacchati bhāskare||
karajālastathā viśvamamanaske vilīyate|
indriyagrāhanirnmukto nirvote nirmalāmṛte||
amanaskahrade snātaḥ paramāmṛtamaśrnute||
praṇaṣṭocchāsaniḥśvāsavidhvastaviṣayagrahaḥ||
niśceṣṭo nirgatārambho hyānandayate yoginam|
utsannasarvasaṅkalpo nirgatāśeṣaceṣṭakaḥ||
svāvagamyolayaḥ ko'pi jayatāṃ vāgagocaraḥ|
ekadaṇḍaṃ tridaṇḍādi yathā bhasmādikantathā||
keśollubchananagnatvaṃ raktavastrasya dhāraṇam|
utpannasvābodhasya udāsīnasya sarvadā||
sadābhyāsaratasyaitanna kutrāpyupayujyate|
tadā dṛṣṭiviśeṣācca vividhānyāsanāni ca||
antaḥkaraṇabhāvācca yogino nopayoginaḥ|
sadā jāgradavasthāyāṃ suptavada yo'vatiṣṭhate||
P.50
niḥśvāsocchāsahīnaśca niścitaṃ mukta eva saḥ|
nidrodau jāgarasyānte yo bhāvastūpajāyate|
taṃ bhāvaṃ bhāvayan yogī niścitaṃ mukta eva saḥ||
yathā suptotthitaḥ kaścid viṣayān pratipadyate|
jāgartyeva tato yogī yoganidrākṣayāttathā||
śrīdāsīnyāmṛtaughena varddhamānena yoginā|
unmūlitamano mūlo jagada vṛkṣaḥ patiṣyati||
yadā yatra yathā yasmāda sthirībhavati mānasam|
tadā yatra mano yāti na vicāryaṃ tatastataḥ|
avāritamanastasamāt svayameva vilīyate||
nivāryamāṇaṃ yatnena tatkartuṃ naiva śakyate|
uttiṣṭhati kṣaṇenaiva mārutasya vaśādadaḥ||
durnivāryaṃ manastāvada yāvattattvaṃ na vindati|
vidite tu pare tattve mano nau stambhakākavat||
yathā tulāṃ tulādhāraḥ cañcalāṃ kurute sthirām|
jāte saukhye sadābhyāsān manovṛttistathātmani||
niḥkṣipte kanake vihāya kaluṣaṃ yada yada bhavennirmalam,
tattattvaṃ sahajasvabhāvamamalaṃ jāte'manaske dhruvam|
tasmāt tyaktvā sakalaviṣayaṃ niṣkalādhyātmayogāt,
vāyornāśastadanumanasastadvināśaśca mokṣaḥ|
saccidevaṃ sahajamamalaṃ niṣkalaṃ nirvikāram,
prāptyai yatnaṃ kuruta kuśalāḥ pūrvamevāmanaskam||
caturvidhā mano'vasthā vijñātavyā manīṣiṇā||
P.51
viśiṣṭaṃ ca gatāyātaṃ suśliṣṭañca sulīnakam||
viśliṣṭaṃ tāmasaṃ proktaṃ rajasantu gatāgatam|
viśliṣṭaṃ sāttvikaṃ proktaṃ rājasantu gatāgatam|
suśliṣṭaṃ sāttvikaṃ proktaṃ sulīnaṃ guṇavarjitam||
viśliṣṭaṃ ca gatāyātaṃ vikalpaviṣayagraham|
suśliṣṭañca sūlīnañca vikalpaviṣayāpaham||
tato'bhyāsaniyogena nirālambaṃ bhaved yadi|
tadā samarasībhūtaṃ paramānandameva tat ||
sānandānniścalaṃ cetaḥ tataḥ suśliṣṭamucyate|
atīva niścalībhūtaṃ sānandañca sulīnakam||
abhyasyato manaḥpūrvaṃ viśliṣṭaṃ calamucyate|
tataścalācalaṃ kiñcit sānandañca gatāgatam||
evambhūtasya karmāṇi puṇyāni saṃśayāni ca|
prayānti naiva limpanti kriyamāṇāni sādhunā|| "iti|
yogārūḍha़sya svarūpaṃ vadan tasyaiva samādhirabhyāsadaśāyāṃ brahmānandāvirbhāvamāha bhagavān vāsudevaḥ|
tathāhi -- "nispṛhaḥ sarvakāmebhyo yukta ityucyate tadā"|
ityārabhya – īkṣate yogayuktātmā sarvatra samadarśanaḥ||
ityanena, śrutiśca, --
"samādhinirdhūtamalasya cetaso—
niveśitasyātmani yatsukhaṃ bhavet|
na śakyate varṇayituṃ girā tadā,
svayaṃ tadantaḥkaraṇena gṛhyata iti ||"
P.52
vidyāraṇyaśrīpādāḥ –
"yāvada yāvadahaṅkāro vismṛto'bhyāsayogataḥ|
tāvattāvat sūkṣmadṛṣṭernijānando'numīyate||
yadyapyasau ciraṃ kālaṃ samādhirdurlabho nṛṇām|
tathāpyakṣaṇiko brahmānandaṃ niścayayatyasāviti||
pātañjale'pi—
guṇabhedena cittasya catastro'vasthā uktāḥ|
vyatthānaṃ, samādhiprārambhaḥ, ekāgratā, nirodhaśceti|
eṣā tu tatra prakriyā | triguṇātmakaprakṛtikāryatvāt cittamapi triguṇam, prakṛtiḥ pūrvamevoktā tattvanirūpaṇāvasare, guṇāstu sattvaṃ rajastamāṃsi| tatra sattvaṃ prasādaprakāśadharmaśīlam, rajaḥpravṛttiparitāpaśokādidharmaśīlam, tamastu pravṛttivirodhisthityākhya dharmaśīlam| gauravāvaraṇadainyādidharmaśīlañca| evañca cittasyaitādṛśaguṇatrayavattvāt, guṇatrayasya ca parasparopamaryopa marddaka rūpavicitrapariṇāmabhedena,
kṣiptamūḍha़vikṣiptaikāgryanirodhākhyabhūmiśabditāḥ pañcāvasthā bhavanti| tatra kṣiptaṃ rajayāteṣu ca viṣayeṣu sukhādiṣu preritamatyantamasthiram| mūḍha़ṃ tamasānidrāvṛttimadamūrchādyupetaṃ krodhādibhiḥ kṛtyākṛtyavivekaśūnyaṃ parāpakāraniratam| vikṣiptam – kṣiptād viśiṣṭam| vaiśiṣṭañca –asthema bahulaṃ kādācitkasthemoyadyapyatrasattvodreko'pyasti tathāpi sattvāt kiñcid ūna raja stamaḥ spṛṣṭatvāt| aiśvarye śabdādi viṣayeṣu ca priyaṃ bhavati| sattvaprādhānyācca tattvaṃ praṇidhitsadapi tattvasya tamasāpihitatvādaṇimādikamevaiśvaryaṃ manyamānaṃ praṇi
P.53
dhitmati| praṇidhatte ca kṣaṇam| atha rajo'śena kṣipyamāṇam yatrālabdhasthiti tatra prayamātraṃ bhavati| śabdādiṣu svarasavāhitvena nirūḍha़ prematvamiti| kṣipte kādācitkasyasamādhervikṣepopasarjanatvāda yoge'nupayogitvamiti| satyapyatravṛttinirodhe pārasparyeṇāpi mokṣasādhanatvābhāvāddheyatva miti| tistro'pyetābhūmayo heyā eva| ekāgratāniruddhetu bhūmīsattvotkarṣāda yathākramaṃ vahirvṛttinirodhaḥ sarvasaṃskāra vilaya dvāreṇa samādhyupayoginyāvityupādeye| tatraikāgyamekatānam, atra hi cittamāvirbhuta satvamayaṃ gatatamaḥpaṭalamapi rajomātrayā saṃspṛṣṭatvād dharmajñāna vairāgyaiśvaryopagaṃbhavati|
yathāsamprajñāta samādhi sampannayormadhumatibhūmikaprajñā jyotiṣormadhyamayoginaḥ cittasatvaniruddhantu-sakalavṛttikaṃ saṃskāramātrabodhaṃ, yathātikrāntabhāvanīyasya yoginaścittasatvamapagata rajoleśamalasvarūpapratiṣṭham, abhyāsa vairāgya puṭapākaprabandhavidhūtarajastamomalasya buddhisattvatapanīyasya svarūpa pratiṣṭhāyām viṣayendriyapratyāhṛtasyānavasitādhikārakatayāca kāryakāriṇo vivekakhyāti paraṃ kāryaviṣaya mavaśiṣyata iti|
atra bhāvanīyapadamātmātirikta vastuparam, evañcaikāgreya vahirvṛttinirodhaḥ| niruddhe ca sarvāsāṃ vṛttīnāṃ sasaṃkārasahitānām pravilayaḥ ityanayorbhūmyoryogasyāsti sambhava iti|
tatra kṣiptamūḍha़e cittabhūmīvyutthānaṃ vikṣiptabhūmiḥ satvodrekāt samādhi prārambhaḥ, ekāgratā nirodhau paryantabhūmī, pratipariṇāmañca saṃskārāḥ, tatra vyatthāna janitā saṃskārāḥ samādhiprārambhajaiḥ saṃskārai rhanyante|
P.54
tajjāścaikāgratājaiḥ| ekāgratā janitānirodhajaiḥ| nirodhajā api saṃskārā staireva hanyante|
yathā suvarṇa smbalitaṃ dhmāyamānaṃ sīsamātmānaṃ suvarṇa malañca nirdahati| evamekāgratā janitān saṃskārān nirodhajā ātmānañca tirddahantīti|
athoktopāyaiḥ sthira cittasya yoginaḥ krameṇa savikalpaka samādhi prakārā āvirbhavanti|
tato'samprajñātākhya nirbījasamādhiścāvirbhavatītye tat pradarśana parāṇi patañjali sūtrāṇi vyākhyāsahitāni pradarśyante|
"kṣīṇavṛtterabhijātasyeva maṇergrahītṛgraṇagrāhyeṣu tatsthatadañjanatā samāpattiḥ", ukta upāyairlabdhasthitikasya cittasya sphaṭikamaṇeriva svabhāvataḥ svasthasya raja stamobhyāmanabhibhave mahābhūtatanmātra sthūla sūkṣmarūpagrāhyaikāgratā pūrvikā grāhyamayatā rūpāgrāhya samāpattiḥ|
tatorajastamoleśānuviddhāntaḥ karaṇaikāgratā pūrvikātammayatārūpā grahaṇasamāpattiḥ, tataḥ sthityākhyaśuddhāntaḥkaraṇe sattvaprakāśaikāgratā pūrvikātanmayatārūpāgrahītṛ samāpattiśca bhavati| sūtretadekāgratā tadañjanatvaṃ tanmayatvanyagbhūtecitte viṣayasya bhāvyamānasya cotkarṣaḥ|
tathāvidhāsamāpattistadrūpapariṇāma ityarthaḥ| sāca samāpattiḥ savitarka nirvitarka savicāra nirvicāra medena caturvidhā, "tatra śabdārthajñānavikalpaiḥ saṅkīrṇā savitarkā samāpattiḥ" śabdārtha vikalpa saṃbhinnaḥ sthūlāvabhāsaḥ sā savikalpā samāpattiḥ| "smṛti pariśuddhau svarūpaśūnyevāryamātranirbhāsā nirvitarkā", yadā artha mātrāviṣṭena tadabhyāsānnāntarīyakatānupagatā saṅketasmṛtistyaktāca, tad yoge ca śrutānusānavikalpau tyaktyau, tadā tacchūnyāyāṃ samādhiprajñāyāṃ svarūpamātreṇaiva sthito'rthaḥ|
tat svarūpatayaiva na tu vikalpate tenākāreṇāpi vindate sā sūthalaviṣayā satī nirvitarkā samāpattiḥ| "etayaiva savicārā nirvicārā ca sūkṣmaviṣayāvyākhyātā" sūkṣmastanmātrendriyādi śabdārtha vikalpa sahitatvena deśakāladharmādyavacchinnaḥ sūkṣmārthaḥ pratibhāti| yasyāṃ sā savicārāsamāpattiḥ|
deśakāladharmādirahito dharmimātratayā sūkṣmo'rthaḥ pratibhāti| yasyāṃ sā nirvicārā samāpattiḥ, sā yogināmeva paraṃ pratyakṣā, asadāropagandhasyābhāvāt| etāsu ca samāpattiṣu savitārkāṃ sthūlaviṣayo mapekṣya nirvitarkāyāḥ prādhyānyaṃ, tato'pi sūkṣmaviṣayāyāḥ savicārāyā stato'pi nirvikalparūpāyāḥ pradhāna paryanta sūkṣmārtha viṣayāyā nirvicārāyāstasyā api nirvicārāyāḥ prakṛṣṭābhyāsavaśānnairmalyaṃ (nirmalaṃ) cittaṃ kleśavāsanā rahitaṃ sthitipravāhayogyaṃ bhavati|
"ṛtambharā tatra prajñā" tadā ca viparyaya gandhenāpi na saṃspṛṣṭā ṛtambharākhyā prajñā jāyate| "śrutānumāna prajñābhyāmanyaviṣayāviśeṣārthatvāt" yā ṛtambharā prajñā sā śabdānumāna jñāna vilakṣaṇā sūkṣma vyavahitān viṣayān sphuṭe naiva rūpeṇa bhāsayati| tathāca bhāṣyakṛdbhiruttolitaṃ ślokadvayam|
"prajñā-prasādamāruhya aśocyaḥ śocato janān| 
bhūmiṣṭhāniva śailasthaḥ sarvān prājño'nupaśyati||"
P.56
"āgamenānumānena dhyānābhyāsa rasena ca|
tridhā prakalpayan prajñāṃ labhate yogamuttamam|| " iti |
"tajja saṃskāro'nyasaṃskāra pratibandhī" etaj janitaiśca saṃskārairvikṣepajāḥ saṃskārāḥ kāryakṣamā bhavanti| "tā eva sabījaḥ samādhiḥ " tadevamuktāścatasraḥ samāpattayaḥ| sahabījenā lambanena vartanta iti vyutpattyā sabījasamādhirityucyate| yaḥ savikalpaḥ sa saṃprajñātasamādhiriti pūrvamūktaḥ, "tasyāpi sabījasyāpi pravilaye sati sarvāsāṃ cittavṛttīnāṃ svakāraṇe pravilayādyā yā saṃskāramātrād vṛttirudeti|
tasyāstasyāneti netīti kevalaṃ paryudasanānnirvikalpaḥ nirbījākhyo'saṃprajñātasamādhirāvirbhavati| yasmin sati puruṣaḥ svarūpamātrajyotiḥ pratiṣṭhaḥ śuddho bhavati| ayameva nirbījasamādhyākhyo yogaḥ paramapuruṣārthahetuḥ, naitad vyatirekena sā mokṣasambhāvanāpyasti| atra ca śruti smṛtītihāsapurāṇādivākyaṃ pramāṇam|
vāsiṣṭhe—
"duḥsahārāma saṃsāro viṣavegṛviṣūcikā|
yoga gāruḍa़ mantreṇa pāvanenopaśāmyati||"
garuḍa़purāṇe—
"tathā yateta matimān yathā syānnirvṛtiḥ parā|
yogenalabhyate sā tu na cānyena tu kenacit||
bhavatāpaprataptānāṃ yogohi paramauṣadham|
parāvara prasaktādhī ryasya nirbveda sambhavā||
P.57
sa ca yogāgninā dagdhaḥ samastakleśa saṃkṣayaḥ|
nirvāṇaṃ paramaṃ sarvaṃ prāpnotyapi na saṃśayaḥ ||
saṃprāptayoga siddhistu pūrṇā yat svātmadarśanārt|
na kiñcid dṛśyate kāryaṃ tenaiva sakalaṃ kṛtam|
ātmārāmaḥ sadāpūrṇaḥ sukhaṃ mat pratikaṃ gataḥ|
atastasyāpi nirvbedaḥ syādānandamayasyaca|
atastasyāpi nirvbedaḥ syādānandamayasyaca||
tapasā bhāvitātmāno yogino saṃyatendriyāḥ|
pratarantimahātmāno yogenaiva mahārṇavam|| "
yājñavalkayasmṛtau—
"ijyācāra dayā hiṃsā dānaṃ svādhyāya karmaṇām|
ayantu paramodharmno yad yogenātmadarśanam"|| iti|
maharṣimātaṅgaḥ –
"agniṣṭomādikān yajñān vihāya dvijasattamaḥ|
yogabhyāsa rataḥ śāntaḥ paraṃ brahmādhigacchati||
brāhmaṇa kṣatriya viśāṃ strī śūdrasya ca pāvanam|
śāntaye karmaṇāmanyad yogānnāsti vimuktaye||
rurmapurāṇe śivāvākyaṃ –
"ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi yogaṃ parama durllabham|
yenātmānaṃ prapaśyanti bhānumanta miveśvaram|
yogāgnirdahati kṣipramaśeṣaṃ pāpapañjaram|
prasannaṃ jāyate jñānaṃ sākṣānnirvāṇa siddhidam||"
"sayāsino gṛhasyasya vanastha-brahmacāriṇaḥ
teṣā nityābhiyuktānāṃ māyātattvaṃ samutthitam||
P.58
nāśayāmi tamaḥkṛtsnaṃ jñānadīpeṇa mācirāt|
vidhūyamohakalilaṃ labdhvāyogamanuttamam||
gṛhastho mucyate bandhānnātra kāryā vicāraṇā|"
tathā ca vānaprastha prakaraṇe—
"yogābhyāsarataścasyādrudādhyāyī bhavet sade" ti|
agninātmani saṃsthāpya dvijaḥ pravrajitobhavet|
yogābhyāsa rato nitya mātmavidyā parāyaṇa" iti|
tathāhi yatidharma prakaraṇe manuḥ –
"bhūtabhāvena vekṣeta yogena paramātmanaḥ|
deheṣu caivopapatti muttameṣvadhameṣu ca ||"
narasiṃha purāṇe—
"yogābhyāsa ratasyeha puḥsonaśyati pātakam|
vighātaye'bhyāsa sampanno yaḥ pumān yogatatparaḥ||
dehadvayaṃ vihāyāśu muktobhavati bandhanāt|"
skandapurāṇe—
"atyalpo'pi yathā dīpo sumahan nāśayettamaḥ|
yogābhyāsastathālpopi mahāpāpaṃ vināśayet|| "
viṣṇudharme—
"yacchreyaḥ sarvabhūtānāṃ strīṇāmapyupakārakam| 
apikīṭa pataṅgebhya stannaḥ śreyaḥ paraṃ vada||
ityuktaḥ kapilaḥ pūrvaṃ devairdevarṣibhistathā|
yoga eva paraṃ śreya steṣāṃ pratyuktavāniti||"
mahābhārate yogamārgaṃ prakṛtyāha vyāsaḥ—
"api varṇāpakṛṣṭasya nārīvā dharmakāṅghinī|
P.59
tāvapyetena mārgeṇa gacchetāṃ paramāṃ gatim||
yadivā sarvadharmajño yadivāpyakṛtī pumān|
yadivā dhārmika śreṣṭho yadivā pāpakṛttamaḥ|
yadivā puruṣavyāghraḥ yadivā klaivyadhārakaḥ||
taratyeva mahāduḥkhaṃ jarāmaraṇa sāgaram|
api jijñāsamāno'pi śabdabrahmāti vartate||" iti|
yogatattva-prakāśe dattātreyaḥ –
"brāhmaṇaḥ śramaṇovāpi bauddhovā patito'pivā|
kāpāliko vā cārvākaḥ śraddhayā sahitaḥ sudhīḥ||
yogābhyāsa rato nityaṃ sarvasiddhamavāpnuyāt| " iti|
śrīmada bhagavad gītāyām –
"yat sāṃkhyaiḥ prāpyate sthānaṃ tad yogairapi gamyate|
ekaṃ sāṃkhyañca yogañca yaḥ paśyati sa paśyati||
yogayukto viśuddhātmā vijitātmā jitendriya|
sarvabhūtātmabhūtātmā kurvannapi na lipyate||
nāsti buddhirayuktasya na cā yuktasya bhāvanā|
na cā bhāvayataḥ śāntiraśāntasya kutaḥ sukham||
yuñjanneva sadātmānaṃ yogī niyata mānasaḥ|
śāntiṃ nirvāṃṇa paramāṃ matsaṃsthāmadhigacchati||
nehābhikramanāśo'sti pratyavāyo navidyate|
svalpamapyasya dharmasya trāyate mahato bhayāt||
vedeṣu yajñeṣu tapaḥsu caiva, dāneṣu yatpuṇyaphalaṃ pradiṣṭam|
atyeti tatsarvamidaṃ viditvā, yogīparaṃsthānamupaiticādyam||
"tasmād yogāya yujyasva yogaḥ karmaṣu kauśalam|| "
P.60
yogaśastre ca –
"etad vimuktisopānaṃ etatkālasya vañcanam|
yad vyāvṛttaṃ manobhyāsādāsaktaṃ paramātmani ||"
yogavīje—
"namasta ādi nāthāya viśvanāthāya te namaḥ|
namaste viśvarūpāya viśvātītāya te namaḥ||
utpatti sthiti saṃhāra kāriṇe te namonamaḥ|
namaste devadeveśa namasye paramātmane||
namaste paripūrṇāya jagadānanda hetave|
sadāśiva jagannātha karuṇāmṛta vāridhe ! ||
sarve jīvāḥ sukhairduḥkhairmāyājālena veṣṭitāḥ|
teṣāṃ muktiṃ kathaṃ deva kṛpayā vada śaṅkara ||
sarvasiddhikaromārgo māyājālanikṛntakaḥ|
janmamṛtyujarāvyādhināśakaḥ sukhadaśca yaḥ||
nānāmārgāstvayā deva kathitāstu maheśvara! |
adhunā mokṣadaṃ mārgaṃ brūhi yogavidāmvara||"
tathāhi īśvara uvāca, --
"vaddhāyeva vimucyante devimārga mataḥ param|
tamahaṃ kathayiṣyāmi tava prītyā sureśvari||
nānāmārgaistu duṣprāpyaṃ kaivalyaṃ paramaṃ padam|
siddhamārgeṇa labhyeta nānyasmācchiva bhāṣitam|
anekaśata saṃkhyābhistarkavyākaraṇādibhiḥ|
patitāḥ śāstrajāleṣu prajñayā te vimohitāḥ||
anirvācyaṃ padaṃ vaktuṃ śakyate na surairapi|
svātma-prakāśa rūpaṃ tat kiṃ śāstreṇa prakāśyate||
niṣkalaṃ nirmalaṃ śāntaṃ sarvātītaṃ nirāmayam|
tadetajjīvarūpeṇa puṇya pāpa phalairvṛtam||"
devyuvāca,--

"paramātma padaṃ nityaṃ tatkathaṃ jīvatāṃ gatam|
tattavātītaṃ mahādevaṃ prasādāt kathayasvame|"
īśvara uvāca, --
"sarvabhāva padātītaṃ jñānarūpaṃ nirañcanam|
vārivat sphuritaṃ svasmin tatohaṅkāra utthitaḥ||
pañcātmakamabhūtapiṇḍaṃ tridhātuka guṇātmakam|
sukhaduḥkhaiḥ sadāyuktaṃ jīva bhāvavayā kulam||
tena jīvābhidhā proktā viśuddhe paramātmani|
kāmaḥ krodho bhayaṃ cintā lobhamoha madādayaḥ||
jarāmṛtyuśca kārpaṇyaṃ śokatandrā kṣudhātṛṣā|
dveṣolajjā sukhaṃ duḥkhaṃ viṣādo harṣa evaca|
jāgrat svapna suṣuptiśca śaṅkā garva stathaivaca||
ebhirddoṣairvinirmuktaḥ sajīvaḥ śiva eva hi||
tasmāddoṣavināśārtha mupāyaṃ kathayāmi te|
jñānaṃ kecidvadantyatra kevalaṃ tattava siddhaye||
yogahīnaṃ kathaṃ jñānaṃ muktiḥ sambhavatīśvari|
yogo'pi jñānahīnastu lakṣamo mokṣakarmaṇi"||
devyuvāca, --
"ajñānādeva saṃsāro jñānādeva vimucyate|
P.62
yogeneṣṭantu kiṃ kāryaṃ me prasannagirā vada||"
īśvara uvāca, --
"satyameva tvayoktante kathayāmi sureśvari! |

"svātmarūpaṃ yadājñātaṃ pūrṇaṃ tadvayāpakantathā|"

"yadi pūrṇa svarūpantat satyametadvarānane ! |
sakalanniṣkalañcaiva pūrṇatvācca tadevahi||

niṣphalaṃ nirmalaṃ sākṣāt svarūpaṅganopamam||
utpatti sthiti saṃhāra sphūrtijñāna vivarjitam|
evaṃ rūpaṃ samāyātaṃ sakalaṃ mohasāgare !||
nimajjati varārohe tavatto'vidyāḥ punaḥ punaḥ|
sukhaduḥṇvādimoheṣu yathā saṃsāriṇaḥ sthitiḥ||
tathā jñānī yadātiṣṭhe dvāsanā vāsita stadā|
tayornāsti viśeṣo'tra samā saṃsāra bhāvanā||

"anyat kiñcitparijñeyaṃ jñānināṃ nāsti śaṅkara|

yogavīje devyuvāca, --
"agre'gnyathe mayāproktaṃ kathayāmi sureśvari|
ādinātha mahāmārga eka eva hi nānyathā||
dvidheva sampratīyeta janmāntara vibhedataḥ|
krameṇa prāpyate prāpyamabhyāsādeva nānyathā||
ekenaiva śarīreṇa yogābhyāsaḥ śanaiḥ śanaiḥ|
cirātsamprāpyate siddhirmarkaṭakrama evaca||
yogasiddhiṃ vinādehaḥ pramādāda yadi naśyati|
parvavāsanāyāyuktaḥ śarīraṃ cānyadāpnuyāt||
tataḥ puṇyavaśāda siddhyed guruṇāsaha saṅgatiḥ|
paścimadvāramārgeṇa jāyate tvaritaṃ phalam||
pūrvajanmakṛtābhyāsātsatvaraṃ phalamaśnute|
etadeva hi vijñeyaṃ tat kākamatamuttamam||
tasmātkākamatānnāsti hyabhyāsasya parampadam|" iti|
bhagavadgītāyāmarjuna uvāca, --
"ayatiḥ śraddhayopeta" ityārabhya "tatoyāti parāṅgatim"
P.77
ityantaṃ praśnottararūpaṃ kīdṛśo yogomayāpivijñeya iti| śraṅgayā yogaṃ jijñāsamāno'pi puruṣaḥ śabdabrahmavedaḥ, tasyātivartanañcedaṃ sarvavedāttu sat (rva) phalāpekṣayā adhikaṃ cirakālaṃ brahmādilokavāsalakṣaṇaṃ phalaṃ prāpnoti|
yadā yogajijñāsā mātreṇaitādṛśaṃ phalaṃ tadā kimuvaktavyam yogābhyāsena phalādhikyamiti kaimutikanyāyamāha "jijñāsupari" ityādinā|
rājayoge, --
"ādiśaktirumācaiṣā mattolabdhavatī purā|
adhunājanmasaṃskāraṃ tvameko labdhavānasi||
anyajanmakṛtābhyāsāt svayaṃ tattvaṃ prakāśate|
suptotthito'sau pratyakṣaṃ hyupadeśādinā vinā ||" iti|
skāndhe, --
"amārgeṇāpi yoyogaṃ addhadhānaḥ samabhyaset|
so'pi kālena bahunā dhruvaṃ yogamavāpnuyāt|| (iti)|
jaigīṣavyo yathāvipro yathācaivāsitādayaḥ|
kṣatriyā janakādyāstu tulādhārādayoviśaḥ||
sampāptāḥ paramāṃ siddhiṃ pūrvābhyastayasvogataḥ|
dharmavyādhādayaḥ sapta śūdrapailavakādayaḥ||
maitreyī sulabhā gārgī śāṇḍilī ca tapasvinī|
etecānye ca bahavo nīcayonigatā api||
jñānaniṣṭhāṃ parāṃprāptāḥ pūrvajanmasamādhitaḥ||"
kiñca pūrvajanmābhyastoyagāḥ svābhyāsapuṇyatāratamyena kecid 
P.78
brahmatvam, keciddevarṣitvaṃ kecidbahmaputratvam, kecinmunitvam, bhayā vattvañca prāptāḥ santastatropadeśaṃ vinaivātmasākṣātkāravanto vabhubuḥ|
yathā hiraṇyagarbha-vasiṣṭha-nārada-sanatkumāra-vāmadeva-śukādayo janmasiddhā ityeva purāṇādiṣu śrūyate|
"nāsti rāgasamaṃ duḥkhaṃ nāsti tyāgasamaṃ sukham|
nāsti śumbhusamo devo nāsti yogasamā gatiḥ||"
bhūmikāstu heyaduḥkhātyantahānākhyakaivalyasādhanībhūtaṃ tatva jñānaṃ saptamaprajñāyāṃ nirūpayatā bhagavatā patañjalinā saptaproktāḥ| saptavidhā tasya prāntabhūmī prajñeti sūtreṇa | sadvyākhyānaṃ utpanna vivekakhyāt puṃsaḥ prāntabhūmī sakala sālambanasamādhiparyanta prajñā saptavidhā bhavati, tatra kāryavimuktirūpā caturvidhā jñātaṃ mayā jñeyaṃ, (na jñātaṃ) na jñātavyaṃ kiñcidanti [1] kṣīṇāmekleśeḥ na kṣetavyaṃ kiñcidasti [2] adhigataṃ mayā hānam [3] prāptaḥ mayā viveka khyātiriti [4] pratyayāntara parihāreṇa tasyāṃ avasthāyāṃ īdṛśyeva prajñā jāyate| eṣādhīḥ kāryaviṣaya nirmalajñānarūpatvāt kāryavimuktirityucyate|
cittavimuktistrividhā, caritārthā me buddhiḥ [1] guṇāḥ kṛtvādhikārāḥ giriśikharapatitā grāvāṇa iva na punaḥ sthitiṃ yāsyanti svakāraṇe pralayābhimukhānāṃ eṣāṃ mohasya mūlakāraṇābhāvānniṣprayojanatvācca kutaḥ pravāhaḥ? [2] sātmībhūtaśca me samādhi [3] stasmin sati svarūpasya ca "aitadātmyamidaṃ sarvaṃ tat satyaṃ sa ātmā tatvamasi" kamihānyaṃ vā vadiṣyata sa etameva puruṣaṃ tamapaśyaditi" "eṣata ātmā tvayyamṛta ... ... iti nānyade ... ... ... "ahaṃ tattvamasi tvañcāha
P.79
masmi" ityādiśrutibhi rbrahmābhedasiddherahaṃ brahmāsmītyākāro vivekakhyātau bhavatyeva| asyāpi na sākṣānmokṣasādhanatvam| kintvetadanantarabhāvi nirvikalpaka dvāraiva tasyaiva sarvavṛttinirodharūpasya sukhyayogasya mokṣasādhanatvena pūrvopanyasteṣu smṛti purāṇavākyeṣu nirddeśāt| asyāśca hānasādhanībhūtāyā vivekakhyāterutpattauyamaniyamādyaṅgānuṣṭhānameva cittaśuddhi dvārā nimittamiti vakṣyate| etāstu vāśiṣṭe vistareṇa proktāḥ|
tathāhi vaśiṣṭha uvāca, --
"imāṃ saptapadāṃ jñānabhūmimākalayānagha!||
bhūmikayānayā bhūyo mohapaṅke nimajjati|
vadanti bahubhedena vādino yogabhūmikāḥ||
mamatvarahitānyūnabhimā eva sukhapradāḥ|
avabodhaṃ vidhijñānaṃ tadidaṃ saptabhūmikam||
suktistu jñeyamityāste bhūmikā bhūmikāntare|
jñānabhūmiḥ śubhecchākhyā prathamā samudāhṛtā|
vicāraṇā dvitīyātu tṛtīyā tanumānasā|
satvāpattiścaturthī syāt tato'saṃsaktināmikā||
padārthabhāvinī ṣaṣṭhī saptamī turyagā smṛtā|
āsāmantasthitāmuktiryasyāṃ bhūyo na śacati ||
etāsāṃ bhūmikānāṃ tvabhidaṃ nirvacanaṃ śṛṇu|
kiṃ mūḍha़ iva tiṣṭhāmi prakṣe'haṃ śāsvasajjanaiḥ||
vairāgyapūrvamicchati śubhecchetyucyate budhaiḥ|
śāstrasajjanasamparkairvairāgyābhyāsapūrvakam|
sadācārapravṛttiryā procyate sā vicāraṇā||
P.80
vicāraṇā śubhecchābhyāṃ bhindriyārtheṣvaraktatā|
yatra sā tanutābhāvāt procyate tanumānasā||
bhūmikā tritayābhyāsā ccitte'rthaviratervaśāt|
satvātmani sthite śuddhe satvāpattirudāhṛtā||
daśā catuṣṭayābhyāsā dasaṃsargaphalābhṛśam|
rūḍha़satvacamatkārā proktā saṃsaktināmikā||
bhūmikā pañcakābhyāsāt svātmārāmatayā dṛḍha़m|
ābhyantarāṇāṃ vāhyānāṃ padārthānāñca bhāvanāt||
paraprayuktena ciraṃ prayatnenāvabodhanam|
padārthabhāvinī nāma ṣaṣṭhī sañjāyate gatiḥ||
bhūmi ṣaṭkacirābhyāsāt bhedasyānupalambhataḥ|
yat svabhāvaikaniṣṭhatvaṃ sā jñeyā turyagāgatiḥ||
eṣā hi jīvanmukteṣu turyāvastheha vidyate|
videhamuktiviṣaye turyātītamataḥparam"||
rāma uvāca—
"saptānāṃ yogabhūmīnāmabhyāsaḥ kriyate katham|
kīdṛśāni tu cihnāni bhūmikāṃ pratiyoginaḥ"||
vasiṣṭha uvāca—
"pravṛttaśca nivṛttaśca bhavati dvividhaḥ pumān|
svargāpavargonmukhayoḥ śṛṇu lakṣaṇametayoḥ|
kiyattannāmanirvāṇaṃ varasaṃsṛtireva me|
iti nirṇaya kartā yaḥ sapravṛtta udāhṛtaḥ||
calārgalayugacchidra kūrmagrīvā praveśavat|
anekajanmanāmante vivekī jāyate pumān||
P.81
asāro vata saṃsāro vvavasthānaṃ samaitayā|
kiṃ karmabhiḥ paryuṣitairdinaṃ tenaiva nīyate||
kriyātiśayanirmuktaṃ kiṃ syād viśramaṇaṃ param|
iti niścayavānantaḥ sa nivṛtti riti smṛtaḥ||
kathaṃ virāgavān bhūtvā saṃsārābdhiṃ tarāmyaham|
evaṃ nivāraṇaparo yadā bhavati sammatiḥ||
virāgamupayātyantarvāsanāsvanuvāsaram|
kriyāsūdārūṇāsu ca kramate modate'nvaham||
grābhyāsu bahuceṣṭāsu satataṃ vicikitsati|
nodāharati karmāṇi puṇyakarmāṇi sevate||
pāpādvibheti satataṃ na ca bhogamapekṣate|
strehapraṇayagarbhāṇi peśalānyucitāni ca||
deśakālopapannāni vacanānyabhibhāṣate|
tadāsau prathamāmekāṃ prāpto bhavati bhūmikām||
manasā karmaṇā vācā sajjanānupasevate|
yataḥ kutaścidānīya jñānaśāstrāṇyavekṣate||
evaṃ vicāraṇānāmnīmāgatī yogabhūmikām|
śrutismṛtisadācāradhāraṇādhyānakarmaṇā|
mukhyayā vyākhyayā khyātān śrayati śreṣṭhapaṇḍitān||
padārthapravibhāgajñaḥ kāryākāryavinirṇayam|
jānātyadhigataśravyo gṛhaṃ gṛhapati ryathā||
sadābhimānamātsaryadambhamohātiśayyatām|
vahirapyāsthitāmīśa tyajatyahiriva tvacam||
itthaṃbhūtamatiḥ śāstre gurusajjanasevayā|
P.82
sarahasyamaśeṣeṇa yathāvadadhigacchati||
asaṃsargābhidhāmanyāṃ tṛtīyāṃ yogabhūmikām|
tataḥ patatyasaukāṇḍaḥ puṣpaśayyāmivāmalām|
yathāvacchāstravākyārthe matimādhāya niścalām|
tāpasāśramaviśrāntairadhyātmakathanakramaiḥ||
saṃsāranindakaistadvadadhyātmakathanakramaiḥ|
śilāśayyāsanāsīno jarayatyāpurātanam||
vanāvanīvihāreṇa cittopaśamaśobhinā|
asaṅgamukhasaukhyena kālaṃ nayati rītimān||
abhyāsāt sādhuśāstrāṇāṃ karaṇātpuṇyakarmaṇām|
jantoryathāvadeveyaṃ vastudṛṣṭiḥ prasīdati|
tṛtīyāṃ bhūmikāṃ prāpya buddho'nubhavati svayam||
dviprakāramasaṅgaṃ syāttasya bhedamimaṃ śrṛṇu|
dvividho'yamasaṅgaḥ syāt sāmānyaḥ śreṣṭha eva ca||
nāhaṅkartācabhoktā ca na bādhyo na ca bādhakaḥ|
ityasañjanamartheṣu sāmānyaṃ saṅganāmakam||
prākkarma nirmitaṃ sarvamīśvarādhanameva vā|
sukhaṃ vā yadi vā duḥkhaṃ kaivātra mama kartṛtā ||
bhobhābhogā mahārogāḥ sampradaḥ paramāpadaḥ|
viyogāyaiva saṃyogā ādhayo vyādhayo dhiyaḥ||
kālakavalanodayuktasarvabhāvān vināśayam|
tiṣṭhatīti vicāro yaḥ sāmānyo'sāvasaṅgamaḥ||
anena kramayogeṇa saṃyogena mahātmanām|
viyogenāsatāmantaḥ prayogenātmasambidām||
P.83
pauruṣeṇa prayatnena santatābhyāsayogataḥ|
karāmalakavadvastunyāgate sphuṭatāṃ dṛḍha़m||

kṛtvā dūratare nnyūnamiti śabdārthabhāvanām||
saṃsārāmbunidheḥ pāre'sāre paramakāraṇe|
na padārtheṇa vākyena jaḍa़ena cetanena ca|
āsitaṃ bhāṣaṇaṃ sitamabhāṣaṇamabhāvanam|

vivekapaṅkerūḍha़o'nta-vivekārkavikāśitaḥ|
phalaṃ phalatyasaṅgākhyāṃ tṛtīyābhūmikābhimām ||

yenāṃśenollasatyeṣā vicāreṇodayannayet |
tamevānudinaṃ yatnāt kṛṣībala ivāṅkuram ||
... ... ... ...
dvitīyāṃ bhūmikāṃ yatnān tṛtīyāṃ prāpnuyāttataḥ|

bhavati proṣitāśeṣasaṅkalyakalanakramāt|

P.84
jāgratīvātra vispaṣṭaṃ bhedabuddhyanuvartanāt|
udeti yogayuktānāmatra kevalamarpitā||
yāṃ dṛṣṭvā mūḍha़buddhīnāṃ samudeti mukṣutā|
kartavyamācaran kāmamakartavyamanācaran||
tiṣṭati prakṛtācāre yaḥ sa ārya iti smataḥ|
prathamāyāmaṅkuritaṃ bhūmau vikāśitantataḥ||
dvitīyāyāṃ tṛtīyāyāṃ phalatyāryatvamuttamam|
arthitāyāṃ mṛto yogī śuddhaḥ saṅkalpasambhṛtān||
bhogān bhuktvā ciraṃ kālaṃ yogavān jāyate punaḥ|
bhūmikā tritayābhyāsādajñāne kṣayamāgate|
sabhyajjñānodaye citte pūrṇacandrodayopamam ||
nirvibhāgamanādyante yogino yuktacetasaḥ|
samaṃ sarvaṃ prapaśyanti caturthīṃ bhūmikāṃ gatāḥ||
advaite sthairyamāyāte dvaite praśamamāgate|
paśyanti svapnavallokaṃ caturthī bhūmikāmitāḥ||
bhūmikā tritayaṃ jāgraccaturthī svapna ucyate|
cittantu śaradabhrāṃśa-vilāpaṃ pravilīyate||
satvāvaśeṣa evāste pañcamīṃ bhūmikāṅgataḥ|
jagadvikalpadoteti cittasyātra vilāpanāt||
pañcamīṃ bhūmikāmetya susaptapadanāsikām|
śāntāśeṣa viśeṣāṃśa stiṣṭhtyadvaitamātrake||
galita-dvaitanirvbāso mudito'ntaḥ prabuddhavān|
susaptadhanaevāste pañjamībhūmikāṅgataḥ||
antarmukhatayotiṣṭhan varhirvṛttiparo'pi san|
P.85
parisantatayānityaṃ nidrālurivalakṣyase||
kurvannabhyāsametasyānbhūmikāyāmbivāsanaḥ|
ṣaṣṭīnturyābhidāmanyāṃ kramāt patati bhūmikām||
yatranāsanna sadrūponā'hannāpyanahaṅkṛtiḥ|
kevalaṃ kṣīṇabhanana āstedvaitaikya nirgataḥ||
nirgranthiḥ śānta sandeho jīvanmukta vibhāvanaḥ||
evarvāṇo'pi nirvāṇaścittaṃ dīpa iva sthitaḥ||
antaḥśūnyo vahiḥśūnyaḥ śūnyakumbha ivāmbare|
antaḥpūrṇo vahiḥpūrṇaḥ pūṇakumbha ivārṇave||
kiñcidevaiṣa sampannaḥ tathaivaiṣa na kiñcana|
ṣaṣṭhyāṃ bhūmyāṃ samaḥsthitvā saptamīnbhūmimāpnuyāt|
videha muktitātūktā saptamī yogabhūmikā|
keścit sāśiva ityuktvā kaiścibrahmetyudāhṛtā||
kaiścidviṣṇuriti proktā kaiścit śūnyamitismṛtā|
arthaityuditākaiścit kaiścitkāla itīritā||
kaiścit prakṛti pumbhāva vibhāga iti bhāgitā|
anyairapyanyathā nānā bhedairātma vikalpitaiḥ||
nityamavyapadeśyāpi kathañcidvyapadiśyate|
saptaitābhūmikāḥ proktā mayā tava radhūdvaha ||
āsāmabhyāsayogena na duḥkhamanubhūyata iti |"
iti śrīparamahaṃsaparibrājakācarya-śrīrāmajandrasadānandasarasvatīśiṣyaśivānandaviracitaṃ yogacintāmaṇī prathamaḥ paricchedaḥ ||1||
P.86
dvitīyo'dhyāyaḥ|
tatra yogiṇaḥ kṛtyākṛtyanirṇayaḥ|
samyagārādhita-sadaguruprasādādāsādita-sarahasya-sāṅgayogo nirantarābhyasanaśīlo yathoktaṃ phalaṃ prāpnotyeva pumān|
