"ācāryād yogasarvasvamavāpya sthiradhīḥ svayam|
yathoktaṃ labhate tena prāpnotyapi ca nirvṛtim||" iti|
sureśvarācāryaḥ –
"guruprasādāllabhate yogamaṣṭāṅgasaṃyutam|
śivaprasādāllabhate yogasiddhiñca śāśvatīm||" iti|
śrutiśca –
"yasya deve parābhaktiryathā deve tathā gurau|
tasyaite kathitā hyarthāḥ prakāśante mahātmanaḥ||"
"ācāryavān puruṣo vede" ti|
yogavīje—
"marujjayo yasya siddhestaṃ seveta guruṃ sadā|
guruvaktaprasādena kuryāt prāṇajayambudhaḥ||"
yājñavalkyaḥ –
"guravaḥ pañca sarveṣāṃ catūrṇāṃ śruti coditāḥ|
mātāpitā tathācāryo mātulaḥ śvaśurastathā||
eṣāṃ mukhyāstrayaḥ proktā ācāryaḥ pitarau tathā|
eṣāṃ mukhyatamasveka ācāryaḥ paramārthavida||
P.87
tasmāt sarveṣu kāleṣu sarvairniḥśreyasārthibhiḥ|
guravaḥ smṛtisampannāḥ pūjyā vāṅmanasādibhiriti||"
rājayoge -- "vedāntatarkoktibhirāgamaiśca,
nānāvidhaiḥ śāstrakadambakaiśca|
dhyānādibhiḥ satkaraṇairna gamyaḥ,
cintāmaṇiṃ hyekaguruṃ vihāya||
dṛṣṭiḥ sthirā yasya vinaiva dṛśyam,
vāyuḥ sthiro yasya vinā prayatnam|
cittaṃ sthiraṃ yasya vināvalambam,
sa eva yogī sa guruḥ sa sevyaḥ||
gururbrahmā gururviṣṇu gururdevo maheśvaraḥ|
garudevātparaṃ nāsti tasmāttaṃ pūjayetsadā||
guruṇā darśite tattve tatkṣaṇāttanmayo bhavet|
matvā vimukta ātmānaṃ bhucyate nātra saṃśayaḥ||
yathāsiddhirasasparśāttāmraṃ bhavati kāñcanam|
gurūpadeśaśrabaṇācchiṣyastattvamayastathā ||"
na kevalaṃ gurūpadeśamātrādeva siddhiḥ svasyaiva pariśramādeva sarvaḥ sarvamāpranoti |
haṭhapradīpikāyām, --
"śrutapratītiḥ svagurupratītiḥ,
svātmapratītirmanaso nirodhaḥ|
etāni svvāṇi samuccitāni,
matāni dhīrairiha kāraṇāni||"
vāsiṣṭhe, -- "upadeśakramo rāma vyavasthāmātrapālanam|
P.88
japtestu kāraṇaṃ śuddhā śiṣyaprajñaiva kevalam|
vinivāritasarvārthādapahastitabandhanāt||
na svadhairyādṛte kaścidabhyuddharati saṅkaṭāt|
vairāgyeṇātha śāśtreṇa mahattvādiguṇairapi||
yatnenāpadvighātārthaṃ svayamullāsayenmanaḥ|
na tattribhuvanaiśvaryānna kośāddhanadhāriṇaḥ||
phalamāsādyate cittād yanmahatvopavṛṃhitāt||"
rājayoge,--
"tasmādupāsīta samyak sahajaṃ prāpya sadguroḥ|
anāyāsena satatamātmābhyāsavato bhavet||
nityābhyasanaśīlasya svasambedyaṃ hi tad bhavet| --
idaṃ taditi nirddeṣṭuṃ (tadvaktuṃ) guruṇāpi na śakyate|| 
vāṅmanaḥ kāyasaṃyogaṃ prayatnena vivarjayet|
brahmāhamasmītyātmānaṃ susthiraṃ dhārayetsadā||"
haṭhadīpikāyām , --
"varjayeddurjane prītiṃ vāhni-strī patha-sevanam|
prātaḥsnānopavāsādi-kāyakle śādikantathā||
atyāhāraḥ prayāsaśca prajalpo niyamagrahaḥ|
janasaṅgaśca līlyañca ṣaḍbhiryogaḥ praṇaśyati||
utsāhāt sāhasād dhairyāttatvajñānaviniścayāt|
janasaṅga parityāgat ṣaḍbhiryogaḥ prasidhyati||
mārutasya vidhiṃ sarvaṃ manoyukta samabhyaset|
anyathā tu na kartavyā manovṛttirmanīṣiṇā||" iti|
ja.89
liṅgapurāṇe, --
"taptāṅgāra samānārī ghṛtakumbhasamaḥpumān|
tasmānnārīṣu saṃsargaṃ yatnataḥ parivarjayet||" iti|
nārī saṃsarga varjanavanmahāvighnabhūtāṃ dhūrtagoṣṭhīñjaprayatnena vivarjayet|
dhūrtasvarūpantu yathā, yogatattvaprakāśe dattātreyeṇoktam, --
"muṇḍito daṇḍadhārīvā kāṣāyavasano'pi vā|
nārāyaṇa vaco vādī jaṭilo bhasmalepanaḥ||
namaḥ śivāya vākye vā svācāra paripūjakaḥ|
sthāna dvādaśa śuddho vā mālā bāhulya bhūṣitaḥ||
kriyāhīno vākyaśūraḥ kathaṃ siddhimavāpnuyāt|
na veṣa dhāraṇaṃ siddheḥ kāraṇaṃ na ca tatkathā|
kriyaiva kāraṇaṃ siddheḥ satyametaddhi sāṅkṛte|
śiśnodarārthaṃ yogasya kathayā veśadhāriṇaḥ||
anuṣṭhānavihīnāstu vañcayatyakhilān janān|
uccāvacaiḥ pralāpaiste kuśalā dāmbhikānarāḥ||
yogino vayamityāhurmūḍha़ābhogaparāyaṇāḥ|
śanaiḥ śanaistathā vidvān yogābhyāsavivarjitān||
kṛtārthaḥ vacanai reva varjayed veṣadhāriṇaḥ|
yataste'mī vighnabhūtā yogābhyāsasya sarvadā ||
varjayanti prayatnena tādṛśān sudhiyonarāḥ|" iti|
vāśiṣṭhe –
"yasya strī tasya bhogecchā niḥstrīkasya kka bhogabhūḥ ?|
striyaṃ tyaktvā jagāttyaktaṃ jagat tyaktvā sukhībhavet||
P.90
na viṣaṃ viṣamityāhu rviṣayā viṣamaṃ viṣam|
janmāntara hā viṣayā ekajanma haraṃ viṣam||
yathā śāstraṃ viharatā tvayā kāryā na siddhiṣu|
ciraṃ kāla parāpakkā siddhiḥ puṣṭapalābhavet"|| iti|
mārkaṇḍeya purāṇe alarkaḥ—
"bhagavan yoginaścaiva śrotumicchāmi tattvataḥ|
brahmavartmanyanusaran yena yogī na sīdati||"
dattātreyaḥ –
"saṅgadoṣodbhavaṃ duḥkhaṃ mamatvāsaktacetasām|
tasmāt saṅgaṃ prayatnena mumukṣuḥ saṃ tyajennaraḥ||
saṅgībhāvān sametyasyāḥ khyāterhāniḥ prajāyate|
nirmamatvaṃ sukhāyaiva vairāgyāddoṣadarśanam||
jñānādeva ca vairāgyaṃ jñānaṃ vairāgyapūrvakam|
tad gṛhaṃ yatra vasati stad bhojyaṃ yena jīvati||
yanmuktaye tadevoktaṃ jñānamajñānamanyathā|
upabhogena puṇyānāṃ apūṇyānāñca pārthiva !||
kartavyānāñca nityānāṃ na kāmya karaṇāttathā|
asañcayādapūrvasya kṣayāt pūrvārjitasya ca||
karmaṇā bandhamāpnoti śarīraṃ na punarnaraḥ|
mānāvamānau yāvetau prītyudvega karaunṛṇām|
tāveva viparītārthau yoginaḥ siddhikārakau|
mānāpamānau yavitau tāvevāhurviṣāmṛtau||
apamānāmṛtaṃ tatra mānastu viṣamaṃ viṣam|
akṣuḥ pūtaṃ nyaset pādaṃ vastrapūtaṃ pivejjalam|
P.91
satyapūtāṃ vaded bāṇīṃ buddhipūtaṃ vicitanyet|
ātithya āddhayajñeṣu devayātrotseveṣu ca||
mahājane ca siddhyarthaṃ na yacched yogavit kvacit|
yathaiva avamanyante janāḥ paribhavanti ca ||
tathodyuktaścaredyogī satāṃ dharmamadūṣayan|
sārabhūtamupāsītajñānaṃ yatkāryasārdhakam||
jñānānāṃ bahutā yeyaṃ yogavighnakarī hi sā|
idaṃ jñeyamidaṃ jñeyamiti yastrṛṣitaścaret||
api kalpasahastreṣu naiva jñānamavāpnuyāt|
vāgadaṇḍaḥ karmadaṇḍaśca manodaṇḍaścate trayaḥ||
yasyai te niyatā daṇḍā sa tridaṇḍī mahāyatiḥ|
sarvamātmamayaṃ yasya sadasajjagadīdṛśam||
guṇāguṇamayantasya kaḥ priyaḥ ko navā'priyaḥ|
viśuddhabuddhiḥ samaloṣṭrakāñcanaḥ,
samastabhūteṣu vasan samāhitaḥ|
sthānamparaṃ śākhatamavyayañca,
paraṃ hi gatvā na punaḥ prajāyate||
samāhitaḥ brahmaparo'pramodī,
śucistathaikāntarucirjitendriyaḥ|
samāpnuyād yogamimaṃ mahātmā, 
vimuktimāpnoti tataścayogataḥ||
nādhmātaḥ kṣudhitaḥ śrānto na ca vyākulacetasaḥ|
yuñjītayogaṃ rājendra yogī siddhyarthamādṛtaḥ||
P.92
rajasā tamaso vṛttiḥ sattvena rajasastathā|
saṃchādya vimale tattve sthito yuñjīta yogavid ||
etatsarvaṃ samākhyātaṃ tavālarka yathārthavid|
prāpsyate yena tadbrahma tatsaṃkṣepānnibodha me||
śaśāṅkaraśmisaṃyogāccandrakānto maṇiḥ payaḥ|
samutsṛjati nāyuktaḥ sopamā yoginaḥ smṛtā||
yarthākaraśmisaṃyogādarkakānto hutāśanam|
āviṣkaroti naikaḥ sannupamā sāpi yoginaḥ||
pipīlikākhunakulagṛhagodhāka(rka)piṅgalāḥ|
vasanti svāmivad gehe dhvaṃse tasya deṣāṃ na kiñcana|
veśmano yatra rājendra sopamā yogasiddhaye||
tadgṛhaṃ yatra vasatistadbhojyaṃ yena jīvati|
yena sampadyate svārthastatsvakaṃ mamatātra kā ?||
atyarpitāpi taiḥ kāryaṃ karoti karaṇairyathā|
tathā buddhyādibhiryogī pārakyaiḥ sādhayet param||
maddehikālpadehāpi mukhāgreṇātyaṇīyasā|
karoti mṛdbhāracayamupamā saiva yoginaḥ||
drvyapūrṇamupādāya pātramārohato bhuvaḥ|
tuṅgamārgaṃ vilokyoccairvijñānaṃ tena yoginaḥ||
ruruśāva-vidhānāgramālokya nīlakākṛtim|
saha tenaiva varddhantaṃ yogī siddhimavāpnuyāt||
jīvanāyālaṃ sarvasve nikhāte puruṣasya yā|
ceṣṭāṃ tāṃ tattvato jñātvā yoginaḥ kṛtakṛtyatā||" iti|
P.93
P.93
skandapurāṇe,--
"nātitṛptaḥ kṣudhārtona na vinmūtraprabādhitaḥ|
nādhvakhinno na cintārto yogaṃ yuñjīta yogavid||
yuktāhāravihāraśca yuktaśceṣṭaśca karmasu|
yuktasvapnāvaboghaśca yogī tattvaṃ prapadyate||"
bhagavadgītāyām, --
"nātyaśnatastu yogo'sti na caikāntamanaśnataḥ|
na cātisvaplaśīlasya jāgrato naiva cārjuna!||
yuktāhāra vihārasya yuktaceṣṭasya karmasu|
yuktasvapnāvabodhasya yogo bhavati duḥkhahā||
ātmaupabhyena sarvatra samaṃ paśyati yo'rjana|
sukhaṃ vā yadi vā duḥkhaṃ sa yogī paramo mataḥ||
brahmapurāṇe, --
"rajanyāḥ paścime yāme pūrve ca susamāhitaḥ|
pūrvāhṇe madhyame cāhni yuktāhāro jitendriyaḥ||
yogaṃ yuñjīteti sambandhaḥ|
yājñavalkyaḥ,--
"nājīrṇī narasodgārī na vinmūtraprabādhitaḥ|
na charddī nātisārī ca nātibhuktaḥ śramānvitaḥ||
na cātikarmaṇi rataścintāvyākulitendriyaḥ|
na yukto gurukārye ca na cātikṣutpipāsitaḥ|
nācareddehabādhāyāṃ daurmanasyādisambhave||" iti|
P.94
vāyupurāṇe, --
`navyādhyārtaḥ kadācane’tyādiniṣedhānantaramuktam|
"etān doṣān viniścitya pramādād yo yunukti vai||
tasya doṣāḥ prakūpyanti śarīre vighnakārakāḥ|
jaḍatvaṃ badhiratvañca mūkatvañcāpi jāyate||
andhatvaṃ smṛtilopaśca jvararogastathaiva ca|
ete doṣāḥ prakūpyanti hyajñānād yo yunakti vai||
tasmājjñānena śuddhena yogī yuñjan samāhitaḥ|
apramattaḥ sadā caiva na doṣān prāpnuyāt kvacit||
mahābhārate –
"mūtrotsarge pūrīṣe ca bhojane ca narādhipa!|
trikālaṃ nābhiyuñjīta yogaṃ yogī kadācana||
apramatto yathā dhanvī lakṣyaṃ hanti samāhitaḥ|
yuktaḥ samyak tathā yogī mokṣaṃ prāpnotyasaṃśayam||
snehapūrṇe yathā pātre mana ādāya niścalam|
puruṣo yadvadārohetsopānaṃ yuktamānasaḥ||
yuktastathāyamātmānaṃ yuktaḥ pārthivaniścalam|
karotyamalamātmānaṃ bhāskaropamadarśanam||
yathā ca nāvaṃ kaunteya ! karṇadhāraḥ samāhitaḥ|
mahārṇavagatāṃ śīghraṃ nayet pārthiva sattam"!
tadvadātmasamādhāne yukto yogena tattvavit|
durgamaṃ sthānamāpnoti hitvā dehamimannṛpa! "||
vyāsaḥ, --
"yogadoṣān samucchedyān pañcamān kavayo viduḥ|
P.95
kāmaṃ krodhaṃ ca lobhañca bhaya svaprañca pañcamam||
krodhaṃ śamena jayati kāmaṃ saṅkalpavarjanāt|
sattvasaṃsevanāddhīro nidrāmucchettumarhati||
dhṛtyā śiśnodaraṃ rakṣet pāṇipādañca cakṣuṣā|
cakṣuḥ śrotre ca manasāṃ manovācañca karmaṇā|
apramādādbhayaṃ jahyād dambhaṃ prājñopasevanāt|
evametān yogadoṣān japennityamatantritaḥ||
manasaścendriyāṇāñca kṛtvaikāgrāṃ samāhitaḥ|
pūrvarātrāparārddhe ca ghārayenmana ātmani||
kuryāt paricayaṃ yoge traikālye niyato muniḥ|
sanniyamyendriyagrāmaṃ kośabhāṇḍamalā iva||
ekāgraṃ cintayennityaṃ yogānnodvejayenmanaḥ|
yenopāyena śkyeta sanniyantuñcale manaḥ||
tañca yukto niṣeveta na caiva vicalettataḥ|
nābhiṣajjet pare vācā karmaṇā manasā'pi vā ||
upekṣako yatāhāro labdhālabdhe samobhavet|
yaścai na mabhinandeta yaścainamapavādayet|
samastayoścāpyubhayornnābhidhyāyecchubhāśubham|
na prahṛṣyeta lābheṣu nālābheṣu ca cintayet||
evaṃ svasthātmanaḥ sādhoḥ sarvatra samadarśinaḥ|
sanmāsānnityayuktasya śabdabrahmātivartate||
etasminnantaremārge viramenna ca mohitaḥ ||" iti|
deśakālābhāve yogo na siddhyatyeva, tasmāttāvevādau nirupyete|
P.96
liṅgapurāṇe, --
"sthānaṃ labdhvaiva kurvīta yogāṅgāni tu vaikramāt|
adeśakāle yogasya darśanaṃ na ca vidyate||"
mārkaṇḍeyapurāṇe, --
"sattvasyānupapattau ca deśakālavivarjite|
asatodarśanaṃ yoge tasmāttat parivarjayet||
nātiśīte nacoṣṇe vai dvandve nāgnyanilātmake|
kāleṣveteṣu yuñjīta na yogī dhyānapatparaḥ||
saśabde'gnijalādhyāse jīrṇagoṣṭhe śivālaye|
śuṣkaparṇakṣaye nadyāṃ śmaśāne sasarīsṛpe ||
sabhaye kūpatīre vā caitye balmīkasañcaye|
deśeṣveteṣu tattvajño yogābhyāsaṃ vivarjayet||"
skandapurāṇe,--
"na toyavahnisāmīpye na jīrṇāraṇya goṣṭhayoḥ|
na daṃśamaśakākīrṇe na caitye na ca catvare||
keśabhasmatuṣāṅgārakīkasādipradūṣite|
nābhyasyet pūtigandhādau na sthāne janasaṅkule||" iti|
liṅgapurāṇādiṣvapi, --
"nāśubhe darjanākrānte maśakādisamanvita" ityādi niṣedhānantagmuktam | (tatraiva)
doṣonetān anādṛtya mūḍhtvād yo yunakti vai|
pravakṣye tasya ye doṣā jāyante vighnakārakāḥ||
vādhiryaṃ jaḍa़tālopaḥ smṛtermūkatvamandhatā|
kāsaśca jāyate sadyastadvadajñānayoginaḥ||
P.97
tasmātsarvātmanā kāryā rakṣāyogavidā sadā|
dharmārthakāmamokṣāṇāṃ śarīraṃ sādhanaṃ yataḥ||" iti|
dattātreyaḥ, --
etaddeśakālaniṣedhānantaraṃ pramāde coktāneva jaḍa़tvādidoṣānabhidhāya taccikitsāmāha,--
"pramādād yogināṃ doṣā yadyete syuścikitsitam|
teṣāṃ nāśāya kartavyaṃ yoginā tannibodha me||
snigdhāṃ yavāgūmatyuṣṇāṃ bhukttā tatraiva dhārayet|
vātagulmapraśāntyarthamudāvarte tathavidhiḥ||
yavāgūścāpi pavanaṃ vātagranthiṃ prati kṣipet|
tadvatkampe mahāśailaṃ sthiraṃ manasi dhārayet||
vighātevacasovāci vādhirye śravaṇendriye|
tathaivāmlaphalaṃ dhyāyet tṛṣṇārto rasanendriye||
yasmin yasminrujoddeśe tasmiṃstadpakāriṇīm|
dhārayeddhāraṇāmuṣṇe śītāśīte vidāhinīm||
kilaṃ śirasi saṃsthāpya kāṣṭhaṃ kāṣṭhena tāḍa़yet|
luptasmṛteḥ smṛtiḥ sadyo yoginastena jāyate||
dyāvā pṛthivyau vāyvagnī vyāpināvabhidhārayet|
amānuṣāḥ sattvajātā vādhāścaitāścikitsitāḥ||
amānuṣaṃ sattvamantaryoginaṃ praviśad yadi|
vāyvagnīdhāraṇā caite dehasaṃsthaṃ vinirdahet||
evaṃ sarvātmanā kāryārakṣā yogavidā nṛpa|
dharmārthakāmamokṣāṇāṃ śarīraṃ sādhakaṃ yataḥ||"
atha vihitāni karthyante tatra kālaḥ pūrvamukto vakṣyate ca|
P.98
ādipurāṇe, --
"ekāntagiridurgeṣu devāyatanagahvare|
yogasyābhyasanaṃ kāryamiti tattvavido viduḥ||
yājñavalkyaḥ, --
"tapovanaṃ susaṃprāpya phalamūlodakānvitam|
tatra ramye śūcau deśe brahmaghoṣasamanvite|
svadharmanirataiḥ śāntairbrahmavidbhiḥ samāvṛte|
vāribhiśca susampanne puṣpairnānāvidhairyute||
phalamūlaiḥ susampanne sarvakāmaphalaprade|
devālaye vā nadyāṃ vā grāme vā nagare'pi vā||
suśobhanaṃ maṭhaṃ kuryāt sūkṣmadvārantu niṣprabham|
suliptaṃ gomayenaiva sudhayā vā pariplutam||
suśobhanaṃ maṭhaṃ kuryātsarvakāmaphalapradam"|
tasmin yogaṃ samabhyasedityuttareṇa sambandhaḥ|
yogatattvaprakāśe,--
"suśubhanaṃ maṭhaṃ kuryāt sūkṣmadvārantu niṣprabham|
suliptaṃ gīmayenaiva sūdhayā vā prayatnataḥ||
makṣikāmaśakairdaṃśairetaiśca varjitaṃ sadā|
dine dine ca sammṛṣṭaṃ sammārjanyā prayatnataḥ|
vāsitaṃ tu sugandhena dhūpitaṃ guggulādibhiḥ||"
haṭhapradīpikāyām,--
"surājye dhārmike deśe subhikṣe nirupadrave|
ekānte maṭhikāmadhye sthātavyaṃ haṭhayoginā||
P.99
alpadvāramandhragartaghaṭitaṃ nātyuccanīcāyitam|
samyaggomayasāndraliptamamalaṃ, niḥśeṣajantūṣitam||
vāhye maṇḍapakūpavedīracitaṃ, prākarasaṃveṣṭitam|
proktaṃ yogamaṭhasya lakṣaṇamidaṃ, siddhairhaṭhādhyāyibhiḥ||"
skānde,--
"sarvā bādhāvirahite sarvendriyasukhāvahe|
manaḥ prasādajanane sragdhūpāmodamodite||" ita|
nandipurāṇe,--
"mandiraṃ ramyavinyāsaṃ manojñaṃ gandhavāsitam|
dhūpāmodādi surabhikusumotkaramaṇḍitam||
munitīrtha-nadīvṛkṣa-padminī śailaśobhitam|
citrakarmanibaddhañca citrabhedavicitritam|
kuryād yogagṛhaṃ dhīmān suramyaṃ śubhavartmanā||"
tathā -- "dṛṣṭvā citragatāśchāntān munīnatimanaḥ śamam|
siddhān dṛṣṭvā citragatāśchāntān munīnatimanaḥ śamam|
siddhān dṛṣṭvā citragatān matirabhyudyame bhavet||
madhye yogagṛhasyātha likhet saṃsāramaṇḍalam|
śmaśānaṃ ca mahāghoraṃ narakāṃśca likhet kvacit||
tān dṛṣṭvā bhīṣaṇākārān saṃsāraṃ sāravarjitam|
anavasādo bhavati yogī siddhyabhilāṣukaḥ||
paśyaṃśca vyādhitān jantūn mṛtān tāṃśca vilakṣaṇāt|
evaṃvidhaśca śuddhātmā sthito yogagṛhe śubhe||"
"yogāṅgānyabhyase" diti samabandhaḥ, liṅgapurāṇādiṣvapyetadevayomaṭhalakṣaṇamuktam| āhāraniyamasya nāḍa़īśuddhidvārā yoge'tyantānukūlatvād āhārāśuddhesattvaśuddhasya yogābhyāsaniṣedhāt sadā 
P.100
cārābhyāsavidhānādavagacchāmaḥ| yadā yadā kṣudhā tadā tadā bhoktavyameveti| na caikakālabhojanasya jñānāṅgabhūtasyānurodhena sarvāpekṣayā pradhānabhūtajñānasya lopo yukta iti | `mukhyāmukhyayoḥ sampāte mukhyaṃ balava’diti nyāyāt| ataeva sarvaśrutyantare yogābhyāsināṃ bhojanāniyamaṃ śrūyate| tathāhi—
"ekakālaṃ dvikālaṃ vā caret paramahaṃsakaḥ|
yena kena prakāreṇa jñānābhyāsaparo yatiriti||"
atra yatipadamāśramatrayasyāpyupalakṣaṇam| athāvaśyaṃ jñātavyaṃ śarīre yoginā yattannirūpyate| na ca tajjñānaṃ vinā yogasiddhirasti| tathāca gorakṣaḥ –
"ṣaṭcakraṃ ṣoḍa़śādhāraṃ dvilakṣaṃ vyomapañcakam|
svadehe ye na jānanti kathaṃ siddhyanti yoginaḥ||"
iti ṣaṭcakraṃ ṣoḍa़śādhāraṃ vakṣyate| dvilakṣaṃ vāhyamabhyantarañca| bhūghrāṇādikaṃ mūlādhārādikañca, vyomapañcakantu—hṛt-kaṇṭha tālubhrūrandhrāntam, ekastambhaṃ sustambhe navagṛhaṃ manasedaṃ śarīraṃ dhriyate, tena stambharūpaṃ mana eva| sūlādhārasvādhiṣṭhānamaṇipurānāhataviśuddhānteṣu yathākramaṃ brahma-viṣṇu-rudreśvara-sadāśivādevatāḥ|
"kenopāyena śuddhāḥ syurnāḍa़yaḥ sarvadehinām|
utpattiṃ cāpi nāḍa़īnāṃ dhāraṇāñca yathāvidhi|
skandhastu kīdṛśaḥ proktaḥ kati tiṣṭhanti vāyavaḥ|
sthānāni caiva vāyūnāṃ karmāṇi ca pṛthak pṛthak||
vijñātavyāni yānyasmin dehe dehabhṛtāmbara|
vaktumarhasi tatsarvaṃ tvatto vettā na vidyate||
P.101
ityukto bhāryayā tatra samyak tad gatamānasaḥ|
gārgī tāñca samālokya tatsarvaṃ samabhāṣata||
śarīraṃ tāvadevedaṃ ṣaṇnavatyaṅgulātmakam|
vidvi tatsarvajantūnāṃ svāṅgulibhiriti priye||
śarīrādadhikaḥ prāṇo dvādaśāṅgulamānataḥ|
caturdaśāṅgulaṃ kecid vadanti munisattamāḥ||
dvādaśāṅgulameveti vadanti jñānino narāḥ|
prāṇo'pānaḥ samānaśca udāno vyāna eva ca||
nāgaḥ karmaśca kṛkaro devadatto dhanañjayaḥ|
ete nāḍa़īṣu sarvāsu caranti haṭhavāyavaḥ||
eteṣu vāyavaḥ pañcamukhyāḥ prāṇādayaḥ smṛtāḥ|
teṣu mukhyatamā vetau prāṇāpānau narottame|
prāṇa evaitayormukhyaḥ sarvaprāṇabhṛtāṃ sadā||" iti|
nāḍa़īśuddhirupariṣṭhād vakṣyate|
nakulīśayogaparāyaṇe, --
"kaurmo vāyuḥ prāṇanādikarmabhedād yathākramam|
prāṇāpāna samānādināmāntaramupāgataḥ||" iti|
amṛtasiddhau, --
"asaṃkhyā vāyavo dehe santi yadyapyudāhṛtaḥ|
tathāpi śreṣṭhakartārau prāṇā'pānau kṛtāvubhau||"
yājñavalkyaḥ, --
"āsyanāsikayormadhye hṛnmadhye nābhimadhyame|
prāṇālayamiti prāhuḥ pādāṅguṣṭhe ca kecana||
adhaścorddhvañca kuṇḍalyāḥ paritaḥ prāṇasaṃjñakaḥ|
P.102
tiṣṭhatyeteṣu caturṣu prakāśayati dīpavat||
apānanilayaṃ kecid gudmeḍhrorujānuṣu|
udare cekṣaṇe kaṭyāṃ jaṅghe nābhau vadanti hi||
vyānaḥ śrotrākṣimadhye ca kakudyāṃ gulprayorapi|
prāṇe gale sphijo deśe tiṣṭhatyatra na saṃśayaḥ||
udānaḥ sarvasandhisthaḥ pādyorhastayorapi |
samānaḥ sarvagātreṣu sarvavyāpī vyavasthitaḥ||
bhuktaṃ sarvarasaṃ rātre vyāpayan vahninā saha|
dvisaptatisahastreṣu nāḍīmārgeṣu sañcaret||
samānavāyurevaikaḥ svāgniṃrvyāpya vyavasthitaḥ|
arcibhiḥ saha sarvatra sāṅgopāṅge kalevare||
nāgādivāyavaḥ pañca tvagasthyādiṣu saṃsthitāḥ|
tundasthajalamannañca rasādi ca samīkṛtam||
tandamadhyagataḥ prāṇaḥ tāni kuryātpṛthak pṛthak|
uparyagnerjalaṃ sthāpyantvannādīni jalopari||
svayantvapānaṃ saṃprāpya tenaiva saha mārutaḥ|
pravāti jvalanaṃ tatra prāṇo madhyagataḥ punaḥ||
vāyunā ca tato vahnirapānena śanaiḥ śanaiḥ|
tadā jvalati viprendra sakale dehamadhyame||
jvālābhijvalanastatra prāṇena preritastataḥ|
jalamatyuṣṇamakarot koṣṭhamadhyagatantadā ||
P.103
annaṃ vyañjanasaṃyuktaṃ jalopari samarpitam|
tataḥ supakkamakarodvahniḥ santaptavāriṇā|
svedamūtrajale syātāṃ rūpaṃ vīryaṃ raso bhavet||
purīṣamannaṃ syād gārgi ! prāṇaḥ kuryātpṛthak pṛthak|
samānavāyunā sārddhaṃ rasaṃ sarvāsu nāḍa़īṣu||
vyomarandhraiśca navabhirvinmūtrādivisarjanam||
kurvanti vāyavaḥ sarve śarīreṣu nirantaram|
niḥśvāsocchāsakāsāśca prāṇakarmeti kīrtitam||
apānavāyoḥ karmaitadvinmūtrādivisarjanam|
hānopādānaceṣṭādivyānakarmāpi ceṣyate||
udānakarmatatproktaṃ dehesyānnayanādivat (yat)|
śoṣaṇādisamānasya śarīre karma kīrtitam||
udgārādiguṇo yastu nāgakarmeti coditam|
nimīlanādikūrmasya kṣutañca kṛkarasya ca||
devadattasaya viprendra ! jṛmbhaṇaṃ karmakīrtitam|
dhanañjayasya śobhādisarvakarmaprakīrtitam||"
tathā pañcānāmapi prāṇādīnāṃ..... na varṇabījāni nakulīśa- gaparāyaṇe, -- 
"prāṇo ghrāṇāgrahṛnnā bhipādāṅguṣṭhāntasaṃsthitaḥ|
nīla....kārarephādibījena viniyojitaḥ||
kṛṣṇo'pānaḥ kṛkaṭisthaḥ pṛṣṭhapṛṣṭhāntapārṣṇigaḥ|
sa tāraka nakārāntabījena viniyojitaḥ||
P.104
śakracāpanibho'nyāni tvagindriyaniketanaḥ|
tārakopeta....bījatejo virājitaḥ||
mūrddhvastho madhyatālvagra-pāṇi-pādatale sthitaḥ|
udāno hyaruṇacchāyastārakopāntakāntayuk||
śvetaḥ samāno hṛnnābhi sarvasandhiniketanaḥ|
praṇavākrāntalāntena vījenātyantamujjvalaḥ||
rephasyādiryakāraḥ sa cāsāvoṅkārasahito yakāraḥ prāṇasya bījamityarthaḥ| praṇavenākrāntaśvāsau lānto vakārasya soṅkārovakāraḥ samānasya bījamityarthaḥ| atra sarvatravāyūnāmarūpāṇāmiti nīlādivarṇaviśeṣo dhyānārthamabhihita iti draṣṭavyam|
yājñavalkyaḥ, --
"dehamadhye śikhisthānaṃ taptajāmbunadaprabham|
trikoṇantu manuṣyāṇāṃ caturastraṃ catuṣpadām||
maṇḍalantu pataṅgānāṃ satyametadvravīmite|
tanmadhye tu śikhātanvī sadā tiṣṭhati pāvakaḥ||
dehamadhyantu kutreti śrotumicchasi cecchṛṇu|
gudāttu dvyaṅgulādūrddhaḍamadho medrāttu dvyaṅgulāt||"
dehamadhyamitīritam|
"kandasthānaṃ manuṣyāṇāṃ dehamadhyānnavo'ṅgulam|
caturaṅgulavistāra māyāmañca tathāvidham||
aṇḍākṛtivadākāraṃ bhūṣitaṃ tattvagādibhiḥ|
catuṣpadāṃ tiraścāṃ ca dvijānāṃ tundamadhyame||
tanmadhye nābhirityuktaṃ nābhau cakrasamudbhavaḥ|
dvādaśārayuktaṃ tacca teva dehaḥ pratiṣṭhitaḥ||

P.105
cakre'smin bhramate jīvaḥ puṇyapāpa pracoditaḥ|
tantupañjaramadhyastho yathā bhramati lūtakaḥ||
jīvasya mūlacakre'smin adhaḥprāṇaścaratyasau|
prāṇārūḍha़o bhavejjīvaḥ sarvadeheṣu sarvadā||
tasyārddhvaṃ kuṇḍalīsthānaṃ nābhestiryagadhogatam|
aṣṭaprakṛtirūpā sā aṣṭadhā kuṇṭalīkṛtā||
yathāvadvāyusañcāraṃ jalānnādīni sarvaśaḥ|

kandamadhye sthitānāḍa़ī suṣumneti prakīrtitā|

nāḍa़īnāmapi sarvāsāṃ mukhyā gārgi caturddaśa|
iḍa़āca piṅgalā caiva suṣumnāṃca sarasvatī||
vāruṇī caiva puṣāca hastijihvā payasvinī|
viśvodarī kuhūścaiva śaṅkhinīca yaśasvinī||
alamvuṣāca gāndhārī mukhyāstvetā caturddaśa||
tāsāṃ mukhyatamāstistrastisṛṣvekottamā matā|
muktimārgeti sā proktā suṣumnā viśvadhāriṇī||
kandasya madhyame mārge! suṣumnā supratiṣṭhitā|
pṛṣṭhabaṃśasthitenāsthrā sahamūrdhvi vyavasthitā||
muktimārgeti sā proktā brahmarandhreti kortitā|

abyaktā saiva vijñeyā sūkṣmāca vaiṣṇavī smṛtā||
haṭapradīpikāyām :--
suṣumnā śūnyapadavī brahmarandhraṃ mahāpatham|
śmaśānī śāmbhavī brahmamārgaścetyeka vācakamiti"| atra suṣumnāyā muktimārgatvaṃ "tayorddhamāyannamṛtatvametī" ti vakṣyamāṇayā chāndogya śrutyā  mukti sādhanadvāradhāraṇādata eva kāśīvadiyamavimukta miti samākhyāyate| tathācoktam – avimuktatraividhyaṃ kāśīmīmāṃsāyāṃ -- "trīṇyādhyātmikādhidaivikādhibhautikānīti" sūtre, ādhyātmikāvimuktamiyaṃ nāḍa़īti skandapurāṇe'pi darśitam|
"sahovāca jāvālirāruṇosi riḍa़ā matā|
varuṇā piṅgasā naḍa़ī tadantastvavimuktaka miti| tathā| sā suṣumnā parā nāḍa़ītviyaṃ vārāṇasī tvasāviti|"
kūrmapūrāṇe'pyasyā eva tattatpradeśaviśeṣāvacchedena muktipradatvāt tat pradeśānāmavimuktatvaṃ samākhyātam|
"bhrūmadhye nābhimadhyeca hṛdaye'pica mūrdhvani|
tathā vimuktamāditye vāṃrāṇasyāṃ vyavakhyitam||
varaṇāyāṃ tathā cāsyāṃ madhye vārāṇasī purīti ca "|
atra cāvimuktaśabdaḥ tad vimuktaśabda paraḥ tathācāyamatrānvayaḥ| yathā vimukta śabdaḥ ādhidaivike dyāvāpṛthivyoḥ sandhirūpe āditeya vvasthitaḥ| yathā cādhibhautike vārāṇasīmadhyavartinivārāṇasī saṃjñake pure vyavasthitaḥ| tathādhyātmike bhrūmadhye hṛdaye mūrddhica cakraviśeṣāvacchinnaḥ suṣumnā pradeśe vyavasthita iti| yājñavalkyaḥ—
P.107
"iḍa़ā ca piṅgalācaiva tasyāḥ savyeca dakṣiṇe|

"meruśrṛṅge sthitaścandrodviraṣṭakalayānvitaḥ|

tathāca paṅkajaṃ śuklaṃ paramātma prakāśanam"||

"catvārica ṣaḍa़ādhārā daśa dvādaśa ṣoḍa़śa|
dveca padmāsanādīnāṃ yatra saṃsthā samīritā|
atra dviraṇḍobālākhya stathāphaṇipiṇākinā|
chagalaṇḍo mahākālaḥ siddhāstvete yathākramam||
P.115
ḍākinī nākinī caiva lākinī kākinī tathā|
śākinī hākinī caiva kramāddevyo bhavantica||
jñātvaivaṃ sarvacakrāṇi sarvasiddhi karāṇi vai|
gurūpadeśa vidhinā'bhidhānāni yathākramam"||
cakrabhedana prakāraḥ śrītripurāsārasamuccaye, -- 
"atha pūraka yoga yojitāntaḥ pṛṣadaśvonijamūlacakradeśe
vidhīta budhaḥ samāhitoyogagranthi vibhedane prayatnam ||
sakṛt samutsṛjya śanairyathāsvam,
saṅkocayet kamvuni bhārga ......marvā
budhastathādhāraniyojitātmā,
saṅkocayet kambuni mārga  succakaiḥ||
vidadhīta muhurmuhurgurukta kramato-
huṅkṛtimaṅkalamna pāṇiḥ|
dṛḍha़ruddhagudadhvajāntarālasthitaḥ,
tejomaya puṣkarāgra yoniḥ||
atha kambuni  mārutañjitātmā,
pratilomena śainaḥ praveśeyettam|
kamalāsana paṅkajasya mūlaṃ, svaguruproktavidhānato durāpam||
amunā vidhinā muhuḥ kṛtena, kramaśaḥ kambuni mārutastato'sau|
P.116
aja paṅkaja mūladeśamārga praṇayi granthi vibhedanaṃ karoti||
parihṛtya svagādhigastato'sau saraṇiñcandramaso nabhomaṇeḥ|
kamalaṃ kamalāsanasya paścāt, vidadhītīrddhamukhaṃpraviśya samyak||
pratibhānamathādbhutaṃ praviṣṭe, bhavanāntaḥ parameṣṭhinaḥ khageśe|
laghutā vapuṣo bhavatyudagrā, jaṭharāntardahanasyaḥ cāpi dīptiḥ||
taḍa़it koṭiprakhyaṃ svarucijitakālānalarucim,
sahasrādityāṃśu prakarasadṛśodyotakalitam|
bhramantaṃ yonyantaḥ sphuradaruṇa bandhūka kusuma--,
prabhaṃdhyāyet kāmajaṭhara śaśabhṛt koṭi śiśiram||
tasyorddhve'gniśikhā ciradyuti lacā puñja prabhā bhāsvarā,
sūkṣmā brahmapatharāntarāmvatā caitanya mātrā kalā|
ekībhūtamanantaraṃ saha tayā dhyāyed dhiyā niścalam||
ātmānaṃ ca vivātadīpa nibhayā haṃsa svarūpaṃ budhaḥ||
śaktiḥ kuṇḍalinīti yā nigaditā āīmasaṃjñā jagat,
jarā, nirmāṇe satatodyatā prilasat saudāminī sannibhama|
śaṅkhāvartanibhāṃ prasupta bhujagākārāṃ jagammohinīm||
tanmādhye paribhāvayed visalatā –tantūpameyākṛtim||
hūṅkāreṇa gurūpadiṣṭa vidhinā prītthāpya suptāntataḥ|
kṛtvā tāṃ kalayā tayā paramayā cidrūpayā saṅgatām|
māyāṃ kuṇḍalinīṃ samāhita manāstāmuccaret kauśikīm,
śaktiṃ brahma mahāpathena sahitāmādhārataḥ svātmanā||
bhitvā liṅgatraya mathabugho brahmanāḍyeha corddham,
gacchantīṃtāṃ kamalaja padāt prāpayedātma śaktim|
vyomānbhroja praṇayi paramānandakandam visarga|
P.117
sthānaṃ candropari parigaladraktapīyūṣa dhāram|
pītvā kalāmṛtarasaṃ punareva dibyam,
madhyaṃ viśed padmadalasya samāhitātmāḥ
pāyāt kulādakulameva punaśca mātrā—
yogena deśikavara pratipāditena||
sā śaktiḥ sakalaśarīra jīvabhūtā, 
prāṇo'sāviti gaditī'tatantrimukhaiyaḥ|
udghātaḥ satu paripaṭhyate munandraiḥ, 
prāṇontaḥ sṛpṛśati yadā kramaṇanītaḥ||
abhuṃ svādhārasthaṃ pratidivasamabhyāsanirataḥ,
karoti śraddhāvān satata mabadhūtākhilamalaḥ|
sa mukto duḥkhaughairapagata jarākleśa maraṇaḥ,
guṇagrāmopetaḥ sura iva ciraṃ jauvati bhuvi|
caturvidhā yā kathiteha sṛṣṭiḥ, pravartate sā sakalā'pi yasyām|
pralīyate cāpi jagat sasagraṃ, kālāgni rudrādiśivāntimetat||
seyaṃ mayoktā khalu yonimidrā, bandhustudevairapi durllabho'syāḥ|
anena bandhena na sādhyate yat, nāstyeva tat sādhukapuṅgavasya|
gurutalpa niṣevanañca pānaṃ, dvijahatyā madhunaḥ suvarṇacauryam|
amunā vilayaṃ prayānti samayak, sakṛdapyācarita bandhanena||
satu mantrivaraḥ sa eva yogī, guṇasiddheḥ padamāsthitaḥ sa eva|
amunā caratīha bandhanaṃ yaḥ, satataṃ svocita dharma karma niṣṭhaḥ||
amunā bhavatīha devatājaṃ, satatābhyāsa kṛtena bandhanena|
na jarā pitṛrāja vaijayantī, na ca mṛtyu pratipāditā'pibhītiḥ||
etasyaiva hi bandhanasya manujaḥ, prāpnoti śaśvat kṛtāt,
P.118
abhyāsādaṇimādi siddhimatulāṃ, savittimapyūrjitām|
yogaṃcāpratimaṃ jayañcarutaḥkālasya vā vañcanaṃ,
tadviṣṇoḥ paramaṃ padantusatataṃ paśyati yat surayaḥ" iti|
pārameśvaratantre, īśvara uvāca,--
"paścimābhimukhaṃ liṅgaṃ yonisthaṃ parikīrtitrama|
hṛdgranthi bindu saṃyuktaṃ svayambhūvāṇa bācakam|
itaraṃ cāntarālasthaṃ visargāghastathā param|
mahāpadma vanañcāntaryoga poṭhaṃprakīrtitam||
kadamva golakākāraṃ tatkāmaṃ bindurupiṇam|
brahmarandhrantu tatraiva suṣumnādhāra maṇḍale||
pṛthivyādīni randhrāṇi sūtrāṇāṃ vācakānitu|
pūrṇākhyaṃ candanantattvaṃ kodaṇḍadvaya madhyagam||
tadūrddha nādanāmani `muḍḍīyānaṃ’ prakortitam |
bandhantasyāḥ pravakṣyāmi mamvittiṃ labhate yayā||
ādau pūraka yogena hyādhāre yojayenmanaḥ|
gudamedrāntare yoni stāmākuñcya prabodhayet||
bhramadyoni gataṃ dhyātvā kāmaṃ bandhūka-sannibham|
sūryakoṭi pratīkāśaṃ candrakoṭi suśītalam||
tasyorddhetu śikhā sūkṣmā vicitrā pāvakīkalā|
tayā sahitamātmānaṃ ekībhūtaṃ vicintayet||
gacchantī brahmamārgeṇa liṅgatraya krameṇa tu|
amṛtantadvisargasthaṃ paramānanda lakṣaṇam||
P.119
drutaṃ raktaṃ satejāḍyaṃ dhārāpātaiḥ pravartitam|
pītvā kulāmṛtandivyaṃ punareva viśet kulam||
punarevākulaṃ gacchemmātrāyogenanānyathāṃ|
sāca prāṇaḥ samākhyātā asmiṃstantre mayodite||
sā mātrā jāyate bhūyaḥ uccāra-vaśavartinī|
udghātaḥ procyate so'pi prāṇastaṃ spṛśate sadā||
eva mabhyasyamānasya ahanyahani niścayam|
jarāmaraṇa duḥkhaughairmūcyate bhavabandhanāt||
caturvidhā tu yā mṛṣṭiyasyāṃ yonau pravartate|
punaḥ pralīyate yasyāṃ kālāgnyādi śivāntagā||
‘yoni mudrā’ parāhyeṣā bandhastasyāḥ prakīrtitaḥ|
tasyāstu bandhamātreṇa tannāstiyanna sādhayet||
cchinnā ruddhāstu ye mantrāḥ cālitā stambhitāśca ye|
dagdhā mattāḥ śikhāhīnāḥ malināstuḥ tiraskṛtāḥ||
meditā brahma saṃyuktāḥ śaptāḥ sammūrchitā hatāḥ|
mandā bālāstathā vṛddhāḥ prauḍha़ā yauvana gavvitāḥ||
ari pakṣesthitā ye ca nirbījā sattvavarjitāḥ|
tathā saṅketa hīnāśca khaṇḍaśaḥ śatadhā tathā||
vidhinānena saṃyuktāḥ prabhavantyacireṇa tu|
siddhi mokṣapradāḥ sarve guruṇā viniyojitāḥ||
yadyaduccarate mantrī mantrarūpaṃ śubhāśubham|
tattatsiddhayatyanāyāsādyonimudrānivandhanāt||
dīkṣayitvā vidhānena śrabhiṣicya sahasradhā|
"tato mantrādhikārārtha meṣāmudrā prakīrtitā|
P.120
brahmahatyā sahasrāṇi trailokyamapi ghātayet||
nāsau lipyati pāpena yonimudrā nibandhanāt"| iti||
atha cakrāṇi layayogopayogīni|
śivayoge, --
"mūlādhāre tridhāvartaṃ brahmacakraṃ bhagopamam|
tatkande'gninibhe dhyāyet adhaḥśaktimabhīṣṭadām||
svādhiṣṭhānaṃ tataścakraṃ tatracāturddalāmbujam||
tadevoḍaḍīyānaṃ dhyāyet paścimābhimukhaṃ śivam|
pañcāvartaṃ nābhicakraṃ sarpākārāntaḍa़innibhām|
tatra kuṇḍalinīndhyāye nmadhyaśaktiṃ susiddhidām||
adhomukhāña  patrāña  yuktaṃ hṛccakramiṣṭadam|
tammadhyakarṇikāndhyāyet suṣumnā madhya saṃsthitām||
pañcamaṃ kaṇṭhacakrañca tatrāṅgala caturthakam||
iḍa़āpiṅgalayordhyāyet suṣumnāṃ madhya saṃsthitām|
ṣaṣṭhañca ghaṇṭikāmūlaṃ liṅgantadrāja daṇḍakam||
dhyāyetta daśamadvāraṃ tatra śūnyaṃ susiddhaye|
bhrūcakraṃ saptamantvekaṃ nālakandaṃ svavāk pradam||
dhyāyeddīpaśikhākāraṃ tammadhye jñāna locanan|
brahmarandhre'ṣṭamañcakraṃ nirvāṇākhyaṃ susūkṣmakam|
tatra `jālandharaṃ’ dhyāyeddhūmraṃ mokṣapradantataḥ|
ākāśa bījaṃ navamaṃ prasiddham,
trikūṭakaṃ pūrṇagireśca pṛṣṭhe|
tatrorddhaśaktiṃ śubhadāṃ suśūnyām,
dhyāyettadaṣṭāra saroja madhye" ||
P.121
śāstrāntaretu,--%following = ŚP 4351-4360
"prathamaṃ mūlacakraṃsyātrirāvṛtti rbhagākṛtiḥ|
apāne malakandākhyaṃ kāmarūpantu tajjaguḥ||
tadeva bahni kuṇḍaṃ syādatrakuṇḍalinī sthitā|
tāñcīvarūpiṇīṃ dhyāye jjotiṣkāṃ muktihetave||
svādhiṣṭhānaṃ navamaṃsyāccakrantanmadhyagaṃ viduḥ|
paścimābhimukhaṃ liṅgaṃ prabālāṅkura sannibham||
tatroḍaḍīyāna pīṭhetu taṃ dhyātvā karṣayejjagat|
tṛtīyannābhicakraṃsyāttanmadhye bhujagī sthitā||
pañcāvartā madhyaśaktiścidrūpā vidyudākṛtiḥ|
tandhyātvā sarvasiddhīnāṃ bhājanaṃ jāyate budhaḥ|
caturthaṃ hṛdaye cakraṃ vijñeyantadadhomukham|
jyotīrūpañca tanmadhye haṃsaṃ dhyāyet prayatnataḥ||
tandhyāyato jagat sarvaṃ vaśyaṃ syānnātra saṃśayaḥ|
pañcamaṃ kaṇṭhacakraṃ syāt tatravāme iḍa़ā bhavet||
dakṣiṇe piṅgasā jñeyā suṣumnā madhyataḥ sthitā|
tatra dhyātvā śucirjyotiḥ siddhīnāṃ bhājanambhavet|
ṣaṣṭhañca tālūkā cakraṃ ghaṇṭikā sthānamucyate||
"daśamadvāramārgantada rājadaṇḍaṃ ca tajjaguḥ|
tatra śūnye layaṃ kṛtvā muktobhavati niñcitam||
bhrūcakraṃ saptamaṃ vidyād bindusthānañca tadviduḥ||
bhrūvormadhye vartulaṃ ca dhyātvā jyotiḥ pramucyate|
aṣṭamaṃ brahmarandhraṃ syāt paraṃ nirvāṇa sūcakam||
taṃ dhyātvā sūcikābhañca dhūmākāraṃ vimucyate|
tacca nālaṃ paraṃ jñeyaṃ mokṣadaṃ līna cetasām||
P.122
navamaṃ byoma cakraṃ syāt dalaiḥ ṣoḍa़śabhiryutam|
samvidrupāca tanmadhye śaktirūrddhvasthitā parā ||
tatra pūrṇagirau pīṭhe śaktiṃ dhyātvā vimucyate|
ūrddhva śakti nipātena adhaḥ śakte rnikuñcanāt||
madhyaśakti praboghena jāyate paramaṃ sukhamiti|
etat phalaṃ tripurāsāra samuccaye vistareṇektam|
"athādhārapaṅkeruhāṇyatra vakṣye,
suṣumnāntarasthāṇi saṃkṣepato'ham|
tadambhoja patrasthitāṃścāpi devān, tadambhojānvarṇaṃ tadādhāra devīṃ|| ajagranthi padmaṃ purā yanmayoktaṃ, tadādhāramādyaṃ vadantīhantaḥ| suvarṇābhavarṇaiścaturbhiḥ sametaṃ dalairyogigāmayaṃ mahāṣary bhūtam|| caturṣvaña  patreṣu devī niruddhāna, dajalāvirṇāṃnyaset kevasān vā| sa vindūna vindūn prabhāvān samiddhān sphurajjotiṣo hākinīdevatāca  mūlādhāre satataṃ dhyānayogāt, stambhakṣobhāvutplutirdārddurīva bhūmityāgaḥ khecaratvaṃ krameṇa, nṛṇāmete sadguṇāḥ sambhavanti|| kāntiprakarṣovapuṣo'pināda-vyakti pradīptirjaṭharānalasya| laghutvamaṅgasya nijendriyāṇāṃ, paṭutvamārogya madīnatā ca|| kṛtvā manoniścala mūlacakre madhye narāḥ kevala mādareṇa| bhūtaṃ bhavaccāpi bhaviṣyadarthaṃ, vadanti śāstrāṇyapi cāśrutāni|| vihāya sadyaḥ kamalāsanasya, vaktāmbujaṃ prīti taraṅgasaṅgat| tadīya vaktāmburuhodarāntaṃ, sarasvatī nṛtyati divyarūpiṇī|| adhigamya rasendra mantrasiddhiṃ apikālaṃ ca vijitya dūrnivāram| ajarāmaratāmavāpyacānte, paramānandapade mudā ramante||
P.123
ekībhūtārṇavāmbhaḥpluta, makhila jagat pūrvamāsīt apūrva,- jyotirmurti svadantarmahita – mahimabhūrliṅgarūpī vabhūva|| ṣaṭsiddhādhiṣṭhitātmā sakalasuranutaḥ ṣaḍabhirāsyairūpetaḥ, ṣaḍa़bhiḥkośaiśca devo, bhavabhayatimiraghvaṃsa haṃso maheśaḥ|| ādyaṃliṅgavapūrbhṛto bhagavato-devodviratḍo'bhavat, vāṇī pūrvamukhaṃ śivasyatu, mahākālastadūrddhānanam|
vaktaṃ dakṣiṇamāśuśukṣaṇirasau devaḥ piṇākī punaḥ,
pāśyātyaṃ chagālāṇaḍa eṣa bhagavān vāmāsyamasyā'bhavat||
svādhiṣṭhāna samāhvayaṃ rasadalairmūle dhvajasyāmbūjam,
bālāditya maṇḍaloparigataiḥ pṛṣṭhādi tarkākṣaraiḥ|
vindūdbhāsita mastakairbhagavato liṅgātmanaḥ śūlinaḥ,
sthānaṃ yogivareṇa gocaramidaṃ cāsyādhipā rākiṇī||
iha svādhiṣṭhāne nihita nijacatolayavaśāt,
mandānandaughastimita-hṛdayaḥ sādhakavaraḥ|
sametaṅgola'ṇḍaḥ kṣititalagato vā mṛgādṛśām,
smarasmerāpāṅgaṃ ramayati gaṇāṃ kāntikalitam|
iha vetti nidhāya mānasaṃ svaṃ vividhaṃ cāśruta śāstrajālamuccaiḥ|
avadhūta jarāmayaḥ samarthyaḥ suciraṃ jīvati vīta mṛtyu bhītiḥ||
vapuṣo'śuci bhājanasya śaśvat, paramāṃ śuddhi mihātanoti puṃsām|
śaradamvuja pelabasya dehe dṛḍha़ruddho ghanatāñca śītaraśmiḥ, calitaṃ sahasā mahārasendraṃ kamalālokanatoviyat sarojāt| iha ye vinidhārayanti santo nanu dhīrāḥpuruṣottamā staeva|| madadhauta kaṭo mahāgajendro vaśatā meti yathā śanairupāyāt| balavāniha bai mudhākaro'sau kramaruddhaḥ svavaśo bhavatyavaśyam|
P.124
etasminkuliśāña yoga niratādādau layo jāyate,
rāgādindriya daṇḍināṃ layavaśāt sambittirapūyarjitā|
antarnīla mahārasadravajuṣāṃ nṛṇāñca cetolayāt|
āvirbhāvamupatyamanda sahajānandacchalendūdayaḥ||
nābhisthaṃ maṇipūrakandaśadale nīlāñjanābhairyutam,
taḍaḍādyaiścāpidaśākṣarairddalagatairbindūblasanmastakaiḥ|
lākinyā samadhiṣṭhitañca satataṃ dhyānairnarāḥ kevalam|
vaśyākarṣaṇa nirviṣīkaraṇabhūrkṣobhādikaṃ kurvate||
sthāne'sminnihitānmanaḥ sukṛtinaḥ pātālasiddhiṃ parā,
khargasyāpratimasya sādhanamapi syādīpsitañca kṣitau|
rūpaṃ bhūmivisarjanaṃ parapure śaktaḥ praveṣṭujarā—
hāniścākhila duḥkha roga śamanaṃ kālasya vā vañcanam||
hṛdaye sarasendragopasandhyā ghanasindūra suvarṇa mañam|
yadanāhata  mityuśanti santo ravisaṃkhyai rūpaśobhitandalaiḥ||
krodhīśādyairbhairabairbhānusaṃrkhyairddaṇḍopetaṃ maṇḍicaṇḍavīryaiḥ|
tat kākinyādhiṣṭhitaṃ divyaśaktyā devyā haṃsenāpi devena śaśvata||
etasmin satataṃ niviṣṭamanasaḥ sthāne vimāne sthitāḥ,
kṣubhyantya dubhūtarūpakāntikalitā divyastriyo yoginaḥ|
jñānañcāpratimaṃ trikāla viṣayaṃ kṣobhaḥ purasya śrutiḥ,
dūrādeva ca darśanaṃciragatiḥ syādeyoginī melanam||
viśuddhākhaṃya kaṇṭhe kamalamatha dhūmrātiricibhi,
ryutaṃ śrīkaṇṭhadisvara parigataiḥ ṣoḍa़śadalaiḥ|
P.125
ihasthāneśākinyatulanija śaktirbhagavatī,
sthitā nityaṃ devī śaraṇagamanārti praśamanī|
abhyāsa yogādiha sanniviṣṭaḥ śāntasya śaśvannara puṅgavasya,
samarthatā syādanadhīta śāstra sadbhāvabhāve vasudhātale'smin||
sthāne'tra saṃsaktamanā manuṣya strikāladarśī vigātādhirogaḥ|
jitvā jarāmañjananīlakeśaḥ kṣitaucara jīvati vīta mṛtyuḥ|| 
iha sthāne cittaṃ satata mavadhāyāttapavano,
yadi kruddhoyogī calayati samastaṃ tribhuvanam|
na ca brahmāviṣṇurnuca hariharonāpidyumaṇi-- 
stadīyaṃ sāmarthyaṃ śamayitumalaṃ nāpigaṇeśaḥ|
ājñā nāma bhrūyugamadhyedalayugmo—
petaṃ padvaṃ śāradacandra mayūkhābham|
hākinyuktvā'trāpica devī dalayugme,
varṇāvantyau binduyotau saṃsmaraṇīyau|
dhyāna yoga niratasya jāyate pūrvajanma kṛtakarmaṇāṃ smṛtiḥ| 
kṣetra bindunilayeca  dūrato darśana śravaṇayoḥ samarthatā||
iha sannihita svacitta vṛttiḥ pratimāyāḥ pratijalpanaṃ karoti|
gamanañcanaraḥ purepareṣāṃ punarutthāpanamapyatho mṛtasya||
nirālamvaṃ mudrāṃnijagurumukhenaiva viditā,
mihasthānevaddhā sthiranihitadhīḥ sādhakavaraḥ|
P.126
sadābhyāsāt paśyatyamaranilayānta rakhilā-
muḍuśreṇīṃ viṣṇorapipada muḍūnāmadhipatim||
brahmāṇaṃ surabṛnda-vanditapadadvandvam mukundantathā|
śambhūndebanikāyanāyakamapi pretādhipamvāripam|
ahnābahnimapi prabhuñca pavanaṃ tārkṣyañca yakṣeśvaram,
gandharvānapi kinnarānapigurau siddhān prasiddhānapi||
raṅgottuṅgataraṅga santatajalāṃ svarṇokakablolinīm,
yogī paśyati śaśvadañcitaruciṃ tañcāpi hemācalam|
uttuṅga pīvarapayodharabhāranamrāṃ, kamrāṅgamaṅgajamaṅgajamandajāyām||
ullāsicilliyugalaṃ cakitainaśāva, lolekṣaṇaṃ visaramapsarasāntathaiva||
yakṣānrākṣasa puṅgavānapimūnīn nityāṃśca bhavyākṛtīn,
antaḥ kāntikalākapāla kalitān vidyādharān mātaraḥ|
samuttuṅgaiḥ, śrṛṅgaiḥ sthagitagagaṇābhoga kakubho-
lasannānāratnākalita kaṭakān bhūdhara varān||
kuraṅgaiḥ sāraṅgairapi ca ramaṇoyāmbanabhuvam,
tathāgurvīmurvīṃ sahita vividhakṣetra sahitām|
taraṅgairuttuṅkairahi makara mātaṅganikaraiḥ|
karālānāṃ bhīmairapi jaladharairbhudharavaraiḥ,
tathā pārāvārān namagasaritovāribharitān||
sahaṃsāliṃ pāstrīmapica sarasāṃ sārasajuṣām||
caranmarālaṃ sacakoramālam, karikarānantaravārinīlam|
kalāpijālaṃ sudharānukulaṃ pravāsīkālaṃ nava meghajālam||
etasmin paramādbhūtāvyayamahā, nandaika kandepare
sthānemānasamātmanaḥ sthiramatiḥ saṃyojayanmudrayā|
P.127
yogītammayatāmupaitiśanakai ścetonirālambayā,
paścānnaija mahāsukhalayavaśā dāvirbhavannāntaram||
līnecetasi tatra viśvanilaye vahnerivādau kaṇā,
dṛśyante tadanantarañca vilasaj jyotsnāsapatnaṃmahaḥ|
nityānanda iti śriyaḥpatiriti śrīvāsudevaḥ pumān||
ātmetyucyata ityacintyamahimā yo yoyate yogibhiḥ|
sthāne'smin parameśa eva bhagavān vyaktobhavatyavyayaḥ||
sākṣīrāhurirāntarā-ntaragataḥ sthānantadetat padam|
yatrāropya samīraṇaṃ sa kṛtinaḥ prāṇaprayānenarāḥ|
divyantat paramaṃ purāṇa puruṣaṃ vedānta vedyaṃ kavim,
viśvādyaṃ praviśanti satata mahānandaikandaṃ vibhum|
sahasrārapadmaṃ visargādadhastādadhovaktamārakta kiñjalkapuñjam| 
kuraṅge nahīnastriśrṛṅgastadantaḥ sphuradratnajāla sudhāṃśu samosti||
tadantargataṃ brahmarandhraṃ susūkṣmaṃ yadādhārabhūtaṃ suṣumnākhyanādvyāḥ| 
tadetat padaṃ divyamatyanta guhyam surairapyagamyaṃ sugopyaṃ prayatnāt"||
etat kailāsa sajñaṃ paramakulapadaṃ vindurūpī svarūpī,
yatrāste devadevo bhavabhaya timira-dhvaṃ sahaṃsomaheśaḥ|
bhūtānāmādi bhūto rasavisarasitāṃśusantataścottamāṅgaḥ
saudhīṃdhārāṃ kimuñcan abhimataphaladoyogināṃ yogagamyaḥ||
sthānasyāsya jñānamātreṇa puṃsām,
saṃsāre'smin sambhavonaivabhūyaḥ|
bhūtagrāmaṃ santatābhyāsa yogāt,
kartuṃ hartuṃ syāccaśaktiḥ samagrā||
P.128
sthāneparehaṃsa nivāsa bhūte
kailāsanāmnīha niviṣṭacetāḥ|
yogīgata vyādhiraghakṛtādhi-
vyādhiściraṃ jīvati mṛtyu-maktaḥ||
sthāne'smin kṣayavṛddhibhāvarahitā nityoditādhomukhī,
vālādityavibhā prabhāśaśabhṛtaḥ sāste kalāṣoḍa़śī|
vālāgrasya vikhaṇḍitasya śatadhā bhāgenavaikena yā,
skṣmratvādātmadṛśīti cāntaraṅgaliṭ pīyūṣadhārādharā||
etasyāḥ parataḥsthitā bhagavatī bhūtādi daivādhipā,
nirvāṇākhya kalārddhacandra kuṭilā sāṣoḍa़śāntargatā|
vālārkasya sahasradhā vidalitasyaikena bhāgena yā,
sūkṣmatvāt sadṛśo trilokajananī yā dvādaśārka prabhā ||
nirvāṇākhyakalāpadoparigatā nirvāṇaśaktiḥ parā,
koṭyādityasamaprabhātigahanā vālāgrabhāgasya yā|
koṭyaṃśena samā samasta jananī nityoditā nirmalā,
nityānandapadacchalenduvigaladdhārā nirālamvajā||
etasyāḥ parataḥ parātparataraṃ nirvāṇa śakteḥ padam,
śaivaṃ śāśvatamaprameyamamalaṃ nityoditaṃ niṣkriyam|
tadviṣṇoḥpadamityuśanti sudhiyaḥ kecitpadaṃ brahmaṇaḥ,
keciddhasapadaṃ nirañjanapadaṃ kecinnirālamvanam|
āropyāropyaśaktiṃ kamalaja nilayādātmanāsākamekam,
sthāneṣvājñāvasāneṣvavahitahṛdayaścintayitvā krameṇa|
nītvānādāvasānāṃ khagamakulamahāpadma kha (saptā) pnāntarasyām,
dhyāyeccaitanyarūpāmabhimataphalasaṃprāptaye śaktimādyām||
P.129
sākṣālākṣārasābhaṃ gṛganagatamahāsadma padmasthahaṃsām,
pītāṃ divyāmṛtaughaṃ punarapi ca viśenmadhyadeśaṃ kulasya|
cakre cakrakrameṇāmṛtarasavisaraistarpayeddevatāstāḥ,
hākinyādyāḥ samastāḥ kamalajapadagāṃ tarpayetkuṇḍalīntām"||
atha ṣaṭ karmāṇi|
haṭha pradīpikāyām –
"dhautī vastistathā netī trāṭakaṃ nīlīkantathā|
kapāla bhāti caitāni ṣaṭkarmāṇi pracakṣate||
karmaṣaṭkamidaṃgopyaṃ ghaṭaśodhanakārakam|
vicitraguṇasandhāyo pūjyate yogipuṅgaveḥ||
atha dhautau | 
caturaṅgula vistāraṃ sūkṣyaṃ vastraṃ śajegraset|
punaḥ pratyāharedetadabhyāsāddhāta karmāvad||
kāsa śvāsa plāhā kuṣṭhaṃ kapharogāśca  vadradhiḥ|
dhautīkarma prabhāvena prayāntyeva na saśayaḥ ||
atha vastā|
nābhidaghnajalepāyūnyasta nākallamāsanaḥ|
ādhārākuñcanaṃ karyāt syāmāsta karmavid||
gulmodaraṃcāpi vāta plākṣā pittakapradbhavāḥ| 
vastikarma prabhāvena vādhyante sakalāmayāḥ||
dhātvindriyāntaḥ karaṇa prasādam,
dadhyāccakāntiṃ dahanapradīptam|
aśeṣa doṣopacayaṃ nihanyāt,
abhyasyamānaṃ jalavasti karma||
P.130
"sūtraṃ vitasti susnigdhaṃ nāsānāle praveśayet|
mukhānnirgamayet sāhi netīsiddhairnigadyate||
kapālaśodhinīcaiva divyadṛṣṭipradāyinī|
yattūrddhaṃ jāta-rogaughān jarayatyā śunetīvit||
atha trāṭakam|
nirīkṣenniścala dṛśā sūkṣmalakṣyaṃ samāhitaḥ|
aśruprapātaparyastaṃmāryaiyastatrāṭakaṃ matam||
moṭanaṃ netrarogānāṃ tandrādīnāṃ kapāṭakam|
etacca trāṭakaṃ gopyaṃ yathāhāṭaka peṭakam ||
atha naulī|
amandāvartavegena tundaṃ savyāpasavyayoḥ|
natāso bhrāmaye deṣā nauliyogopracakṣate||
mandāgni sandīpana pācakāgni,
sandāyikānandakarī tathaiva|
aśeṣa doṣāmayaśoṣanīca,
haṭhakriyāmauliriyaṃ hi naulī ||
atha kapāla bhātī|
bhastreva lohakārāṇāṃ recapurau samaṃbhramau|
kapālabhātī vikhyātā kaphadoṣa viśoṣiṇī|
atha mudrā|
pavanayoga saṃgrahe, --
mahāmudrā mahāvandho mahāvedhaśca kheṃcarī|
`śakticolo’ `mūlavandha’ `uḍḍīyāṇāṃ’ tataḥ param|
`jālandharā’ bhidhovandho `viparīta kṛti’ statheti||
P.131
haṭha pradīpikāyāṃ, --
sa śailabana dhātrīṇāṃ yathādhārohināyakaḥ|
sarveṣāṃ yoga tantrāṇāṃ tathādhārohi kuṇḍalī||
"suptāguruprasādena bodhitā sukhadā bhavet|
tathā sarvāṇi padmāni bhidyante granthayo'pica||
prāṇasya śūnyapadavī tathā rājan yathāyateḥ|
tathācittaṃ nirālamvaṃ tathākālasya vañcanam||
tasmāt sarvaprayatnena pravodhayitu mīśvari! |
brahmadvāramukhesuptāṃ mudrābhyāsa parobhabet||
mahāmudrā mahāvandho mahāveghaśca khecarī|
`uḍḍīyāno’ `mūlavandhaḥ sahi `jālandharā’bhidhaḥ||
karaṇīviparītākhyā tathāvaiśakticālanam|
etaddhimudrānavakaṃ jarāmaraṇa-varjitam||
anādināthenoditaṃ aṣṭaiśvaryapradāyakam|
vallabhaṃ sarvasiddhīnāṃ durllabhaṃ marutāmapi||
gopanīyaṃ prayatnena yathā ratnakaraṇḍakam|
kasyacinnaivavaktavyaṃ kuyalastrīsurataṃ yathā|
atha mahāmudrā skande –
śoghanaṃ nāḍa़ījālasya ghaṭanaṃ candrasūryayoḥ|
rasānāṃ śoṣaṇaṃ samyaṅ mahāmudrābhidhīyate||
aiśvaraprokte—
mahāmudrāṃ pravakṣyāmi tantrai'smin mama vallabhām|
yāṃ prāpya siddhakāḥ siddhiṃ kapilādyāḥ purāgatāḥ||

P.132
gudaṃ saveyana saṃpīḍya pādamūlena sādaram|
gurupadeśatoyoniṃ gudamedrāntarālagām||
"savyaṃ prasāritaṃ pādaṃ dhṛtvā pāṇiyugena tu|
naradvārāṇi saṃyamaya civukaṃ hṛdayopari||
cittaṃ catuṣpathe datvaḥ prālabhed vāyudhāraṇām|
`mahāmudrā’ bhavateyaṣā savvatantreṣu gāpitā||
vāmaṅgena samabhyasya dakṣāṅgenābhyaset punaḥ|
prāṇāyāmaṃ samaṃ kṛtvā yogā niyata-mānasaḥ||
mudrāmenāntu saṃprāpya  guruvaktāt surgāpitām|
anena vidhināyāgo mandabhāgyo'pi siddhyati||
sarvāsāmevanaḍa़īnāṃ cālanaṃ bindudhāraṇam|
jāraṇaṃ tu kaṣāyasya pātakānāṃ vināśanam||
kuṇḍalītāpanaṃ vāyobrakṣparandhra-praveśanam|
sarvarogopaśamajaṃ jaṭharāgni-vivarddhanam||
vapuṣaḥ kānti kamavā jarāmṛtuḥ vināśanam|
vācchitārthabalaṃ sokhyamindri nāñcamāraṇam||
etānuyaktāni sarvānimārgārūḍha़sya yauginaḥ |
mudrākāmadughāhye pā sādhakānāṃ mayoditā ||
guptācāreṇa kartavyā na deyā yasya kasyacit|
dvayaṃ khalu `mahāsudrā’ mahāsiddhai ! praśasyate||
mahākleśāyato doṣā jīryante maraṇādayaḥ|
`mahāmudrā’ñca tenaiva yadānta vibudhottamā iti|
P.133
haṭhapradīpikāyām –
"yathā daṇḍāhataḥ sarpodaṇaḍākāraḥ prajāyate|
ṛjvībhūtā tathāśaktiḥ kuṇḍalī sahajābhavet||
pādamūlena vāmena yoniṃ saṃpīḍya dakṣiṇam|
pādaṃ pramāgtiṃ dhṛtvā karābhyāṃ pūrayet mukham||
kaṇṭhevandhaṃ samāropya dhārayed vāyu mūrddhataḥ|
tathā'sau maraṇāvasthāṃharate vipadāśrayām||
nahipathyamapathyaṃ vā rasā sarve'pi nīrasāḥ|
apimuktaṃ viṣaṃ ghoraṃpīyūṣamiva jīryati||
kṣata-kuṣṭha-mudāvarta-gulmaplīhā purogamāḥ|
tasyarogāḥ kṣayaṃyānti mahāmudrāñca yo'bhyaset|
atha mahāvandhaḥ|
īśvara prokte –
"yau'nyāṃ prasāritaḥ pādo vinyasyatamurarupari|
gudayonī samākuñcaya nītvācāpānamūrddhagam||
yojayitvā samānena kṛtvāśvāsa madhomukham|
vandhayedūrddhagatyarthamapāna prāṇayoḥ sudhīḥ||
kathito'yaṃ mahāvaddhaḥ siddhimārga pradāyakaḥ|
nāḍa़īgaladraptavahamūrddhaṃ nayati yoginaḥ||
ubhābhyāṃ sādhayet padmāmekaikaṃ suprayatnataḥ|
matamatra tu keṣāñcit kaṇṭhamandhaṃ vivarjayet||
rājadaṇḍadvayaṃ tatra jihvayoḥ stambhayediti|
bhavedabhyāsatovāyuḥ suṣumnā madhyasaṃgataḥ||
---------------------------
P.134
anena bapuṣaḥkānti dṛḍha़vaddho'sthi pañjaraḥ|
sampūrṇa hṛdayoyogī bhavanteyatanniyoginaḥ||
vandhenānena yogīndraḥ sādhyet sarbbamīpsitam|
apānaprāṇayoraikyaṃ kṛtvā tribhuvaneṣu vai"||
haṭha pradīpikāyām--,
pārṣṇiṃ vāmasya pādasya yonisthāne niyojayet|
vāmorūpari saṃsthāpya dakṣiṇaṃ caraṇaṃ tathā||
pūriyitvā mukhe vāyuṃ hṛdaye cibukaṃ tathā|
niṣpīḍya yonimākuñcaya manomadhye niyojayet||
recayecca śanaireva mahābandho'ya mucyate|
`ayaṃ khalu mahābandho’ mahāsiddhipradāyakaḥ||
kālapāśa mahābandhu vimocana vicakṣaṇaḥ|
ayaṃ tu sarvanāḍa़īnāṃ ūrddhaṃ gamana rodhakaḥ|
triveṇyā saṅgamaṃ dhatte  kedāraṃ prapayenmanaḥ|
atha mahāvedhaḥ –
"rūpalāvaṇya sampannā yathā sūtrī puruṣaṃ vinā|
mahāmudrāmahāvedhau niṣphalau vedhavarjitau||
mahābandhe sthitoyogī kṛtvā pūrakamekadhā|
vāyūnāṃ gatimāvṛtya nibhṛtaṃ kaṇṭhamudrayā||
mamahastayugo bhūtvā sphijau santāḍa़yecnaiḥ|
puṭadvayaṃ samākramya vāyuḥ sphūrati madhyagaḥ||
soma sūryāgni sandhānaṃ jāyate cāmṛtāyate|
mṛtāvasthā-samutpannaṃ tato mṛtyubhayaṃ kutaḥ|
P.135
mahāvedho'ya mabhyāsān mahāsiddhi pradāyakaḥ|
balīpalita vedhaghnaḥ sevyate sādhakottamaiḥ||
etattrayaṃ mahāguptaṃ jalā mṛtyu vināśanam|
vahnivṛddhikarañcaiva aṇimādi guṇa pradama||
aṣṭadhā kriyate caiva yāme yāme dine dine|
puṇya sambhāra sandhāyi pāpaughābhidūraṃ sadā|
samayak śikṣāvatāmeva svalpaṃ prathama sādhane ||
īśvara prokte|
mahābandhasthito yogī kukṣimāpūrya vāyunā|
sphijau saṃtāḍa़yed vidvān vedho'yaṃ kīrtitomayā||
vedhenānena saṃviddhaḥ vāyunā yogipuṅgavaḥ||
granthīn suṣumnā mārgeṇa brahmarandhraṃ bhinattyasau|
yaḥ karoti sadābhyāsaṃ mahāvedhaṃ sugopitam|
vāyusiddhirbhavettasya jarāmṛtyu vināśinī||
cakramadhyesthitā devāḥ kampante vāyutāḍa़nāt|
kuṇḍalyapi mahāmāyā kailāśāṃse vilīyate||
tasmād yogī prayatnena karoti tritayaṃ kramāt||
"etattrayaṃ prayatnena caturvāraṃ karoti yaḥ|
ṣaṇmāsābhyantare mṛtyuñjayatyeva na saṃśayaḥ||
etat trayasya māhātmaṃ siddho jānāti netaraḥ|
yajjñātvā siddhakāḥ sarve siddhyākhyāṃ lebhire parām||
gopanīyaṃ prayatnena sādhanaṃ siddhimicchatā|
anyathā naiva siddhiḥ syād mudrāṇamāmeṣa nirṇayaḥ"||
P.136
atha khecarī|
ya़oga vīje|
"vitastipramitaṃ dīrghaṃ vistāraṃ caturaṅgulama|
mṛdulaṃ dhavalaṃ proktaṃ veṣṭanādhāra lakṣaṇam||
haṭha pradīpikāyām –
"chedanaṃ cālanaṃ  dohe jihvāṃ savarddhayettāvat
yāvadiyaṃ bhrūmadhyaṃ spṛśati tadā khecarī siddhiḥ"|
kalāṃ  parāñmukhīṃ kṛtvā tripathe parivartayet||
sā bhavet khecarīmudrā vyomacakraṃ taducyate||
rasanā bhūrddhagāṃ kṛtvā kṣaṇārddhaṃ yadi tiṣṭhati|
viṣayaimucyate yogī vyādhi mṛtyu jarādibhiḥ||
khecaryā mudritaṃ yena vivaraṃ lambikorddhataḥ|
na tasya kṣarate binduḥ kāminyāliṅgitasya ca||
calitopi yadā binduḥ saṃprāptaśca hutāśanam|
vrajatyūrddhaṃ hi tacchaktyā niruddho yonimudrayā ||
saṃpātana vidhau binduḥ pāṇḍuro lohitastayā|
pāṇḍuraṃ śukramākhyātaṃ lohitākhyaṃ mahārajaḥ||
sindūraṃ sannibhaṃ vojaṃ ravisthāne sthitaṃ rajaḥ|
śaśisthāne bhaved bindu stayorakyaṃ sudurllabham|
binduḥ śivo rajaḥ śaktibindurindurajāraviḥ|| 
"yātibindoḥ sahaikatvaṃ bhaveddivya vapustadā|
śukraścandreṇa saṃyukto rajaḥ sūryeṇa savṛtam||
P.137
tayoḥ, samarasakatvaṃ yo jānāti sa yogavit|
cittāyattaṃ nṛṇāṃ śukraṃ śukrāyattaṃ ca jīvitam||
tasmāt citta ca śukraṃ ca rakṣaṇīyaṃ prayatnataḥ|
... ... viyati śikhare, merumūle tadasti|
tattvaṃ caitat pravadati sudhīḥ sammukhe nimnagānām|
candrāt sāraḥ sravati satataṃ tena mṛtyurnarāṇām||
tad badhnīyāt svakaraṇa mudrā nānyathā kāyasiddhiḥ|
indhanāni yathā vahnistailavartīva dīpakaḥ||
nityaṃ somakalāpūrṇaṃ dehī dehaṃ na muñcati|
rasanāṃ veśayedūrddhaṃ pivet tacchāvitaṃ jalam||
gomāṃsaṃ bhakṣayennityaṃ pivedamara vāruṇīm|
kulīnaṃ tamahaṃ manye netarān kula-ghātakān||
gośabdenoditā jihvā tat praveśo hi tāluni|
gomāṃsa bhakṣaṇaṃ tattu-mahāpātaka-nāśanam||
jihvā praveśa sambhūta vahninotpāditaḥ khalu|
candrāt stravati yaḥ sāraḥ sā syādamaravāruṇī||
ekaṃ sṛṣṭimayaṃ bījamekā mudrāca khecarī|
eko devo nirālamva ekāvasthā manonmanī||
nāsanaṃ siddhisadṛśaṃ na kumbhaḥ kevalopamaḥ|
na khecarī samā mudrā na nādaḥ sadṛśolayaḥ||
mūrddhaḥ ṣoḍa़śapatra padmagalitaṃ prāṇādavāptaṃ haṭhāt,
ūrddhāsyorasanāṃ niyamya vivare śaktiṃ parāṃ cintayan|
tat kallolakalājalañca vimalaṃ jihvākulaṃ yaḥ pivet|
nirdoṣa sa mṛṇāla komala tanuryogī ciraṃ jīvati||
P.138
"cumbantī yadi lambikostramaniśaṃ jihvā rasasyandinī,
sakṣārā kaṭukāmladugdha sadṛśaṃ madhvājyatulyaṃ yadā,
vyādhīnāṃ haraṇaṃ javāntaḥ karaṇaṃ śāstrāgamoccāraṇam,
tasya syādihasiddhiraṣṭaguṇitā siddhāṅganā karṣaṇam"||
skandapurāṇe –
"kapāla kuhare jihvā praviṣṭā viparītagā|
bhruvorantargatā dṛṣṭirmudrā bhavati `khecarī’||
cittaṃ carati khe yasmāt jihvā carati khe gatā|
tenaiṣā khecarī nāma mudrā siddhairniṣevitā||
yāvad baddhā nabhomudrā tāvadbindurnagacchati|
yāvad binduḥ sthirodehe tāvat kālabhayaṃ kutaḥ||
pīḍyate na sa rogeṇa na ca lipyeta karmṇā|
vādhyate na sakālena yo mudrāṃ vetti khecarīm||
rasanāṃ tāluvivare nidhāyorddha mukhāmṛtam||
pivennirjaratāṅgcchet ṣanmāsānnātraṃ saṃśayaḥ|
ūrddhajihvosthirobhutvā somapānaṃ karoti yaḥ||
māsārddheṃna na sande ho mṛtyu jayati yogavid|
sampīḍya rasanāgreṇa rājadanta vilaṃ mahat||
dhyātvāmṛtamayīṃ devīṃ ṣaṇmāsena kavirbhavet|
amṛtāpūrṇa dehasya yoginodvitri vatsarān||
ūrddhaṃ pravartate reto hyaṇamādiguṇodayam|
nityaṃ somakalāpūrṇaṃ śarīraṃ yasya yoginaḥ||
takṣakenāpidaṣṭasya viṣaṇḍaṃ na ca sarpati"|| iti|
P.139
dattātreyaḥ –
"antaḥ kapāla vivare jihvāṃcāvartya cārpayet|
bhrūmadhye dṛṣṭirapyeṣā mudrā bhavati "khecarī" ||
na rogo maraṇantasya na nidrā na kṣudhā tṛṣā|
na ca mūrchā bhavettasya yo mudrāṃvetti khecarīm"|| iti|
īśvara uvāca –
"lamvikorddhaṃ sthite garte rasanāṃ viparītagāṃm|
sayojayet prayatnena na sudhākūpe vicakṣaṇaḥ||
mudraiṣā khecarī proktā bhaktānāmanurodhataḥ|
nirantaraṃ kṛtābhyāsāt pīyūṣampratyahampivet||
tena vigraha middhiḥ syānmṛtyumāmātaṅga keśarī|
mudraiṣā khecarī yasya susthirāsyādatantritā||
śatabrahmagatenāpi kṣaṇārddhaṃ manyate hi saḥ"| iti||
atha mūlabandhaḥ|
"pādamūlena saṃpīḍya gudamārgaṃsuyantritam|
balādapānamākṛya kramādūrddhaṃ samabhyaset||
kathito'yaṃ mūlabandho jarāmaraṇa-nāśanaḥ|
apānaprāṇayoraikyaṃ kṣayomūtra pūrīṣayoḥ|
bandhenānena sutarāṃ yonimudrā prasiddhyati|
siddhāyāṃ yonimudrāyāṃ kinna siddhatibhūtale|
bandhasyāsya prasādena gagane vijitānilaḥ|
padmāsanāsthitoyogī bhuvamutsṛjya vartate||
P.140
sugepte nirjane deśe bandhamenaṃ samabhyaset|
saṃsāra sāgaraṃ tartuṃ yadīcched yogi puṅgava" | iti||
haṭha pradīpikāyām, --
"pārṣṇibhāgena mampoḍya yoni mākuñcayed gudam|
apānamūrddhamākṛṣya `mūlabandho’'yamucyate||
adhogatamapānañca tadūrddhaṃ kurute haṭhāt|
ākuñcanena tatprāhu "mūlabandha" na yoginaḥ "
yogavīje, --
"gudaṃ pārṣṇātu sampīḍya yoni mākuñcayedvalāt|
vāramvārantathācorddhaṃ samāyati samīraṇaḥ||
prāṇāpānau nāda vindu-mūlabandhena cākatām|
gatvā yogasya saṃsiddhiṃ gacchato nātra saṃśayaḥ||
apānecorddhage jāte samprāpte vahnimaṇḍale|
tathānala śikhā dīrghā varddhate vāyunāhatā||
tato jātau bahnyapānau prāṇamukta svarūpakau|
tenātyanta pradīptastujvalanodehajastathā||
tena kuṇaḍalinī suptā satataṃ sampravodhyate|
daṇḍāhatabhujaṅgīva niścita mṛjutāmvrajet||
vilamapraviṣṭeva tathā brahmanāḍyantare vrajet|
tasmānnityaṃ mūlabandhaḥ kartavyo yogipuṅgavaiḥ"||
uḍaḍīṇaṃ kurute yasmā daviśrāntaṃ mahākhagaḥ|
uḍaḍīyāṇanta devasyānmūlabandho'bhidhīyate||
P.141
udare paścimaṃ bhāgaṃ nābherurddhaṃ samācaret|
`uḍaḍīyāno’hyasau bandho mṛtyu-mātaṅga kheśarī||
uḍaḍīyānantu sahajaṃ guruṇā kathitaṃ yathā|
abhyaste tu tadantrastu vṛddho'pi taruṇāyate||
nābherurddhamadhovāpi tālaṅkuryāt prayatnataḥ|
ṣanmāsamabhyasenmṛtyuñjayatyeva na saṃśayaḥ||
iṭha pradīpikāyām, --
"sati vajrāsane pādau karābhyāṃ dhārayedṛḍha़m|
gulphadeśa samīpetu udarantat prapīḍa़yet||
paścimaṃ tālamudare kārayeccivukaṃ hṛdi|
śanaiḥ śanairyathā prāṇastundaśurddhi nigacchati|
sarveṣāmeva bandhānāṃ uttamohyuḍaḍīyānakaḥ|
uḍaḍīyānobandhaeṣa kaṇṭhaduḥkhavināśanaḥ|
nityaṃ yaḥ kurute yogī caturvāraṃ dine dine|
tena nābherviśuddhiḥ syādeyana siddhobhavemmarut||
ṣammāṣamabhyasan yogī mṛtyuñjayati tattvavit|
tasyodarāgnirjvalati rasavṛddhiśca jāyate||
anena sutarāṃ siddhiḥ vigrahasya prajāyate|
rogāṇāṃ saṃkṣayaścāpi yogino bhavati dhruvam||
gurorlavdhā  prayatnena sādhayettu vicakṣaṇaḥ|
nirjane susthite (ra) deśe bandhamparama durllabham" | iti|
P.142
atha jālandharaḥ|
"vadhvā  gala  śirājālaṃ hṛdaye civukatryaset| 
`bandhojālandharaḥ’ prokto devānāmapi durllabhaḥ||
nābhisvavahnirjantūnāṃ sahasra kamalacyutam|
pivet pīyūṣaṃ rasavat tadarthaṃ bandhayedimam||
bandhenānena pīyūṣaṃ svayaṃ pivati buddhimān|
amaratvañca samprāpya modate bhuvanatraye||
`jālandharobandha’eṣa siddhānāṃ siddhidāyakaḥ|
abhyāsaḥ kriyate nityaṃ yoginā siddhimicchatā"| iti|
yogavīje,--
"kaṇṭhamākuñcya hṛdaye sthāpayeddṛḍha़ micchayā|
`bandhojālandharā’khyo'ya mamṛtāvyayakārakaḥ" || iti|
haṭha pradīpikāyām,--
"badhnātīha śirājāla madhogāmi nabhojalam|
tatojālandharaḥ proktaḥ kaṇṭhaduḥkhīghanāśanaḥ||
jālandhare kṛtebandhe kaṇṭhasaṃkoca lakṣaṇe|
na pīyūṣaṃ patatyagre naca vāyuḥ prakupyati||
madhyacakramidaṃ jñeyaṃ ṣoḍa़śādhāra bandhanam|
bandhatrayamidaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ mahāsiddha niṣevitam||
sarveṣāṃ haṭhatantrāṇāṃ sādhanaṃ yoginoviduḥ"||
atha viparīta karaṇī|
haṭhayogeḥ, --
"nābhideśe sthitonityaṃ bhāskodahanātmakaḥ|
amṛtātmā sthito nityaṃ tālumadhye ca candramaḥ||
P.143
varṣatyadhomukhaścandro grasatyūrddhvamukhoraviḥ| 
karaṇantacca kartavyaṃ yena sā prāpyate sudhā|
viśuddhe paramecakre ruddhā somakalāmṛtam|
tanmārgeṇāmṛtaṃ yāti vañcayitvā mukhaṃ raveḥ||
amṛtaṃ kandare kṛtvā nāsāntaṃ śikhare kramāt|
svayamuccālito yāti vañcayitvā mukhaṃ raveḥ||
vaddhaṃ  somakalājalañca vimala kaṇṭhasthalādūrddhvato--
nāsāntaṃ śikhareṇa yāti gaganaṃ dvāraṃ tataḥ sarvataḥ|
ūrddhvasyobhuvi sannipatya nitarāmuttānapādaḥ pivet,
evaṃ yaḥ kurute jitendriyacayo naivāsti tasya kṣayaḥ"||
haṭha pradīpikāyām, --
"yat kiñcinchavate candrādamṛtaṃ divyarūpica|
tat sarvaṃ grasate sūryastena piṇḍaṃ vināśica||
tatrāsthikaraṇaṃ divyaṃ sūryasya  mukhavañcanam|
gurupadeśatojñeyaṃ na tu śāstrārtha koṭibhiḥ ||
ūrddhanābhiradhastālu rūrddha  bhānuradhaḥ śaśī|
karaṇī viparītākhyā sarvavyādhi vināśinī"||
dattātreyaḥ—
"ūrddhabhānuradhaścandra stadyathā śrṛṇu sāṅkṛte ! |
adhaḥśiraścorddhapādaḥ kṣaṇaṃ syāt prathame dine||
kṣaṇācca kiñcidadhika mamyasecca dine dine|
baliñca palitañcaiva ṣanmāsārddheṇa dṛśyate||
māsamātrantu yonitya mabhyaset satu kālajit|
nityamabhyāsayuktasya jaṭharāgni-vivarddhanam||
P.144
āhārobahula stasya sampādyaḥsāṅkṛte dhruvam|
alpāhāro yadi bhabedagnirddāhaṅkarotivai"||
atha śakticālanam|
īśvara prokte, --
"ādhāre kamale suptāṃ cālayet kuṇḍalīndṛḍha़ām|
apāna vāyunākṛṣya basādākuñcya buddhimān||
śakticālana mevaṃ hi pratyahaṃ yaḥ samabhyaset|
agnivṛddhirbhabettasya rogāṇāñcaḥ vināśanam||
vihāya nidrāṃ bhujagī svayamūrddhambhavet khalu|
tasmād abhyasanaṅkāryaṃ yoginā siddhimicchatā||
yaḥ karoti sadābhyāsaṃ śakticālanamuttamam|
yena vigrahasiddhiḥ syādaṇimādiguṇapradā||
gūrūpadeśa vidhinā tasya mṛtyubhayaṅkutaḥ|
muhūṃrtadvaya paryantaṃ vidhināḥ śakticālanam ||
vajrāsanagatonityaṃ māsārddhantu samabhyaset|
vāyunā jvalito vahniḥkuṇḍalīndahate'niśam||
santaptā sāgnināḍa़īca śaktistrailokya mohinī|
viśate vajratuṇḍena suṣumnā vadanāntare||
vāyunā vahninā sārddhaṃ brahmagranthiṃ vibhidya sāḥ|
viṣṇugranthiṃ tatobhitvā rudragranthaucaḥ tiṣṭhati||
tatastu kumbhakairgāḍha़ṃ pūrayitvā punaḥ punaḥ|
bhidyante granthayovaṃśe tapta līhaśalākayāḥ||
tathaiva pṛṣṭhavaṃśe syād granthibhedas tu vāyunā|
pipīlikā yathā lagnā kaṇḍus tatra pravartate||
P.145
suṣumnāyāṃ tathābhyāsāt satataṃ vāyunā bhavet|
rudragranthiṃ tato bhittvā tato yāti śivātmakam|| iti

haṭhayoge, --
"udghāṭayet kapāṭantu yathā kuñcikayā haṭhāt|
kuṇḍalinyā tathā yogī mokṣadvāraṃ vibhedayet||
yena dvāreṇa gantavyaṃ brahmasthānannirāmayam|
mukhenācchādya taddvāraṃ prasaptā parameśvarī||
kundorddhve kuṇḍalī śaktirbaddhvā mokṣāya yoginām|
bandhanāya ca mūḍha़ānāṃ yastāmvetti sa yogavit||
anbhodhiśailadvīpānāmādhāraḥ śeṣakuṇḍalī|
aśeṣayogatantrāṇāmādhāraḥ kuṇḍalī tathā||
kuṇḍalāṅgī kuṇḍalinī bhujagī śaktirīśvarī|
kuṭilārundhatī devī śabdāḥ paryāyavācakāḥ||
kuṇḍalī kuṭilākārā sarpaṃvat parikīrtitā|
sā śaktiścālitā yena samuktonātra saṃśayaḥ||
gaṅgā yamunayormadhye bālaraṇḍāntapasvinīm|
balātkāreṇa gṛhnīyāttadviṣṇoḥ paramampadam||
pucchaṃ pragṛhya bhujaṅgī suptāmudvodhayedabhi|
nidrāṃ vihāya sā ṛjvī ūrddhamuttiṣṭhate haṭhāt||
"paristhitā caiva phaṇāvatī sā,
prātaśca sāyaṃ praharārddhamātnam|
prasūrya sauryā paridhānamuktā,
pragṛhya niryāti vicālitā sā||
"P.146
vajrāsanasthito yogī cālayitvā tu kuṇḍalīm|
sūryādanantaraṃ bhastrā kunḍalīmāśu bodhayet||
bhānorākuñcanaṃ kuryāt kuṇḍalīñcālayettataḥ|
mṛtyuvaktragatasyāpi tasya mṛtyubhayaṅkutaḥ ? ||
nāsā dakṣiṇamārgavāhi pavano ghrāṇena dīrghī kṛtaḥ,
candrābhaḥ paripūritā mṛtatanoḥ prāgghaṇṭhikāyāstataḥ|
bhindan kāla viśālavahni vaśagān bhrūrandhra nāḍa़īgaṇān,
tatkāyaṅkurute punarnavataraṃ jīrṇadrumaskandavat||
kuṇḍalīñcālayitvā tu kuryād bhastrāmbiśeṣataḥ|
evamabhyāsato nityaṃ yaminaḥ śaṅkate yamaḥ||
tadābhyaset sūryabhedamujjayoñcāpi śītalām|
evamabhyāsa yuktasya yamastu yaminaḥ kutaḥ ?||
muhūrtadvaya paryantaṃ nirbharaṃ cālanāddhi vai|
brahmacaryatasyaiva nityaṃ hitamitāśinaḥ||
maṇaḍalādṛśyate siddhiḥ kuṇḍalābhyāsa yoginaḥ|
dvisaptatisahasrāṇāṃ nāḍ़īnāṃ malaśodhanam||
kutaḥ prakṣālanopāyaḥ kuṇḍalābhyāsato vinā"|
atra śakticālane padmāsanameva draṣṭavyam,
na vajrāsanamanubhavavirodhāt|
tathāca granthāntare, --
"kṛtvā saṃpucitau karau dṛḍha़taraṃ baddhāca padmāsanam,
gāḍha़ṃ vakṣasi saṃnidhāya civukaṃ dhyānañca taccetasi|
vāraṃ vāramapāna mūrddhamanilaṃ protsārayet pūritam,
prāṇaṃ muñcati bodhameṃti niyataṃ śaktiḥ prabodhodayāt"||
P.147
iti mudrā nava proktā ādināthena śambhunā|
ekaikaṃ tāmu yamināṃ mahāsiddhi-pradāyinī||
dattātreyaḥ –
"abhyāsabhedatobhedaḥ phalantu samameva hi"|
abhyāsakramaścoktaḥ pavanayogasaṃgrahe,--
"mahāmudrāṃ samabhyasya mahābandhaṃ tataḥ param|
mahavidhaṃ ca niyataṃ prakuryācchakti cālanam||
āsanaṃ sudṛḍha़ badhvā mūlabandhaṃ vidhāya ca|
uḍaḍīyānaṃ tathā bandhaṃ tatojālandharābhidham||"
abhyasediti samvandhaḥ| haṭha pradīpikāyām, --
"mūlasthānaṃ samākṛṣya uḍaḍīyāṇantu kārayet|
iḍa़āṃ ca piṅgalāṃ badhvā vāhayet paścime pathi||
anenaiva vidhānena sevayet pavanālayam|
tato na jāyate mṛtyurjarārogādikaṃ tathā"|| iti yogabīje –
"caturṇāmapi bhedānāṃ kumbhake samupasthite|
bandhatrayamidaṃ kāryaṃ bakṣyamāṇaṃ mayā sphuṭam||
"pūrakānteti kartavyo bandhojālandharābhidhaḥ|
kumbhakānte recakādau kartavyastūḍḍīyānikaḥ||
adhastvākuñcanenāśu kaṇṭhasaṅkocanena ca|
madhye paścimatānena syāt prāṇobrahāmaṇāḍa़īgaḥ||
apānamūrddhamusthāpya prāṇaṃ kaṇṭhādadhonayet|
yogī jarā vimuktaḥsan vayasā ṣoḍa़śobhavet"||
P.148
uḍaḍīyānasya sāhajikatvāt kumbhakadaśāyāṃ kartavyaḥ| atra kecid yadyapi kapilādisiddhānāmeva mahāmudrādayaḥ sammatāḥ na yājñavalkyādīnāṃ munīnāṃ tathāpi sādhāraṇyena boddhabyā ityāhuḥ| tattu tada granyārthāvabodha vidhuratayā| tathāpi nādotpatti prakaraṇe "apānamūrddhamākṛṣya praṇavena samāhitaḥ" ityatra mūlabandhaḥ kaṇṭhataḥ eboktaḥ taduktyāca tat samvandhināvuḍaḍīyāna jālandharāvapi prāptau| eka samaya vicchedena trayāṇāmanuṣṭhānasya pūrvaṃdarśitatvāt| kiñca—
"jihvāyāṃ vāyumānīya jihvāmūle nirodhayet|
yaḥ pivedamṛtaṃ viddhān sakalaṃ bhadramaśnate||"
ityatra khecarīṃ vinānupapatteḥ spaṣṭatvāt| tasmāt santi yājñavalkyādīnāmapi mudrā abhipretāḥ| pratipādanantu granthāntare prasiddhatyād viśeṣeṇa na kṛtamatretyavadhyeyam| idānīṃ pūrvokta vāhyābhyantarasādhana sampannasyādhāra mudrā cakrādi jñānavatī mumukṣościttaśuddhidvārā mokṣa sādhanībhūta tattvajñānopayogīji yogāṅgāni nirupyante| tathāca pātañjale -- "yogāṅgānuṣṭhānā dīpti rāvivekakhyāte"- raśuddhikṣaye cittasatvasya prakāśāvaraṇa kleśākhyāyā aśuddhernāśe sati yā bhavati jñānadīptiḥ sātvika pariṇāmo vibekakhyāterheturityarthaḥ| yogāṅgoddeśa paraṃ ca sūtram "yama niyamāsana prāṇāyāma pratyāhāra dhāraṇādhyāna samādhayo'ṣṭhāvaṅgāni" iti  yājñavalkyaḥ –
"vakṣyāmyaṅgāni te samyag yathāpūrbbaṃ mayāśrutam|
samāhitamanā gārgi ṛṣibhiḥ saha saṃśrṛṇu||
yamaśca niyamaścaiva āsanaṃ ca tathaiva ca|
prāṇāyāma stathā gārgi ! pratyāhāraśca dhāraṇā||
P.149
dhyānaṃ samādhi retāni yogāṅgāni varānane ! |
yamaśca niyamaścaiva daśadhā samprakīrtitaḥ|"
āsanānyuttamānyaṣṭau trayanteṣūttamottamam|
prāṇāyāyastridhā proktaḥ pratyāhāraśca pañcadhā||
dhāraṇā pañcadhā proktā dhyānaṃ ṣoḍha़ā prakīrtitam|
evaṃ teṣūttamaṃ proktaṃ samādhistveka rūpataḥ||
bahudhā kecidicchanti visatareṇa pṛthak śrṛṇu|
ahiṃsā satyamasteyaṃ brahmacaryaṃ dayārjavam||
kṣamā dhṛtirmitāhāraḥ śaucañceti yamā daśa |
karmaṇā manasā vācā sarvabhūteṣu sarvadā||
akleśajananaṃ proktaṃ ahiṃsā saiva yogibhiḥ|
satyaṃ bhūtahitaṃ proktaṃ yatpadārthābhibhāṣaṇam||
karmaṇā manasā vācā paradravyeṣu nispṛhā|
asteyamiti tatprokta mṛṣibhistattvadarśibhiḥ||
karmaṇā manasā vācā sarvāvasthāsu sarvadā|
sarvatra maithunatyāgo brahmacaryaṃ pracakṣate||
brahmacaryāśramāṇāṃ hi yatīnāṃ naiṣṭhikasya ca|
brahmacaryamidaṃ proktaṃ tathaivāraṇyavāsinām||
asāvṛtau svadāreṣu saṅgatiryā vidhānataḥ|
brahmacaryā tu saivauktā gṛhasthāśrama vāsinām||
rājñaścaiva gṛhasthasya brahmacaryaṃ prakīrtitam|
śuśrūṣāca gurornityaṃ brahmacaryamudāhṛtam||
dayo ca sarvabhūteṣu sarvatrānugrahaḥ smṛtaḥ|
vihiteṣu tadanyeṣu manovāk kāyakarmabhiḥ||
P.150
pravṛttau vā nivṛttauvā ekarūpatvamārjavam|
priyā priyeṣu sarveṣu samatvaṃ yaccharīriṇām||
kṣamā saiveti vidvadbhiḥ gaditā vedavādibhiḥ|
arthahānau ca bandhūnāṃ viyogeṣvapi sampadi||
tayoḥ prāptau ca sarvatra cittasya sthāpanaṃ dhṛtiḥ|
aṣṭau grāsā muneḥ proptāḥ ṣoḍa़śāraṇya vāninām||
dvātriṃśattu gṛhasthasya yatheṣṭaṃ brahmacāriṇām|
eṣāmayaṃ mitāhārohyanyeṣāmalpa bhojanam||
śaucañca dvividhaṃ proktaṃ vāhyamābhyantarantathā|
mṛjjalābhyāṃ smṛtaṃ vāhyaṃ manaḥ śuddhistathāntaram||
manaśuddhiśca vijñeyā dharmeṇādhyātma-vidyayā|
adhyātma vidyā dharmaśca pitrācāryeṇa vānagha! ||
tapaḥ santoṣa āstikyaṃ dānamīśvara pūjanam|
siddhānta śravaṇañcaiva hrīrmatiśca japovratam"||
ete tu niyamāḥ proktā stāṃśca sarvān pṛthak śrṛṇu|
vidhinoktena mārgeṇa kṛcchacāndrāyaṇādibhiḥ||
śarīra śoṣaṇaṃ procu stāpastāpasa uttamam|
idantu yogābhyāsāt prāgeva draṣṭavyam||
abhyāsakāle kāyakleśopavāsādīnāṃ niṣedhāt|
bhagavadgītoktaṃ maunadilakṣaṇaṃ yujyate||
tathāhi—
"devadvija guruprājñaṃ pūjanaṃ śīcamārjavam"|
yājñavalkyaḥ –
"yaddṛṣṭvā lābhato nityamalaṃ puṃso bhavediti|
matintāmṛṣayaḥ procuḥ santoṣaṃ sukha lakṣaṇam"||

dharmādharmeṣu viśvāso ya stadāstikyamucyate|
nyāyājjitaṃ dhanaṃcānna manyadvā yat pradīyate||

yathāśaktya()rcanaṃ bhktyā hyetadīśvara-pūjanam|

siddhānta śravaṇaṃ proktaṃ vedānta śravaṇaṃ budhaiḥ||
dvijavat kṣatriyasyāpi siddhānta śravaṇaṃ bughaiḥ||
viśāñca kecidicchanti śīlavṛttavatāṃ satām|
śūdrāṇāṃ kṣatriyāṇāñca svadharmasya tapasvinām|
siddhānta śravaṇaṃ proktaṃ vedānta śravaṇaṃ budhaiḥ||
veda laukikamārgeṣu kutsitaṃ karma yadbhavet|

guruṇācopadiṣṭo'pi bedavāhya vivarjitaḥ |

P.152
"asteyaṃ brahmacaryañca tyāgo lobhastathavaca|

pātañcale'pi –
"ahiṃsā satyamasteyabrahmacaryaparigrahā yamāḥ"
śauca santoṣa tapaḥ svādhyāyeśvara praṇidhānāni niyamāḥ"| iti garuḍa़ purāṇetu—

acāpalyaṃ dhṛtiḥ satyaṃ jñānañcaiva yamaṃ viduḥ||
ānṛśaṃsyaṃ ghātakatvābhāvaḥ|

śamaḥkṣamā damaḥ sṛṣṭirityaṣṭau niyamāḥsmṛtāḥ"||
athāsanam|
tatra patañjaliḥ –
"sthirasukhamāsanaṃ"  āsyate'nenetyāsanaṃ padmādi | tad yadā niṣkampaṃ sukhamanudvejanīyañca bhavati tadā yogāṅgatāṃ bhajate| tadā tasyaiva sukhāpattyarthaṃ sūtram| "prayatna śaithilyānantasamāpattibhyāmiti"|  tatrāntyeti bhāva pratyayāntapāṭha iti bhojadevaḥ | prayatna śaithilyainanta samāpattibhyāṃca sthiraṃ sukhaṃ ca bhavatīti samvandhaḥ| yadāsanaṃ badhnāti tadā prayatnaśaithilyepyakleśa iva niṣpadyate, yadā vākāśagata anante cetasaḥ samāpattiḥ kriyate| avadhānena tādātmyamāpadyate tadā dehāṅkārābhāvānnāsanaṃ duḥkha janakaṃ bhavati|
P.153
asmiṃścāsanajaye sati samādhyantarāyabhūtāni prabhavantyaṅgamejayatvādayaḥ| "ananta samāpattibhyāmiti bhāva pratyayarahitaḥ sūtrapāṭha iti bhāṣya sampradāyaḥ| atra anante nāganāyake sthiratarekṣaṇaḥ sahastra vidhṛta viśvambharā maṇḍale samāpannaṃ cittamāsanaṃ nirvartayatīti vyākhyānamiti vācaspatimiśrāḥ |
āsana sāmānya lakṣaṇaṃ tu karacaraṇa saṃsthāna viśeṣa āsanamiti| viśeṣa lakṣaṇaṃ tuṃdharmaputrikāyām,--
"sthātavyau bhūtale pādā vāsanasthena yoginā|
dakṣiṇaṃ vāmabhāge tu vāmapādantu dakṣiṇe||
anyo'nyābhimukhaṃ kṛtvā mṛgaśīrṣakaraḥ svayam|
yoga saṃsiddhi hetostu `mṛgasvastika’ mucyate||
talapādena vāmena jānunā dakṣiṇena ca|
sthitvāhasta puṭaṃ kārya `marddhacandrāsanaṃ’ viduḥ||
bhavi sthitvā tu jānubhyāṃ tāvanmātreṇa yoginā|
hṛdi hasta puṭaṃ kāryaṃ jñeya `mañjanikā’ sanam|
"sukhaṃ pratāritau pādau suśrliṣṭau bhūmi saṃsthitau|
upaviśyārddhakāyantu `daṇḍāsana’miti smṛtam"||
upaviśyārddhakāyantu `daṇḍāsana’miti smṛtam"||
"āsanasthena pādau dvau nyasyatau bhūtale samau|
jānudvaye hastatalaṃ kṛtvā `pīṭhāsanaṃ’ bhavet || iti"
mataṅgaḥ –
"paryaṅka yoga paṭṭena badhnīyāt pṛṣṭha yogataḥ|
ākuñcya jānunī sam.k pādaṃ kṛtvā tu dakṣiṇam||

`paryaṅka’miti vikhyāta māsanaṃ yogasiddhidam||"

āgneyapurāṇe, --
"pādau dvau dviguṇī kṛtya tiryagūrddhaṃ yathākramam|

hastau jānūpari sthāpya svāṅgulīḥ saṃprasārya ca |

athaikasmin eva dṛḍha़ baddhaḥ siddhāsane sadā|
