bimbamadhyastham ālokya netrābhyāṃ manasā saha||
iḍa़yā pūrayed vāyuṃ vāhyaṃ dvādaśa mrātrakam|(+)
tato'gniṃ pūrvavad dhayāyet sphurajjvālāvalīyutam||
rephaṃ ca bindusaṃyuktam agnimaṇḍalasaṃsthitam|
dhyāyan virecayet paścān mandaṃ piṅgalayā punaḥ||
tataḥ piṅgalayā(+) pūryya prāṇaṃ dakṣiṇataḥ sudhīḥ|
tadidaṃ recayeddhīmān iḍa़yā tu śanaiḥ śanai||
tricaturvatsaṃravāpi tnicaturmāsa mevavā|
ṣaṭkṛtva ācaredevaṃ triṣukāleṣu yatnataḥ||
nāḍa़īśuddhi mavāpnoti pṛthak cihnopalakṣitām||
śarīralaghutā dīptirjaṭharāgni-vivarddhanam||
nādābhivyaktirityetat cihnaṃ tat siddhisūcakam|
yāvadetāni saṃpaśyet tāvadevaṃ samācaret||"
"vidhyuktakarmma saṃyukta" iti yathāyogyaṃ varṇāśrama vihita, karmmānuṣṭhānayuktaḥ "yamādī"
tyādiśabdena niyamāsanayorgrahaṇam, teṣā yamaniyamāsanādīnāṃ yeguṇāste tatsiddhicihnavṛttāstasyātiśayā vairatyāgādi svāṅgajugupsādi dvandvānabhighātādayaḥ| taiḥ saṃyukta ādyaḥ tapovanaṃ tatogatvādirnāyaṃnāḍa़īśuddhau deśa niyamabidhirvākyasya klamaḥ (+) paratvāt| ato'yaṃ prāṇāyāmādi yogāṅgapañcāṅgatayā prāptānā yogadeśānāṃ ekadeśasyānuvādaḥ `kuśān samāstoryyāthavājim (+)’ mityatrāthaveti śabdona vikalpaparaḥ| kintu anukta samuccayārtho'vyayānāmanekārthāt|
tathācāyamarthaḥ, --
mṛdukuśoparicarmmaḥ (+) vastramapi datvetyarthaḥ|
p.163
tathāca bhagavadgītāyām, --
"nātyucchitaṃ nātinīcaṃ cailājinakuśottara" miti kuśottaramityatra `pāṭhakramāt arthakramovalīyāniti’(+) nyāyāt pāṭhakramo pūrvaparītakrama(+) iti śaṅkarācāryya varaṇairapyabhidhānāt kuśopari byāghrasya (+) carmma tadupari kobhalaṃvastramitivyākhyeyam| cailājinayoḥ kuśottare'dhastādyasmin (+) āsane taccailājina kuśottaramitiṃ vyākhyāyāṃ (+) tu na kvāpi anupapattiḥ|
yogasari, --
kuśacailājināstīrṇa iti|
nāradīya puramāṇe, --
"kuśaiścamṛdubhicchanne pavitreṇāya carmmaṇā|
tatnopaviṣṭaḥ satataṃ yogāṅgāni samabhyaset|| iti|
yājñavalkyaḥ –
mṛdvāsanopari kuśān-samāstīryya tato'jinamiti|
anyetu, --
athaveti śabdaḥ kuśājinayo rvikalpapara ityāhuḥ|
tadayuktaṃ| sarvvalokagurorbhagavadvāsudevasya vākyārthānusāraṇevānyavākyārthasyocitatvāt|
kiñca vaśiṣṭhagītāyāṃ vastrasyānuktatvāt kuśājinayo virkalpāśaye kevalakuśanirmmite kevalājina nirmmite vāsane yogābhyāsāsanbhavasyānubhava-siddhatvāt|
nanu "āsanantūla sampūrṇāṃ sitavastrāvaguṇṭhitam|
kṛṣṇājinottarīyañca vinyastaṃ mṛdukarmmaṇi||
ityagastya gītāsu,
"dheryyasampannaḥ satataṃ vastrañcājinamevavā||
nātyucchitaṃ nāti nīcamiti" dattātreyavākyeca
p.164
kuśarāhityasyāpi darśanādyukta eva vikalapa iti cet| na tatrāpi sarvvaśākhādhikaraṇa nyāyena trivṛtśrūtau bhūtadvayopasaṃhāravadvā kuśopa saṃhārasyāvaśyakatvāt| tathāca "sthirasukhamāsana" mityapi yuktaṃ bhavati| atra tūla sampūrṇa sitavastrāvaguṇṭhita māsanaṃ yativyatirikta viṣayaṃ jñeyam| tasya dharmmaśāstre tūlasitavastrayorniṣedhāt| tathāhi—
"vyākhyā śiṣye divāsvāpo maṭha-pustaka-saṃgraham|
tūlaṃ tūlapaṭīcaiva yatīnāṃ patanāya ṣaṭ"||
etena yatrāsanādīni ṣaḍa़ṅgānyevoktāni tatrāpi yamaniyamayorupasaṃhārodarśitaḥ| tayoḥ sakala vrata sādhāraṇatvāt keṣucideva grantheṣu na viśeṣeṇa pratipādanamityavadheyam| ayañcāsanavidhirmanāḍa़īśuddhimātra paraḥ, kintu sāṅgayogābhyāsārthopi draṣṭavyaḥ| "attro(+)paviśya yogān samabhyase" ditināradīyavacanāt| nāsāgre śaśabhṛdvimbamiti amṛtaṃ sravatīti draṣṭavyam|
saptamasya śa, ṣa, sahetyādi vargasya caturthaṃ harati vījam, pūrayed vāyumiti amṛta miśritamiti draṣṭavyam| etena nāḍa़īśuddhisādhanaṃ pūrakodarśitaḥ| atra kecittato'gni mityādināpiṅgalayā punarityantena recakodarśitaḥ| pūrvvabadityanena nāsāgraityasyāpi (+) parāmarśaḥ| tathāca candrāvagnidhyāna sahitau pūrakarecakāvevātra nāḍa़īśuddhi sādhanatvenopadiṣṭau na tu kumbhako'pi| ataeva
"āpūryyarecayet paścādvartma nā kṛṣṇavartmaṇaḥ" iti|
yogasāre—
"sokāreṇa dvitīyena madhyagenopaśobhitam|
tripañca vārān vā pūryya marutaṃ candravartmanā||
tato virecayesādhā (+) leśato jātayedasa(+) " iti|
p.165
nakulīśa yoga parāyaṇe ca, --
nāḍa़īśuddhi sādhanatvena pūraka recaka mātraṃ śruta mityāhuḥ| tatra tathāsati pūrakānantaryyasya tato'gnimiti tataḥ śabdenaiva lābhāt paścāt piṅgalayā puna rityatra paścāt padaṃ punaruktaṃ syāt| dhyāyan virecayeditiliṅgaiva dhyāna-viśiṣṭa recana vidhinā dhyāyediti vidhi śravaṇamatyanta manarthakaṃ syāt| tasmādebaṃ vyākhyeyam| sarvvapada pradhānabhūta vidhi pratyayasyānyathānetu maśakyaṃ tyāt, dhyāyediti liṅgāddhvānaṃ saviṣayā bhāvanā vidhīyate| tatra dhyānaṃ na cittasantatimātraṃ kintukumbhakasahitaṃ pūrvvottara granthaikavākyatā valāt| tathāhi na pūrake idaṃ dhyānaṃ sambhavati pūraye dityanenaḥ (+) pūraka samāpti ruktā sarvveṣā mākhyātānāṃ samāptiḥ śabdārtha iti nyāyāt|| tathāḥ tato'gniṃ dhyāyediti anena pūraka sasāptyanantara meva dhyānaṃ (+) vihitam| nāpi recaka idaṃ sambhavati dhyāyet paścāt virecayediti (+) pūrvvanyāyena dhyāna samāptyanantara meva recaka vidhānāt| tasmād recakāt pūrvvaṃ (+) pūrakāccānantaramiti pūrvvottara parāmarśa(+) vākyāṃ pramāṇa valena (+) dhyāyedityanena kumbhakākhya nirodhasahita dhyānameva phala sādhanaṃ (+) vidhīyatena (+)| ityeva vaśiṣṭhavākyārthaḥ| ataḥ kumbhaka rahitayo reva recakapūrakayornāḍa़īśuddhi sāmarthyamastītiyuktiyuktam | (+) kālikāpurāṇe nāḍa़īśuddhi prakaraṇe –
"saumye nākarṣayed(+) vāyuṃ nābhāvutkṛṣyadhārayet|
vidhāryya śaktito bhūya āgneye (+) kiñciducchaset||
evaṃ saṃśodhayet vāyuṃ pādāṃṅguṣṭhidaṣu sthitam|
sadā sarvvagatād vāyornāḍa़ī śodhana miṣyate||
iti| tathānye ṣvapi | vahugrandheṣu nāḍa़īśuddhiḥ prakaraṇe spaṣṭa eva kumbhakasahita(+) dhyāna mevātra(+) vidhīyata ityevayuktam|na kṣaitraṃ(+) vyākhyāne
p.166
recakāṅgabhūta-devatādi dhyānāllābhastato'gnimityādestu pūrvvavad dhyāyedityanena janitāhuyasya nirākāṅkṣatvāditivācyam| dhyāyan virecayediti sākāṅkṣapadānurodhena agneḥ suṣumnādhiṣṭhātṛtvasya piṅgalāyāśca sūryya daivatatvasya sarvvayogaśāstreṣu prasiddhatvāt sūryyapada matrādhyāharaṇīyam| tataścāgnirupaṃ (+) lohitaṃ sūryyaṃ dhyāyan virecayediti vākyārthaḥ sampadyate| ata eva sūryyasyāgnirūpadhyāna vivakṣayaiva vartma ṇākṛṣṇavartnaṇa" iti nāḍya़ā leśato `jātabedasa’ iti yogasāranakulīśa yogaparāyaṇayoḥ (1) sūryyaevāgnipadasya prayuktatvāt|| tathāca, amṛtasiddhau
"yone vahniḥ savai sūryyo yaḥ sūryyaḥ sa hutāśanaḥ|
etāvekakarau dṛṣṭau sūkṣama(+) bhedena bhedinau" ||
"evaṃ ca mandaṃ piṅgalayā punaḥ padamapi saphalaṃ, punardhyāyan ityaiva manvaya sāmañjasyāt| anyapūrvyarecakānantaryyasyānuktatvāt puna recayediti punaḥ śandānanvayaḥ syāt idamatrālocanīyam, `nāsā śaśabhṛddhimva’ mityatra nāsāgrapadaṃ bhrūmadhyaṃ lakṣayati| bhrūmadhyasya candrādhiṣṭhānatvaina sarbbayogaśāstreṣu prasiddhatvādatastaddhyānasya tatraivocatavāt| pūrvva vaddhvayāyedityatra dvādaśamātrā parimita kālalyaiva(+) parāmarśaḥ natvanyasya| dehamadhya iti ladhyāharaṇīyam| kumbhake tatraiva vāyordhāraṇāt| tatścāyamarthaḥ `śravat pīyūṣadhāraughaṃ vījayutaṃ candramaṇḍalaṃ bhrūmadhye’(+) netrābhyāmālokyāmṛtamiśritaṃ vāyuṃ dvādaśamātrikaṃ pūrayet| tato dehamadhye vījayutaṃ sphurajjvālāvalīkalitaṃ trikoṇamagniṇḍalaṃ dhyāyan dvādaśa (+) mātrakaṃ vāyuṃ suṣumnāyāṃ dhārayet| tato hṛdi nābhau vā
p.167
vahnirupaṃ sūryyaṃ dhyāyam dvādaśa mātrakaṃ vāyuṃ piṅgalāya virecayediti| punardhyānādipūrvvakaṃ piṅgalayāpūryyahṛdi(+) niruddhvediḍa़yā virecayet|
yogabhāskare—
"iḍa़yā pūrayedvāyuṃ dhārayettu suṣumnayā|
recayet piṅgalānāḍyā prāṇāyāmakramaṃ spṛtaḥ"|
ebañca yāvannāḍa़īśuddhisūcakāni cihnāni bhavanti tāvat prātarmaṃdhyāhna (+) sandhyākāle ṣaṭhkṛtva ācaret| tataḥ śuddhanāḍa़īgaṇoyogīṃ prāṇoyāme samarthaḥ syāt| tadetat uktam|
"yāvadetāni saṃpaśyet tāvadevaṃ samācaret " iti—
skandapurāṇe --
"śuddhimeti yadā sarvvaṃ nāḍa़īcakraṃ malākulam|
tadaiva jāyate yogī kṣamaḥ prāṇa nibandhane||
dṛḍha़āsano yathāśakti prāṇaṃ candreṇa pūrayet|
recayedatha sūryyeṇa vāmāyāmo'ya mucyate||
śravat pīyūṣadhāraughaṃ dhyāyaṃścandramasantviti|
vāmāyāmena yogīndraḥ sukhamāpnoti tatkṣaṇāt||
raviṇā prāṇamākṛṣya pūraye daudarīṃ darīm|
kumbhayitvā śanaiḥ paścāt yogī candreṇa recayet|
jvalajjavalana puñjābhaṃ śīlayannuṣṇaguṃhṛdi||
anena yāmyāyāmena yogīndraḥ sukhabhāgbhavet|
itthaṃ māsatrayābhyāsāt ubhayāyāma sevanāt||
śuddhanāḍa़īgaṇoyogī siddhaḥ prāṇo'bhidhīyate|
yatheṣṭaṃ dhāraṇaṃvāyoranalasya pradīpanam||
nādābhivyaktirārogyaṃ bhavennāḍa़ī viśodhanāt"|
p.168
haṭhayoge -- "prāṇañcediḍa़yā pivenniyamito bhūyo'nyathā recayet 
pītvā piṅgalayā samoraṇamatho vaddhvā tyajedvāmayā|
sūryyācandramasoranena vidhinā vimbadvayaṃ dhyāyatām, 
śuddhānāḍa़īgaṇā bhavanti yamināṃ māsatrayādūrddhvataḥ||
yadānāḍa़ī-viśuddhiḥ syāttadācintā nirākṛtā|
kāyasyakṛśatā kānti stadājāyeta niścitam||
prātarmadhyandinesāyamarddharātre ca kumbhakān|
śanairaśītiparyyantaṃ caturvvāraṃ samabhyaset||
kanīyasi bhavet svedaḥ kampo bhavati madhyame|
uttiṣṭhatya tameprāṇa-rodhepadmāsanasthite||
jalena śramajālena gātramarddanasācaret|
laghutā dṛḍha़tācāpi tenagātrasyajāyate"||
śāstrāntare, -- (1)
"prāṇāyāmānnaraḥṣaṣṭhiṃ kuryyādeva maharmukhe|
catvāriṃśācca madhyāhne sandhyāyāṃ viṃśatirbhavet||
arddharātre viṃśatiḥ syādevaṃ prāṇavinigrahaḥ" | iti||
dattātreyaḥ—
"asmin pīṭheṃ samāstīryya hyāsanannistaraṅgakam|
atropaviśya medhāvī padmāsana samanvitaḥ||
ṛjukāyaḥ prāñjaliśca praṇamediṣṭadevatām|
tatādakṣiṇahastasyahyaṅguṣṭhenaiva piṅgalām (1)|
nirudhya pūrayedvāyu miḍa़yātuśanaiḥ śanaiḥ||
p.169
yathāśaktyā nirodhena tataḥ kuryyācca(+)kumbhakam|
tatastyajet piṅgalayā śanaireva na vegataḥ||
punaḥ piṅgalayāpūryya pūrayedudaraṃ śanaiḥ|
dhārayitvā yathāśakti recayediḍa़yāśanaiḥ||
yayātyajettayāpūryyadhārayedanirodhataḥ|
evaṃ prātaḥ prakurbbīta tathā viṃśatikumbhakān||
kumbhakaṃ sahitaṃ nāma sarvvanigraha-varjjitam|
evaṃ madhyāhna(+) samaye kuryyādviṃśati kumbhakān|
kuryyādrecaka pūrābhyāṃ sahitaṃ prativāsaram||
sahitorecapūrābhyāṃ tasmātsahita kumbhakaḥ|
kuryyādevaṃ caturvvāra manālasyo dinedine||
evaṃ māstrayaṃkuryyānnāḍa़īśuddhistato bhavet|
yadānāḍa़ī viśuddhiḥ syāttadā cihnāni yoginaḥ||
jāyantetāni dehevai tanme nigadataḥ śrṛṇu|
śarīra laghutādīptirjaṭharāgnivivarddhanam|
kṛśatvañca śarīrasya tadā jāyetaniścatam"|| iti|
yogabhāskare, --
"nāsikārandhra yugmeṃna sūtramākṛṣyagharṣaṇam|
jīrādhika-sitābhakṣya utkledaśca jalānnayoḥ||
nāḍa़ī śuddhaumuni-proktā upāyā evamādayaḥ|
yathokta-bhojanaṃvāyorbandhaścaivetimematiḥ"||
yājñavalkyaḥ, -- (1)
"na prāṇenanāpānena vaigairvāyuṃ-samutsṛjet|
yena śaktūn karasthāṃśca śvāsayogena cālayet||
p.170
śanairnāsāpuṭevāyumutsṛjennatu vegataḥ|
nakampayeccharīrantu sa yogo paramomataḥ"|| iti| 
skandapurāṇe, --
"atha vāhyāsane yasmin sukhamasyopajāyate|
svastikādau tadadhyasya yogaṃyuñjīta yogavid||
ūrusthottāna caraṇaḥ savye nyasyottaraṃ karam|
uttāna kiñcidunnāmya vaktaṃ(+)viṣṭabhyacorasā||
nimīlitākṣaḥ satvastho dantairddantānasaṃspṛśan|
tālusthācala-jihvaśca sambṛttāsyaḥ suniścalaḥ||
sanniyamyendriyagrāmaṃ nātinīcocchitāsanaḥ|
madhyamañcottamañcātha prāṇāyāma mupakramet||
trayānāmapilaghumadhyamottamānāṃ prāṇāyāmāṇaṃ lakṣaṇaṃ bakṣyate| (+)
"krameṇa sebyamāno'sau nayate yatracecchati|
prāṇāyāmeṇa yuktena sarvvavyādhikṣayobhavet||
ayuktābhyāsa yogena sarvvavyādhi samudbhavaḥ|
hikkāśvāsaścakāśaśca śiraḥ karṇākṣi-vedanāḥ||
bhavanti vividhā rogāḥ pavanasya vyatikramāt|
yuktaṃ yuktaṃ tyajed vāyuṃ yuktaṃ yuktañca pūrayet||
yuktaṃyuktañca vadhnīyāditthaṃ siddhyati yogavit|
haṭhānniruddhaḥ prāṇo'yaṃ romakūpeṣu niḥsaret||
dehaṃ vidārayatyeṣaḥ kuṣṭhādi janayatyapi|
tataḥ pratyāyitavyo'sau krameṇāraṇyahastivat||
vanyagajogajārirvā krameṇa mṛdutāmiyāt|
karotiśāstradṛṣṭyāvai na tañca pagliṅghayet||
p.171
tathā prāṇohṛdisthoyaṃ yoginākramayogataḥ|
gṛhītaḥ sevyamānastu viśrambham upagacchati||
yāvaddehesthitaḥ prāṇo jīvitaṃ tāvaducyate|
nirgate tatra maraṇaṃ tataḥ prāṇaṃ nirodhayet||
yāvad vaddho maruddehe yāvad vṛttau nirāśrayam|
yāvad dṛṣṭirbhruvormadhye tāvatkālabhayaṃ kutaḥ||
kālasādhyamatobrahmaprāṇāyāma paraḥ sadā|
yoginaḥ siddhimāpannāḥ (+) samyak prāṇa-niyantraṇāt"||
vāsiṣṭhe rāma uvāca—
"brahmannati ciraṃkālaṃ kathaṃ jīvanti yoginaḥ|
etanme saṃśayaṃcchindhi sarvvabrahmabidāṃvara! |
śrīvaśiṣṭha uvāca—
"prāṇānāṃspandanāt spandaṃstacchāntau te'dadhansamam| (1)
yataḥ sthitādhāraṇāyāntanna naśyanti yoginaḥ||
savāhyābhyantaraspandaścittajovātajo'thavā|
na yasya vidyate tasya dūrasthau vikṛtikṣayau||
sa vāhyābhyantare śānte spande pavana-kṣetasoḥ|
dhātavaḥ saṃsthitiṃ dehe na tyajanti kadācana||
asamyak saṃsthite bhūpe yathāvarṇāśramaḥ kramaḥ"|| iti
mārkaṇḍye ya purāṇe, --
śrṛṇuṣva ca mahīpāla ! prāṇāyāmasya lakṣaṇam|
yuñjataśca sadāyogaṃ yādṛg vihita (+)māsanam||

p.172
padmamarddhāsanaṃvā'pi tathā svastika māsanam|
āsthāya yogaṃ yuñjīta kṛtvāca praṇavaṃ hṛdi||
samaḥ samāsanobhūtvā saṃhatyacaraṇāvubhau|
saṃvṛttāsyastathaivorū samyagviṣṭabhya cāgrataḥ||
pārṣṇibhyāṃ liṅgavṛṣaṇāvaspṛśan prayataḥ sthitaḥ|
kiñcidunnamitaśirā daṇḍairdantān saṃspṛśan||
saṃpaśyan nāsikāgraṃ svaṃdiśaścānavalokayan|
itthaṃ yogī yatāhāraḥ prāṇāyāma-parāyaṇaḥ||
jitāṃ jitāṃ śanairbhūmi māroheta yathāgṛham|
doṣān vyādhi tathāmoha makrāntābhūmi revaca||
vivarddhayati nārohettasmād mūmimanirjitām|
upāyaścātra kathito yogibhiḥ parasarṣibhiḥ||
yena vyādhyādayodoṣā na jāyante hi yoginaḥ|
yathātoyārthinastoyaṃ yantranālādibhiḥ śanaiḥ||
āpiveyuḥ tathā vāyuṃ pived yogījitaśramaḥ|
mṛdutvaṃ sevyamānāstu siṃhaśārddūla-kuñjarāḥ||
yathā yānti tathā prāṇoḥ vaśyobhavati yoginaḥ|
viśvāsatāṃ yathecchāto nāgaṃ nayati hastipaḥ||
tatheva yogīsvacchandaṃ prāṇaṃ nayati sādhitam|
yathāhi sādhitaḥ siṃho mṛgān hanti na mānavān||
tadvanniruddhapavanaḥ kilviṣaṃ na nṝṇāṃ tanum|
tasmād yuktaḥ sadā yogī prāṇāyābha parobhavet"||iti|
yājñavalkyaḥ –
"tammāttvañca varārohe! nityaṃ karmma samācara|
sandhyākāleṣu vā nityaṃ prāṇasaṃyamanaṃ kuru||
p.173
prāṇāyāma parāḥ sarvve prāṇāyāma-parāyāṇāḥ|
prāṇāyāma biśuddhā ye te yānti paramā gatim||
prāṇāyāmādṛtenānyat tārakaṃ narakādiṣu|
saṃsārārṇavamagnānāṃ tārakaḥ prāṇa-saṃyamaḥ" | iti
atha prāṇāyāma nirūpaṇam| tatra bhagavān patañjaliḥ, --
"tasmin sati śvāsa praśvāsayorgativicchedaḥ prāṇāyāma" (1) iti tatra bhāṣyakṛtaḥ "satyāsena vāhyasya vāyorācamanaṃ śvāsaḥ kaukṣyasya vāyorniḥ sāraṇaṃ praśvāsaḥ, tayorgativiccheda ubhayābhāvaḥ prāṇāyāma" iti (2) tatra vācaspati vyākhyā – (3) "recaka pūraka kumbhakeṣvasti śvāsapraśvāsayorgativicchedaḥ, prāṇāyāmaḥ" sāmānya-lakṣaṇa metat—tathāhi yatra vāhyavāyurācamyāntarddhāryyate pūrake tatrāstiśvāsapraśvāsayorgativiccheda iti| yatrā'pi kaukṣyovāyurvirecyate vahirdhāryyate recake tatrasthitaḥśvāsa praśvāsayogativicchedaḥ| evaṃ kumbhaketūbhayābhāvaḥ iti bhojadevaistu (4) "tasminnāsanesthairyyesati tannimittaka prāṇāyāmalakṣaṇe yogāṅgaviśeṣo'nuṣṭheyobhavati| kīdṛśaḥ śvāsa praśvāsayostridhārecana (+) pūraṇākṣepadvāreṇa vāhyābhyantareṣu sthāneṣu nāsāgre hṛdayenābhauca gateḥ pravāhamya vicchedodhāraṇaṃ prāṇāyāma ityucyate"|
skandapurāṇe, --
"ṣaṭtriṃśadaṅgulo haṃsaḥ prayāṇaṃ kurutevahiḥ|
savyāpasavyamorgena tena prāṇo'bhidhīyate||
p. 174
prāṇodehagatovāyurāyāmastannivandhanam" (1) iti||
ādipurāṇe—
"antaścarāṇaāṃ vāyūnāṃ vāhyabhyantara-rodhanam|
prāṇāyāma iti proktaḥ dvividhaḥ sa ca karthyate"||
dvividhaḥ sagavarbhovigavarbhaśceti|
yājñavalkyaḥ –
"prāṇāpāna samāyogaḥ prāṇāyāma itīritaḥ|
prāṇāyāmastridhāproktaḥ pūra-kumbhaka recakaiḥ||
pūraka lakṣaṇam—
"vāhyādāpūraṇaṃ vāyorudare pūrako hisaḥ"
kālikāpurāṇe -- "pūrakaḥ pūraṇātproktaḥ"
devalaḥ -- "niḥśvāsādyamana pūraṇa"miti—
skandapurāṇe—
"vāhyenavāyunā dehaṃdṛtivat paripūrayet|
paripūrṇastathātiṣṭhet pūraṇāt pūrakaḥsmṛtaḥ||
yājñavalkyaḥ—
vāhyesthitaṃ nāsa-puṭenavāyum,
ākṛṣyatenaiva śanaiḥ samantāt|
nāḍa़īścasarvvāḥ paripūrayed yaḥ,
sa pūrako nāma mahānirodhaḥ"||
p.175
devalaḥ -- "tenaiva vartmanātasya koṣṭhasyāntaḥ praveśanam"|
"pūraṇāt pūrakaḥ proktaḥ"
iti tena nāsāpuṭa-mukha-brahma-randhra-lakṣaṇena|
vāyu saṃhitāyām, --
"nāsikā puṭamaṅgalyāpīḍyaikamapareṇa tu|
nāsāpuṭenāpareṇa pūraṇāt pūrakaḥ smṛtaḥ"||
kumbhakalakṣaṇaṃ yājñavalkyaḥ – (2)
"saṃpūryyakumbhavad vāyorddhāraṇaṃ kumbhakobhavet"
kandapurāṇe –
"namuñcati na gṛhnāti vāyumantarbahiḥ sthitam|
āpūryyakumbhavattiṣṭhedacalaḥ sa tu kumbhakaḥ"||
yogi yājñavalkyaḥ –
"na recakonaiva ca pūrako'yaṃ, nāsāgravarttīsthitaeva bāyuḥ| (1)
suniścalaṃ dhārayate krameṇa, kumbhākhyametat pravadantitajjñāḥ||
"recakaṃ pūrakaṃ muktvā yatsukhaṃ vāyudhāraṇam|
prāṇāyāmo' yamityuktaḥ savai kevalakumbhakaḥ||"
p.176
ārecyāpūryya yat kuryyāt mavai sahitakumbhakaḥ|
yecānye kumbhaka viśeṣāste vakṣyante|
recaka lakṣaṇaṃ yājñavalkyaḥ –
"vahiryadrecanaṃ vāyorudarādrecakaḥ smṛtaḥ|"
tathā-- "niṣkramyanāsā vivarādaśeṣam,
prāṇaṃvahiḥ śūnyamivānilena|
nirucchya saṃtiṣṭhaticorddhvabāyuḥ,
sa recakonāma mahānirodhaḥ"||
kālikā purāṇe –
"bhānurdevatayā muñcadvāyuṃ sarvvāṅga saṃyutam|
vyāhṛti praṇavādyena yattadrecaka saṃjñitam"||
skandapurāṇe –
"nāsikā puṭamaṅgulyā pīḍa़yitvā pareṇa tu|
udarādrecayedvāyuṃ recanādrecakaḥ smṛtaḥ||"
devalaḥ—
"ajasraniḥśvāso recakaḥ| ajasro'khaṇḍaḥ, niḥśvāso niḥsāraṇam|
"yadvāyorvrahmagandhreṇa na cāsye na ca vā vahiḥ|
niḥsāraṇa mīdaryyasya recanātsyātsarecakaḥ"||
jaigīṣabya yogaśāstre, --
"recakaścharddanādvāyoḥ kaukṣyasya parikīrttitaḥ"|
laghumadhyamottama bhedena prāṇāyāmatraividhyaṃ kathyate|
p.177
nāradīyapurāṇe, --
"laghurddhvādaśamātrastu dviguṇaḥ sa tu madhyamaḥ|
triguṇābhi stu mātrābhiruttarīya udāhṛtaḥ|| (+)
prathamena jayetsvedaṃ dvitīyena tu vepathum|
viṣādaṃ(+) hi tṛtoyena jayeddoṣānnanukramāt"||
nandipurāṇe,--
"kanīyasastumātrābhiḥsthitodvādaśabhiryataḥ|
madhyamo dviguṇābhistu mātrābhiḥ parikīrttitaḥ||
triguṇābhiḥsmṛto hyevaṃ jyeṣṭhaḥ sthairyyaṃ samānayet"|
devalaḥ, --
sa punareka dvitribhiruddhvātairmṛdumadhyastīkṣrobhavati|
skandapurāṇe –
"kanīyān sakṛdudghātādyatodvādaśamātrakaḥ|
madhyamastu triruddhātaiḥ ścaturviṃśatimātrakaḥ|
uttamastu trirudghātaiḥ ṣaṭtriṃśanmātrakaḥ smṛtaḥ|
svedakampābhighātānāṃ janakaścottamomataḥ||
ebhiścihnaiścihṇitavyaḥ siddhaḥ prāṇo'trayoginā"|
vāyu saṃhitāyām, --
"kanyasādikramavaśāt prāṇāpāna-nirodhanam|
taccaturddhopadiṣṭaṃsyānmātrāguṇāvibhāgataḥ||
kanyasaḥ sakṛduddhātaḥ sa ca dvādaśamātrakaḥ|
madhyamastu dviruddhātaścaturviṃśatimātrakaḥ||
uttarastu triruddhātaḥ ṣaṭtniṃśanmātrakaḥ smṛtaḥ|
svedakampādijanakaḥ prāṇāyāmastadttaraḥ||
p.178
ānandodbhava romāñca netnodvarttāṅgamoḍa़nam| (+)
jalpa bhramaṇa mūrcchādyaṃ jāyatecātrayoginaḥ||
liṅga purāṇe, --
"prāṇāyāmasya mānantu mātrādvādaśakaṃ smṛtam|
nīco dvādaśamātrastu sakṛduddhātardūritaḥ|| (+)
madhyamastudviruddhātaścaturvviṃśatimātrakaḥ|
mukhyastu ya striruddhātaḥ (+)ṣaṭtriṃśanmātra(+) ucyate||
prasve dakampanotthāna-janakaśca yathyakramam|
ānandojāyatecātra nidāghūrṇastathaivaca||
romāñcodhvani samvittiraṅgamoḍa़na kampanam|
bhramaṇa svedajalpādya samvinmūrcchā jayed yadā||
tadottamottamaḥ groktaḥ prāṇāyāmaḥ suśobhanaḥ"|
ghūrṇaścittāndolananaṃ dhvaniḥ sagvittiścāntaranādābhivyaktiḥ||
vasiṣṭhayoge, --
"prasvedaṃ janayedyastu prāṇāyāmeṣu so'dhamaḥ|
madhyamaḥ kampanātproktaḥ utyānāccottamobhavet"|
mātrālakṣaṇantu yathā, brahmavaivartte, --
"nimeṣonmeṣaṇaṃ mātrādvirvālaghūkṣarantathā|"
nandipurāṇe –
"tvaritaṃ laghubhir gharmmair mātrādvādaśabhiḥ (+) smṛtā" | iti 
skandapurāṇe, --
"jānuṃ pradakṣiṇīkṛtya na drutaṃ na vilagvitam|
karoti choṭikāṃ yāvat sā mātreticagīyate" || iti||
p.179
dattātreyaḥ –
"aṅgulīrmokṣitāstistrojānvoścaparimārjjayet|
pradadyācchoṭikāmekāṃmātrāsaṃkhyāyate tathā|| iti|
tathaiva—
"dvādaśośchvāsaḥ (+) kanīyān dviguṇomadhyamaḥ smṛtaḥ|
triguṇottamamātraḥ syānmātrābhedaḥ prakīrttitaḥ"||
yājñavalkyaḥ –
"aṅgulimokṣa tritayaṃ jānūpariparimārjjanañcāpi|
"tālatrayamapi tajjñā mātrāsaṃjñāṃ praśaṃsanti||
godohaṃ vatmapānaṃ vā iṣukṣepamathāpivā|
ghaṇṭāyāstanitañcāpi atimātrāhyudāhṛtā|
deśakālānusāreṇa sadāsevyāḥ śanaiḥ śanaiḥ"| iti|
udghātalakṣaṇaṃ ca, yathā śāstrāntare –
"prāṇenotsāryyamānena apānaḥ pīḍyate yadā|
gatvāco[r]ddhvaṃ nivartteta etadudghātalakṣaṇam"||
`atha pūrakakumbhakarecakeṣu dhyānajapaviśeṣaḥ pradarśyate|
vyāsaḥ –
"nābhipadmasthitaṃ dhyāyetu karaṃktaṃ pūrakeṇa tu|
nīlotpalābhaṃ hṛdpadme kumbhakena janārddanaṃ||
lalāṭasthaṃ śiva śvetaṃ recakena vicintayet|
śuddha sphaṭika saṅkāśaṃ nirmmalaṃ pāpanāśanam"||
dṛhaspatiḥ –
"raktaṃ prajāpatiṃ dhyāyet viṣṇaṃ nīlotpalapramaṃ|
śaṅkaraṃ tryamvakaṃ śvetaṃ dhyāyanmucyet vandhanāt"||
p.180
yājñavalkyaḥ –
"kumbhakena hṛdisthāne dhyāyet tu kamalāsanaṃ|
brahmāṇaṃ rakta gaurāṅkaṃ caturvaktaṃ pitāmahaṃ|| (+)
nīlotpaladalaśyāmaṃ nābhimadhye pratiṣṭhitaṃ|
caturbhujaṃ mahātmānaṃ pūrakeṇa vicintayet||
recakeneśvaraṃ vidyāllalāṭasthaṃ maheśvaraṃ|
śuddhasphaṭika saṅkāśaṃ nirmmalaṃpāpanāśanam||
pūrake viṣṇusāyujyaṃ kumbhrake vrahmaṇo'ntikam|
recakeṇa tṛtīyena prāpnuyādaiśvaraṃ padam|
yadā nirodha saṃyogāddevatātraya cintanam|
agne rvāyorapāṃ yogāttadā śuddhyetavaitribhiḥ||
nirodhājjāyatevāyurvāyoragniśca jāyate|
āgnerāpo'bhijāyante tato'ntaḥ śudhyatetribhiḥ||
`prāṇāyāma’ iti proktaḥ pūrakumbhaka recakaiḥ|
varṇatrayātmakāhyete pūrakumbhakarecakāḥ||
sa eṣaḥ praṇavaḥ proktaḥ prāṇāyāmaśca tanmayaḥ|
iḍa़yāvāyumāropya pūrayitvodaraṃsthitaḥ||
śanaiḥ poḍa़śabhātrābhirakāraṃ tatra saṃsmaraṇ| (+)
dhārayet pūritaṃ paścātprāṇaṃ vāhyanilānvitam||
śanaiḥ piṅgalayāgārgi! dvātriṃśanmātrayāpunaḥ|
makāramūrttimatrāpi saṃsmaraṇasusamāhitaḥ||
prāṇāyāmo bhavedeṣaḥ punaścaivaṃ samabhyaset|
praśvedajanakoyastu prāṇāyāmeṣu so'dhamaḥ|
kampanānmadhyamaḥ proktaunmānāccottamaḥ smṛtaḥ|
pūrvvaṃ pūrvvaṃ prakurvvīta yāvaduttama sambhravaḥ||(+)
p.181
sambhavatyuttame gārgi ! prāṇāyāmasukhībhavet|
prāṇojayati tenaiva dehasyāntastato'dhikam||
dehaścottiṣṭhate tena kṛtāsana parigrahaḥ|
niśvāsocchrāsakau tasya na vidyate kadācana||
dehe yadyapi tau syātāṃ svābhāvikaguṇāvubhau|
tathāpi naśyata stauca prāṇāyāmottamena hi||
tayornāśe mamarthaḥsyāt karttuṃ kevala kumbhakam|
sahitaṃ kevala siddhiḥsyāttāvat sahitamabhyaset|
kevale kumbhakesiddhe reca-pūraṇavarjjita||
natasyadurllabhaṃ kiñcitriṣulokeṣu vidyate|
manojavatvaṃ labhate pātakānica naśyati||
mukterayaṃ mahāmārgī makārākhyo'ntarātmakaḥ|
nādaṃñcot pādayatyeṣa (+) kumbhakaḥ prāṇasaṃyamaḥ||
prāṇa saṃyamanaṃnāma dehe prāṇasyadhāraṇam
eṣa prāṇajyopāyaḥ sarvvamṛtyūpaghātakaḥ"||
haṭhayoge, --
kumbhakāt kuṇḍalī vodhaḥ kuṇḍalīvo(ro) dhobhavet|
anargulaḥ suṣumnānto haṭha siddhiśca jāyate"||
vāyusaṃhitāyām –
"recakāditrayamidaṃ na drutaṃ na vilamvitam|
udghātakramayogena manyase yogasādhanam|
recakāditrayābhyāso nāḍa़īśodhana pūrvvakam|
śvāsātkramaṇa (1) "paryyantaṃ prokto yogānumārgaṇaiḥ||

p.182
sa dvivighaḥ prāṇāyāmaḥ sargabha vigarbha bhedāt|
tathāhi, nāradīya purāṇe, --
"sargaṃbhayogamātiṣṭhedagarbhaṃ vātu(yu) yantritaḥ|
prāṇāyāmo'sya mathanaṃ tasyādauyuktirucyate||
agarbhaśca vigarbhaśca dvividhaḥ saṃprakorttitaḥ"|
asya prāṇākhyasyavāyormathanamiva mathanaṃ
prāṇāyāmo navanītātmasthānasthānīyatvāt| tayo-
lakṣaṇaṃ yathā, liṅgapurāṇe,
"sagarbho'garbha ityuktaḥ sajapo vijapaḥ kramāt"
vāyu saṃhitāyām, --
"japadhyānaṃ vināgarbhaḥ sagarbhastat samanvitaḥ|
agarbhādgarbha saṃyuktaḥ prāṇāyāmaḥ śatādhikaḥ||
japo manasā prasaṃkhyānam|
nandipurāṇe,--
"jāpyena tu japaṃkuryyāt avilamvitamadrutam|
manasaiva prasaṃkhyānaṃ prāṇāyāmavidhī sadā"||
jāpyena gāyatryādimantreṇa|
skandapurāṇe, --
"mantrāṃśa devatādhyānāt sajjavālaḥ (sagarbha) parikīrttitaḥ|
mantrāvacana (1)vidyastu praśāntaḥ parikīrttitaḥ|
mantra oṃkāraityuktaḥ sarvva mantravijjitvaraḥ||
p.183
sarvvadaivata sandeha ādhāraḥ sarvvayoginām|
yasyodare mantrajātaṃ sarvvadeva pratiṣṭhitam||
satu mātrātrayayuto (1) i-u-mā iti tāḥsmṛtāḥ||
asmin mātrātraye sarvva bhāvābhāvaṃ pratiṣṭhitam|
brahma viṣṇumaheśānāṃ kramāntamātrātrayaṃsmṛtam|
skandomāśaṅkaraścoktābhūrbhūvaḥ svaśca korttitāḥ|
trayo'gnayastu tāḥ proktā mātrā praṇavasaṃyutāḥ||
tasya mātrātrayadhyānāṃt prāṇāyāmaḥ sadhūmakaḥ|
kalāṃśa devatā dhyānāt "sajjvālaḥ" parikīrttitaḥ||
mātrādhūmāṃśanirvvastu yoganāḍa़ī samāśrayāt||
"praśānta uktaḥ śāstreṣu sarvvabhāva vinirgataḥ||"
etemedāḥ sa garbhasya, iha sagarbhasya prāṇāyāmasya trayobhedāḥ pradṛśyante| sadhūmaḥ sajvālaḥ praśānta iti | tatra mātrātrayadhyānāt sa dhūmakaḥ| mātrārupāṇāṃ prāṇavāṃśānāṃ taddevatānāṃ dhyānāt sajvālaḥ| "mantrāvacanaviddhastu" praśāntaḥ| mantrasyāvacanaṃ manasyālocanam| tena viddhayuktaḥ| anenārthānmantravācyārthadhyānarahito'potyuktaṃ bhavati| "kalāmātrādhūmāṃśa" ityādi mātrā tadaṃśadhyānarahitaḥ praśāntaḥ| atrāṃśa śabdena mātrāvācyādevatāgṛhyante| mātrā dhūmadevatājāvālārūpanirgato vaktuḥsamāśrayāt| yoganāḍa़ī samāśrayāccapraśānta iti| yoganāḍa़ī suṣumnā| tadevaṃ sagarbhohi prāṇāyāmodarśitaḥ|
mārkaṇḍeyapurāṇe, --
"omityuccāraṇāt sarvvaṃ gṛhītaṃ sadasdbhavet|
ityetadakṣaraṃbrahma paramoṅkāra saṃjñitam||
p.184
praṇavo dhanuḥ śarohyātmā brāhmaṇohyāyudhānvitaḥ|
apramattenaveddhavyaṃ śaravattanmayobhavet (1)"||
yājñavalkyaḥ –
"siddhāntānāñca sarvveṣāṃ vedavedāntayostathā|
anyeṣāmapiśāstrāṇāṃ niṣṭhāoṃkāra ucyate||
praṇavādyāḥ śrutāvedāḥ praṇave paryyavasthitāḥ|
vāṅmayaṃ praṇavesarvvaṃ tasmāt praṇavamabhyaset||
tathā, -- praṇaveṇa sasaṃyuktā vyāhṛtibhiśca saṃyutāḥ|
gāyatrīrvājapettisraḥ prāṇasaṃyamane dvijaḥ"||
anena gāyatrīgarbhaḥ prāṇāyāmodarśitaḥ|
asya lakṣaṇamāha, manuḥ, --
"sabyāhṛtiṃ sa praṇavāṃ gāyatrīṃ śirasāsaha|
triḥpaṭhedāyata prāṇāḥ prāṇāyāmaḥ sa ucyate||
vyāhṛtayobhūrādyāḥ saptavikhyātāḥ|
"saptavyāhṛtayaḥ proktā (2) prāṇāyāmeṣu nityaśaḥ| bhūrbhūvaḥ svarmaharjanaḥ tapaḥ satyaṃ tathaivaca"|| iti yogiyājñalkya vacanāt|
śivaścātra – oṃ āpojyoti rityādi yojurbedomantraḥ| 
tathāca kyāsaḥ , --
"oṃ āpojyotirityeṣa bhūrādiṣu navasvapi"|
p.185
praṇavayogamāha, yājñavalkyaḥ –
"bhūrbhūvaḥ svarmaharjjanastapaḥ satyaṃ tathaiva ca"|
pratyoṅkārasamāyuktāstatastat savituḥ param"||
omāpojyotirityetat śiraḥ paścāt prayojayet|
trirāvarttana yogāddhi prāṇāyāmastu śabditaḥ"|| iti|
oṃkāro'pyatra mantrādaudraṣṭavyaḥ|
tathāca yamaḥ—
"oṃṅkāra pūrvvikāḥ sapta japettu vyāhṛtī stathā|
śirasāsaha gāyatrīṃ prāṇāyāmaḥ parantapaḥ||
japedityatra manaseti draṣṭavyam|
tathāca, samvarttaḥ –
"praṇavena tu saṃyuktā vyāhṛtīḥ sapta nityaśaḥ|
sāvitrīṃ śirasā sārddhaṃ manasā triḥ paṭheddhvijaḥ||(+)
yājñavalkyaḥ, --
"brāhmaṇaḥ śrutisampannaḥ svadharmma nirataḥ sadā|
sa vaidikaṃ japenmantraṃ laukikaṃ na kadācanaṃ||
kecidbhūtahitārthāya japtusicchanti vaidikam|
dvijasya kṣatriyasyoktaḥ prāṇasaṃyamane japaḥ||
"baiśyānāṃ dharmmayuktānāṃ strośūdrāṇāṃ tapasvinām|
prāṇasaṃyamane gārgi! mantraḥ praṇava varjjitaḥ||
namo'ntaḥ śivamantrovā vaiṣṇavoveṣyate vudhaiḥ|
yadvā samabhyasecchūdo laukikaṃ vidhipūrvvakam||
na baidikaṃ japecchūdraḥ striyaśca na kadācana"|| iti|
tadevaṃ sagarbhabhedā nirūpitāḥ, skandapurāṇe, --
p.186
"recakaḥ śūnyakaścaiva pūrakaḥ kumbhrakastathā|
prāṇāgamana sannyāsaḥ prāṇāyāmaścaturvvidhaḥ"||
tatra recitādayolakṣitāḥ kecidvakṣyanteca|
śūnya lakṣaṇaṃ kālikā purāṇe, --
"tataḥ sarvvanirābhāsaṃ madhye jñānaṃ vicintayet|
vāhyasthaṃ vodhayedvāyu śanyakantadvinirddiśet"||
tata iti bhānu daivatyayā nāḍyā vyāhṛti praṇavādyena recakaṃ kṛtveti| 
jaigīṣevya yogaśāstre, --
"āyāmaiḥ pañcabhiḥ khinno recakādyairyathākramam|
recakaḥ pūrakaḥ kumbha utkarṣohyapakarṣakaḥ"||
khiboyogī (+) siddhimāpnoti iti samvandhaḥ|
padmapurāṇe, --
"prāṇaḥ prāṇamayo vāyu rvāyurātmā nigadyate|
tasyāyāmaḥ samuddiṣṭaḥ ṣaṭpakāraḥ suyoktṛbhiḥ||
vāyorapi nirodhena yuktaḥ samabhidhīyate"||
ṣaṭprakārakatvañca, --
recakapūrakau dvau sahitaḥ kevalaśceti|
kumbhakau dvau dvāvutkarṣāpakarṣakau||
devalaḥ –
"kumbhaḥ saptavidhojñeyo recitādi prabhedataḥ|
recitaḥ pūritaḥ śāntaḥ pratyāhārottarodharaḥ||
samaścetivinirddiṣṭaḥ kumbhakaḥ saptabhedataḥ"|
tathārecitādeḥ saptavidhasyāpi lakṣaṇañcāha, --
p.187
"recitasyavahistambho vāyorecita kumbhakaḥ|
pūritasyodare rodhaḥ proktaḥ pūrita-kumbhakaḥ||
kāyasyāntarvahirvyāptiryā sa syācchāntakumbhakaḥ|
sthānayontare rodhaḥ pratyāhārākhyakumbhakaḥ||
āpūryya yatkramādūrddhva mūrddhvaṃrodhohṛdādiṣu|
uttaraḥ kumbhakaḥ sasyāt adhodhomūrddhvato'dharaḥ||
recanā pūraṇe tyakktā manasā marutodhṛtiḥ|
yā nābhyādipradeśeṣu samaḥ kumbhaḥ prakīrttitaḥ"||
kāyasyeti recakapūrakau vihāya śarīrasyāntarvahiśca manasā vāyorddhāraṇādvyāptirityarthaḥ| "sthānayo" rityatra sīmā draṣṭavyā| pādatala guhyanābhi-hṛdu-dara-kaṇṭha ghaṇṭikā-bhrūmadhya-lalāṭa-brahmarandhrāṇi sthānāni| eteṣu sthāneṣu dvayordvayorantare madhye vāyorddhāraṇam pratyāhārākhyakumbhakaḥ| 
hṛdādisthāneṣu mūrddhatohṛdaya-paryyantaṃ recanāpūraṇe iti sthānabhedāt śamaśāntayorbhedaḥ|
recakādicaturvvidha prāṇāyāmalakṣaṇa māha, bhagavān patañjaliḥ -- "satu vāhyābhyāntarastanbhabṛttirddeśakāla saṃkhyābhiḥ paridṛṣṭodorghasūkṣmaḥ" "vāhyābhyantara viṣayāpokṣo caturtha"| iti (1)
asyārthaḥ –
vāhyavṛttiḥ śvāsorecakaḥ, antarvṛttiḥ praśvāsaḥ pūrakaḥ antastambhavṛttiḥ kumbhakaḥ| recanaṃ pūraṇaṃ vihāya sakṛdvalavadvidhāraka prayatnāvaruddhe tasmin jalamiva niścalatayā prāṇī'vasthāpyata iti kumbhakaḥ|
sa yathā tapte nyastamupale jalaṃ sarvvataḥ pariśuṣyat saṅkocamāpadyate tathā śarore sūkṣmaḥ stabhyeta iti stambhavṛttiḥ| trividho'yaṃ prāṇāyāmo
p.188
deśena (kālena) saṃkhyayā copalakṣito dīrghasūkṣmasaṃjño bhavati| deśena nāsāddhādaśāntādau kālena (tālachoṭikādinā) saṃkhyayā (pūrvvoktayā) copalakṣito, tathā ṣaṭtriṃśanmātrādipramāṇakam, saṃkhyayā copalakṣita etāvadvāraṃ kṛta, etāvadbhiḥ śvāsa praśvāsaiḥ prathama udghāto bhavatītyetajjñānāya saṃkhyayopagrahaṇamupāttam|
udvātaśca nābhimūlāt preditasya (+)vāyoḥ śirasyabhihananam| sa khalvayaṃ divasa pakṣamāsādikrameṇābhyasto dīrghadeśa-kāla-vyāpitayaiva dorghopalakṣyatayā ca sūkṣmo dīrghasūkṣma śabdābhidheyo bhavati| dvitīyārthastu, -- vāhya viṣayo dvādaśāntādiḥ, ābhyantara viṣayo hṛdaya-nābhicakrādiḥ to dvau viṣayāvākṣipya paryyālocyaiva stambharūpogativicchedaḥ sa caturtha prāṇāyāma iti| tṛtīyastrāt kumbhakādayamasya viśeṣo vāhyantyantara viṣayau paryyālocyaiva sahasā taptopalapatita jalavadyugapat stambhravṛttyā niṣpādyate| ayantvālocana sāpekṣam| bahuprayatnapūrvvamabhyasyamāna svāṃ tāmavasthāmāpannastattadavasthāvijayānukrameṇa bhavatīti| caturvvidhasyāpi prāṇāyāmasya phalamāha, bhagavān patañjaliḥ, -- "tataḥ kṣīyate prakāśāvaraṇam" "dhāraṇāsuca yogyatā manasa" – iti (1)| tata datasmāt (+) prāṇāyāmāt prakāśasya cittasattvagatasārvvajñyarūpasya yadāvaraṇaṃ kleśarūpam kṣīyate vinaśyati, tathāca manaso dhāraṇāsu vakṣyabhāṇalakṣaṇāsu yogyatā bhavati| prāṇāyāmaiḥ kṣīṇadoṣaṃ manoyatrayatra dhāryyate tatra tatra sthirobhavatīti sūtrāthaḥ||
"kṣīrādyuktivaśāt sadbhirlakṣyate ca ghṛtaṃ yathā|
dārutovā'nalo labhyo vāyunātmā tathāvyayaḥ||
prāṇāyāso'sya mathanaṃ tasmādau yuktirucyate|
p.189
tathā, --
samastendriyabṛttiśca prāṇovāyuḥ prakaurttitaḥ|
tajjayādindriyāṇyeva nirjjitāni bhavanti hi||
vṛttibṛttimato yasmādabhedaḥ parikīttitaḥ|

śanairniścalatāmeti prāṇāyāmakaṣāhatam"||

cittejātā kācidānandalīlā"||
mūrcchā, --
"pūrakānte gāḍha़taraṃ vandhojālandharaḥ śanaiḥ|

yoginīcakra saṃsevyaḥ sṛṣṭisaṃhāra kārakaḥ|

yogavīje, sūryyabhedanam, --
"āsane sukhade yogī vaddha vajrāsanastataḥ|
dakṣanāḍyā samākṛṣya vahiḥsthaṃ pavanaṃ śanaiḥ||
ākeśāgraṃ nakhāgrañca śirodhāvadhikumbhakam|
tataḥ śanaiḥ savyanāḍyā recayetpavanaṃ sudhoḥ||
kapālaśodhanaṃ vātadoṣaghnaṃ kṛmidoṣahṛt|
punaḥ punaridaṃ kuryyāt `sūryyabhedana’muttamam||"

"mukhaṃ saṃyamya nāḍa़aubhyāmākṛṣya pavanaṃ punaḥ|

gacchata stiṣṭhataḥ kāryya `mujjāyā’ khyañca kumbhakam"||
bhastikā, (+)—
ūrbborupari saṃsthāpya ubhe pādatale tathā|
padmāsanaṃ bhavet samyak sarvvapāpapraṇāśanam|
samyak padmāsanaṃ vaddhvā samagrīvodaraḥ sudhoḥ|
p.191
mukhaṃ saṃyabhya yatne prāṇaṃ ghrāṇena recayet||

dhārayennāsikāṃ madhyatarjjanībhyāṃ vinā dṛḍha़m||

"jihvayā vāyumākṛṣya pūrvvavat kumbhakādanu|

gulma-plīhodaraṃcāpi vāta-pittaṃ kṣudhāṃ tṛṣām|

yājñavalkyaḥ –
"kiñca prāṇajayopāyaṃ tava vakṣyāmi tattvataḥ|
vāhyāt prāṇaṃ samākṛṣya pūrayitvodaraṃ sthitaḥ||
nābhimadhye ca nāsāgraṃ pādāṅguṣṭheca yatnataḥ|

p.192
sarvvaroga vinirnmukto jīvedyogī gataklamaḥ|

yaḥ pivedabhṛtaṃ vidvān sakalaṃ bhadramaśnute||

P.198
pāyūpasthe tathāvastau nābhi-hṛt-kaṇṭha-vakṣasi|

yāvamanolaya stasmin nābhau savitṛmaṇḍale|

santaptāvanhinā tatra vāyunā cālitā svayam|

saṃprāptemārute tasmin suṣamnāyāṃ varānena ! |
mantramuccāryyamanasā hṛnmadhye dhārayet sudhīḥ||

śaroraṃ visisṛkṣuścettadevaṃ sabhyagācaret||

brahmaivāsau bhaved gārgi ! na punarjanmabhāgbhavet"|

apibhrūṇa hataṃ (ṇaṃ) māsāt yunantyaharahaḥ kṛtāḥ||
ṛtutrayād yunantyeva janmāntarakṛtādghāt|
vatsarād brahmavidvān syāttāṃ svasmānnityamabhyaset"|
yogābhyāsaratāścaivaṃ svadharmma niratāścaye|
prāṇa saṃyamanenaiva sarvemuktā bhavantihi" ||
skandapurāṇe, --
prāṇāyāma nirupaṇānantara muktam|
"brahmacārījitakrodhojitalobhovimatsarī|
avdamitthaṃ sadābhyāsād yogīyogonigadyate||"
haṭhapradīpikāyām –
"āsanaṃ kumbhakaṃ citraṃ mudrākhyaṃ karaṇaṃ tathā|
athanādānusandhāna mabhyāsānukrameṇa ca||"
P.212
brahmacārī mitāhārī tyāgī yogaparāyaṇaḥ|
abdādūrddhvaṃ bhavet siddho nātrakāryyavicāraṇā||"
tatraiva –
"vapaḥ kṛśatvaṃ vadena prasannatā
nādasphuṭatvaṃ nayaneca nirmmale|
arogitā vindujayo'gnidīpanaṃ
nāḍa़īviśuddhi rhaṭhaśuddhilakṣaṇam||"
īśvaraprokte –
ārambhaśca ghaṭaścaiva tathā paricāyo'pica|
niṣpattiḥ sarvvayogeṣuṃ yogāvasthābhavantitāḥ||
tathāhi –
"yadānāḍa़ī viśuddhiḥ syāt yoginastattva darśinaḥ|
tadāvidhvasta pāpasya bhavedārambha sambhavaḥ||
cihnāni yogino dehe dṛśyante nāḍa़iśuddhitaḥ|
kathyante mama bhaktāya tāni saṃkṣepato mayā||
samakāyaḥ sugandhiśca sukāntiḥ surasādharaḥ|
prauḍha़vahniḥ subhogīca sukhī sarvvāṅgasundaraḥ||
sampūrṇa hṛdayo yogī sarvvotsāhī valānvitaḥ|
tato yatheṣṭa śaktiḥ syāt yogino vāyudhāraṇe||
yatheṣṭa dhāraṇād vāyoḥ kumbhakaḥ sidhyati dhruvaṃ|
kevale kumbhakesiddhe kiṃ nasyādiha yoginaḥ||
svedaḥ sañjāyate dehe yoginaḥ prathamodhamaḥ
dvitīyo jāyate kampo durddhvaro madhyamo mataḥ||
P.214
bhavaduḥkhāni saṃhṛtya samādhirjāyate tadā|
yadā samādhi sampannaṃ sahajānanda saṃbhṛtam||
cittametat tadāsiddhaṃ sarvvaduḥkhabhavayopahaṃ|
yat karoti yadāyogī sarbbaṃ bhavati tatkṣaṇāt||
yoginaḥ siddhakāyasya jānīyāllakṣaṇaṃ dhruvam|
kāyavyūhādikaṃ yacca parapura praveśanam||
īkṣaṇaṃ śravaṇaṃ dūrāt tat siddhaṃ vāyulakṣaṇam|
jīvanmukti stadājñeyā sarvvābhīṣṭaphalapradā||
jovammukti gatoyogī mahāmukti padaṃ brajet|
svacchāsamādhisampanno yogīndrohi bhavettadā||
tadā mahāsamādhiśca `dharmmamegha’ samāhvayaḥ (1)|
tadānandamayoyogī sarbbajñaḥ sarvadarśanaḥ||
sapūjyatriṣu lokeṣvavyāhatājñaśca sarvadā|
avyāhatagatiḥ prājño vyāhatānanda sambhṛtaḥ||
sarvveśvaryyaguṇopetaḥ sarvvamiddhisamanvitaḥ|
abandhaḥ sarvvabhūtānāṃ yogīndro doṣavarñjitaḥ||
tasmāt sarvvamayoyogī sarvvabhūtamayohisaḥ|
santuṣṭa stārayellokāt kruddhaḥ sarvvavināśakaḥ||
jñātasiddhiryadāyogī tadānaiva bhayaṅkaraḥ|
evaṃ varṣa sahasrāṇi lakṣaṇica śatānica||
parvvatāgre guhāvāsamānandāḥ siddhayogina" iti|
P.215
īśvaraprokte –
tato'bhyāsa krameṇaiva ghaṭādi tritayambhravet|
yena syāt makalā siddhiryoginaḥ praśi(ṣi) tā dhruvam||
bāksiddhiḥ kāmacāritvaṃ dūradṛṣṭi stathaivaca|
dūraśrutiḥ sūkṣmadṛṣṭiḥ paradehe praveśanam||
vinmūtralepane svarṇamadṛśyaṃ karaṇantathā|
bhavantyetāni mahatāṃ khecaratvañca yoginām||
yadā bhaved ghaṭāvasthā pavanābhyāsināṃ parā|
tadā saṃsāra cakre'smin tannāsti yannasādhayet||
dṛḍha़āsano bhavedyogī jñānī deva samastathā|
āsane sudṛḍha़ī bhūte vāyumadhye viśatyasau|
kiñcidvāyu praveśena yogino jñānasambhavaḥ|
sañjāte jñānasambhāre yogīdeva samobhavet||
doṣaduḥkhakṣudhānidrā jarāmṛtyuvivarjvitaḥ|
sṛṣṭisaṃdbāra karttā'sau yogīśvarasamo bhavet||
astuvā māstuvā siddhiratraivākhaṇḍitaṃ mukham|
prāṇā'pānaunādavindū jībātmaparamātmānau|
militvāghaṭate yasmāt tasmādvaighaṭa ucyate||
yāma mātraṃ yadādharttuṃ samarthaḥ syādatantritaḥ|
pratyāhāra stadaivasyānnāntarā bhavati dhruvam||
yadyajjānāti yogīndra stattadātmani bhāvayet||
yairindriyairvvi dhānantu tadindriyajayobhavet||
yāma mātraṃ yadāpūrṇaṃ bhavedabhyāsayogataḥ|
ekavāraṃ prakurbbīta tadā yogītu kumbhakam|
P.216
daṇḍāṣṭakaṃ yadāvāyu ryogini niścalobhavet||
asāmarthyaṃ tatoguptā tiṣṭhedvātṛṇavat sudhīḥ|
tataḥ paricayāvasthā yogino'bhyāsato bhavet||
yadābāyuḥ candrasūryyoṃ tyaktvātiṣṭhati niśvalaḥ|
vāyau paricitevāyuḥ suṣumnā byomni sañcaret||
kriyāśaktiṃ gṛhītvāt cakrān bhitvātu niścatam|
tadā paricayāvasthā bhavedabhyāsayogataḥ||
trikūṭa karmmaṇo yogī tadāpaśyati niścitam|
tataḥ svakarmma-bhogāya `kāyavyūhaṃ’ samācaret|
asmin kāle mahāyogī pañcadhādhāraṇañcaret||
yena bhūcarasiddhiḥ syāt tatdbhūta-bhayāpaham|
ādhāre ghaṭikāḥ pañca nābhibhrūmadhyage tathā||
bhrūmadhyorddhve tathā pañca ghaṭikādhārayedvudhaḥ|
tadā bhuvādinā naṣṭoyogīndrona bhavet khalu||
medhāvī pañcabhūtānāṃ dhāraṇaṃ yaḥ samācaret|
śata brahmagatenāpi mṛtyustasya na vidyate||
tato'bhyāsakrameṇaiva niṣpattiryogināmbhavet|
anādi karmmavījāni yena cchitvāmṛtambhavet||
yadā niṣpattisampannaḥ samādhiḥ sveṣṭayā bhavet|
jīvanmuktasyamāsāttu bhaveddhīrasya yoginaḥ||
gṛhītvā cetanā vāyuṃ kriyāśaktiñca vegavāna|
sarbbāṃścakrānvibhidyāśu jñānaśaktau vilīyate"||
`trikūṭaṃ karmmaṇaḥ’ ityāderayamarthaḥ| kānicit karmmāṇi kevala svargopabhogyāni| kānicit punaravīcyādinarakopabhogyānīti

P.217
karmmaṇāṃ trikūṭatvam| tadbhogārthaṃ svasyaiśvaryyānubhavārthaṃ ca yogī kāyavyūhaṃ samācaret|
tatprakriyā yathā pātañjale, --
"sākṣātkṛta tattvasya yogino yugapat karmmaphalopamogāyānimādyaṣṭaiśvaryyānubhavāyaca yugapat nirmmiteṣu kāyeṣu trittāni mūlakāraṇāsmitā mātrā prabhavanti| āgneriva sphuliṅgāḥ teṣāñca cittānāṃ yogābhyāsakālona-mūlabhūtaikacittaprayojayatvāttadanusāritvam| teṣāñcajanmādisiddhiprabhavacittavilakṣaṇatvahetuṃ karmmavāsanārāhityaṃ coktvā yoginaśca phalatyāgānusandhānakṛtatvādanyakarmmavilakṣaṇamaśuklākṛṣṇa karmmoktāṃ| karmmaṇaśca phalatyāgānusandhānenānuṣṭhānāt phalānārambham, sahasrajanmāntarita prācīna jñānavāsanāyā-api tattaccharīra grahaṇe anubhūtasmṛtyādi phalotpādanena ānantaryyavāsanānābhanāditvam, anāditve'pikrameṇānubhavarāgāvidyāmūlatvāt, jñānayogābhyāṃ vā vidyāyādagdhakalpatvena nirmmūlatvāt karmmaṇāṃ sutyajatvaṃ| nanu bhogekṣayenaiva karmmakṣayaścettadā prāyaścitta pratipādakaśruti-smṛtītihāsa purāṇādikabhapramāṇaṃsyāditicenna, karmma vaicitryāt| tathāhi santi tāvat sarvagrāṇināṃ karmmāṇi kānicit kṛcchracāndrāyaṇādi dehānta prāyaścittanāśyāni, kānicitt bhogaikanāsyāni; tatra yāni bhogaikanāśyāni tad bhogārthaṃ yoginā kāyabyūhaḥ karttavya eva| yacca "mābhuktaṃkṣīyate karmma" ityādi vākyaṃ tad bhogaikanāśya prārabdhakarmmaviṣayam, akṛtaprāyaścittānāṃ prāṇināṃ na bhogaṃ vinā karmmakṣīyata ityetadviṣayaṃ vā jñeyam| anyathāprāyaścittapratipādakānāṃ śrutismṛtītihāsapurāṇānāmaprāmāṇyaṃ durbbāraṃ

P.218
syāditidik| etāścāvasthāstāmasarājasasāttvikākhyamṛdumadhyādhimātrādhimātratarādhikāri-bhedenavahukālenālpakālenavā bhavanti|
tathācāmṛtasiddhau,--
"vyādhitādurvbalā vṛddhau (+) niḥsatvāgṛhavāsinaḥ|
mandotmāhā mandavīryyā jñātavyā mṛdavonarāḥ||
dvādaśabhirvarṣai reṣāmekāvasthāprasiddhvayati|
nīti prauḍhāḥ samābhyāsāḥ savīryyāḥ samabudvayaḥ||
madhyasthā yogamārgeṣu tathā madhyama yogataḥ|
madhyodsāho madhyarāgā jñātabyā madhyavikramāḥ||
aṣṭābhistu varṣai reṣāmekāṃvasthā prasiddhyati|
bīryyavantodayāvantaḥ kṣamāvanto mahāśayāḥ||
svasthāne saṃsthitāḥ svasthābhaveyuḥ sthirabūddhayaḥ|
svākṣarāśca sadābhyāsāḥ sadā satkāra saṃyutāḥ||
jñātavyāḥ puṇyakarmmāṇo hyadhimātrāhiyoginaḥ|
ekāvasthādhimātrāṇāṃ ṣaḍa़bhirvarṣeḥ prasiddhāti||
mahābalā mahākāyā mahāvoryyāmahāsvanāḥ|
mahāmohojbhkitāḥ śāntā mahākāruṇikānarāḥ||
sarvvaśāstrakṛtābhyāsāḥ sarbbaṃ lakṣaṇabhūṣitāḥ|
sarvvajña sadṛśākārāḥ sarbbavyādhi-vivarjjitāḥ||
barayaubana-sampannā nirbbikārā narottamāḥ|
nirmmalāśca nirātaṅkā nirvvighrāśca nirākulāḥ||
janmāntara kṛtabhyāsā gotravanto mahāśayāḥ|
tārayantica sattvāni taranti svayameva ca ||

P.119
adhimātratarāḥ sattvā jñātavyāḥ sarbbalakṣaṇāḥ|
tribhiḥ samvatsarai reṣā mavasthaikā prasyiddhvati||(+)
avasthābhiśca tisṛbhi ryogaḥ sampūrṇatāṃ brajet"||
yogaśāstre, --
dvārāṇāṃ narakaṃ niruddhvaya marutaṃ pītvā dṛḍha़ṃ dhāritam, 
nītvākāśamapānavahni sahitaṃ śaktyā samuccālitam|
ātmadhyānayutaṃ tvanena vidhinā vinyasya mūddhri(+)sthitam,
yāvattiṣṭati tāvadevamarutāṃ saṅgenasambhūyate||
nābhau saṃyamyacittaṃ pavanagatimadhaḥ saṃniruddhvaya prayatnāt,
ākuñcyāpānamūlaṃ hutavaha saśiṣaṃ tattuyat sūkṣmarūpam|
tannītvā hṛtsaroje tadanuca golake tāluni brahmarandhre’
bhitvā śūnyātiśūnyaṃ praviśati gaganaṃ yatra devomaheśaḥ||

vāsiṣṭhe, --
"pūryyaṣṭakāparākhyasya jīvasya prāṇanātmakām|
viddhvi kuṇḍalinīmanta-rāmodasyeva mañjarīm||
śaktiṃ tāṃ pūrakābhyāsādāpūryyasthīyatesamam|
tadaiti bhairavaṃ sthairyyaṃ kāyasyapīnatā tathā||
yadā pūraka-pūrṇāntarāyatva prāṇamārutam|
nīyate sambidevorddhva soḍha़ṃ dharmmaśramakramam||
sarpivattvaritaivorddhaṃ yāti daṇḍopamāṃgatāḥ|
nāḍa़īḥ sarbvāḥ samādāya dehabaddhā latopamāḥ||

P.220
tadā samastamevedaṃ āplāvayati dehakam|

P.221
atha pratyāhāra-nirūpaṇam|
tatra pratyāhāra svarupaṃ phalañcāha patañjaliḥ, -- "sva (sva) viṣaya samprayoge cittasvarūpānukāraiventriyāṇāṃ pratyāhāraḥ" (1) "tataḥ paramāvaśyatendriyāṇāṃ iti sūtrābhyām| etadarthastu, -- indriyāṇi sva sva viṣayebhyaḥ pratisamāhriyante'sminniti bratyāhāraḥ| sakathaṃ niṣpādyata ityata āhaca kṣurādīnāmindriyāṇāṃ viṣayorupādistena saṃyoga stadābhigukhyena varttanaṃ tadekabhāva stadābhimukhaṃ parityajya svarūpamātreivasthānam| tasmin sati cittamātrānukārīṇi bhavanti, tataścittamanuvarttamānāni madhukararājamiva makṣikāḥ sarvāṇīndriyāṇi prayānti| ataścitta nirodhena tāni pratyāhṛtāni bhavanti| tata eva mabhyasyamāne pratyāhāre tathāvaśyāni āyattāni mampādyante yathā vāhyābhimukhaṃ nīyamānānyapi nayāntītyarthaḥ|
śivagītāyām, --
"indriyāṇāṃ vicaratāṃ viṣayeṣu svabhāvataḥ|
nigrahaḥ procyate sadbhiḥ pratyāhāraḥ sa utyate"||

P.222

yogaśāstre, --
"candrāmṛtamayīndhārāṃ pratyāharati bhāskaraḥ|
yatpratyāharaṇantasyāḥ pratyāhāraḥ sa ucyate||
yathā tṛtīya kālastho raviḥ pratyāharet prabhām|
tṛtīyāṃśasthitoyogī vikāraṃ mānasantathā||
yājñabalkyaḥ, --
"indriyāṇāṃ vicaratāṃ viṣayeṣu svabhāvataḥ|
balādāharaṇanteṣāṃ pratyāhāraḥ sa ucyate||
yadyat paśyati tat sarvaṃ paśyedātmavadātmani|
pratyāhāraḥ sacaprokto yogavidbhirmahātmabhiḥ||
karmmāṇi yāni nityāni vihitāni śarīriṇām|
teṣāmātmanyanuṣṭhānaṃ manasāyadvahirvinā||
pratyāhāro bhavet so'pi yogasādhanamuttamam|
pratyāhāre praśastaṃ tat sevitaṃ yogibhiḥ sadā||
aṣṭādaśastu yadvāyormmarmmasthāneṣu dhāraṇam|
sthānāt sthānāt samākṛṣya pratyāhāronigadyate||
aśvinautu tathā būtāṃ gārgi! devamiṣagvarī|
marmma sthānāni sirddhyarthaṃ śarīre yogamokṣayoḥ||
tāni sarvāṇi vakṣyāmi yathāvacchṛṇu suvrate !|
pādāṅguṣṭheca gulpheca jaṅghā savye tathaivaca||
citte mūleca jānvośca madhye corudvayasyaca|
pāyumūlantataḥ paścāt madhye dehañcameḍhrakam||

nābhiśca hṛdayaṃ gārgi ! kaṇṭhakūpastathaivaca|

etattuyogasiddhvayartha magastyenāpikīrttitam||
pratyāhāreṣu sarveṣu praśastamiti yogibhiḥ|
nāḍa़ībhyāṃ vāyumāropya kuṇḍalyāpārśvayorṇaraḥ||
dhārayedrāgayukto'pi bhavarogād vimucyate|
pūrvavad vāyumāropya hṛdaye dyomni dhārayet||
-------------------------------------------------------
P.225

so'pi yāti varārohe paramātmapadaṃ naraḥ|
vyādhayaḥ kiṃ punastasya vāhyābhyantaravarttinaḥ||
nāsābhyāṃ vāyumāropya pūrayitvodaraṃ sthitaḥ|
bhruvormadhyāddṛśoḥ paccāt samāropya samāhitaḥ||

dhārayet kṣaṇamātraṃ yaḥ so'pi yāti parāṃ gatim|
kiṃ punarvahunoktena nityakarmma samācara||
ātmanaḥ prāṇamāropya bhruvormadhyāt suṣumnayā|
yāvat manolayastasmin tāvat saṃyamanaṃ kuru||

dattātreyastu –
indriyānīndriyorthebhyo yaḥ pratyāharati sphūṭam|
yogīkumbhakamāsthāya pratyāhāraḥ sa ucyate||
yaṃ yaṃ paśyati cakṣurbhyāṃ taṃ tamātmani bhāvayet|
yaṃ yaṃ śrṛṇoti karṇābhyāṃ taṃ tamātmani bhāvayet||

jihayā yadrasati tat tadātmani bhāvayet|
tvacā yat spruśate yogī tat tadātmani bhāvayet||
evaṃ jñānendriyāṇāṃ yat tat tadātmani bhāvayet|
yāmamācaṃ (+) pratidinaṃ yogī yatnādatandritaḥ||
------------------------------------------------------------
P.226
tadāvicintya sāmarthyaṃ yogino jāyate dhruvam|
dūraśrutirdūradṛṣṭiḥ kṣaṇaṃ dūragatistathā||
vāksiddhiḥ kāmacāritvaṃ adṛśyakaraṇaṃ tathā|| iti

iti śrīmat paramahaṃsa paribrājakācāryya śrīrāmacandra sadānandasarasvatīśṣyi śivānandasarasvatī viracita—
yogacintāmaṇī tṛtīyaparicchedaḥ|
-------
P.227
atha dhāraṇā nirupyante|
tatra lakṣaṇamāha patañcaliḥ –
"deśavandhaścittasya dhāraṇeti sūtreṇa (1) asyārthaḥ –
deśe ādhyamātmikenābhicakrahṛdayakaṇṭhamukha-bhrūmadhyamṛrddhvādisthāne vāhyenāsādau cittasya bandho viṣayāntara parihāreṇa yaccittasyasthāpanaṃ sā dhāraṇetyucyate| ayaṃ bhāvaḥ maitrādirivāsitāntaḥ – karaṇena yamaniyamavatā jitāsanaparivṛtaprāṇacikṣepeṇa pratyāhṛtendriyagrāmeṇa ṛjukāyena jitadandvena yoginā nirvvādhepradeśe saṃprajñāta(2) samādherabhyāsāya nāsagrādau cetaḥ ritharīkarttavyamiti yathoktaṃ viṣṇupurāṇe—
"prāṇāyāmena pavanaṃ pratyāhāreṇa cendriyam|
vaśīkṛtya tataḥ kuryyāccittavṛttiṃ śubhāśaye"||
śivagītāyām –
"hṛt puṇḍarīke nāsāgre mūrddhi (+) sarvatrasambhave|
evamādiṣu deśeṣu dhāraṇā citta bandhanam"||
---------------------------------------------------------------------
P.228
skandapurāṇe –
"āsanenasamāyuktaḥ prāṇāyāmena saṃyutaḥ|
pratyāhāreṇa sampranno dhāraṇāñca samabhyaset||
hṛdaye pañcabhūtānāṃ dhāraṇaṃ yat pṛthak pṛthak|
manasoniścalatvena dhāraṇā sā vidhīyate||
haritālanibhāṃ bhūmiṃ salakārāṃ savedhasāṃ|
catuṣkoṇāṃ hṛdidhyāyet yayāsyāt kṣitidhāraṇā||
kaṇṭhendu tattvamūrddhvandunibhaṃviṣṇusamanvitam|
vakāra vījaṃ kundābhaṃ dhyāyannamvujayediti||
tālusthamindragopābhaṃ trikoṇaṃ rephasaṃyutam|
rudreṇādhiṣṭhitaṃ samyak dhyātvā vahniṃ jayedityi(+)
vāyutattvaṃ bhrūvormadhye vṛttamañjanasannibhaṃ|
yaṃ vījamīśadaivatyaṃ dhyāyan vāyuṃ jayediti"||
"ākāśaṃ ca viśuddha vārisadṛśaṃ yad brahmarandhrasthitam,
tannāthena sadāśivena sahitaṃ sāntaṃ hakārākṣaram||
prāṇaṃ tatravinīya pañcaghaṭikāścittānvitaṃ dhārayet,
eṣā mokṣakapāṭapāṭana paṭuḥ proktānabhodhāraṇā||"
"stambhanī plāvanī caiva dahanī bhrāmaṇī tathā|
śamanīca mavantyetā bhūtānāṃ pañcadhāraṇāḥ||"
yājñavalkyaḥ—
yamādi guṇayuktasya manasaḥ sthitirātamani|
dhāraṇetyucyate sadbhiḥ śāstratrātparyyavedibhiḥ||
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
P.229
svasmin brahmapure gārgi yadidaṃ hṛdayāmvujaṃ|
tasminnevāntarākāśe yadvāhyākāśa dhāraṇam||
eṣāca dhāraṇetyuktā yogaśāstra viśāradaiḥ|
cintakairyogaśāśtrajñairbiddhadbhiśca suśaṅkitaiḥ||
dhāraṇāḥ pañcadhā proktāstāśca sarvvāḥ pṛthak pṛthak|
bhūmirāpastathā tejo vāyurākāśa mevaca||
eteṣu yad devānāṃ vai dhāraṇaṃ pañcadheṣyate|
pādādiūnu(+) paryyantaṃ pṛthivī sthānamucyate||
ājānoḥ pāyū paryyantamapāṃ sthānaṃ prakīrttitam|
āpāyorhṛ(+)dayāntaṃ yad vahnisthānaṃ taducyate||
hṛ(+)nmadhyāttu bhrūvormmadhyaṃ yāvadvāyukulaṃ smṛtam|
ābhrūmadhyāttumūrddhānta mākāśamiticocyate||
pṛthivyāṃ dhārayet gārgi ! brahmāṇaṃ parameṣṭhinam|
viṣṇu mākhanile rudra mīśvaraṃ vāyumaṇḍale||
sadāśivaṃ tadā vyomni dhārayettu samāhitaḥ|
pṛthivyāṃ vāyumāropya lakāreṇasamanvitam||
dhyāyaṃ ścarmmukhākāraṃ brahmāṇaṃ sṛṣṭikāraṇam|
dhārayet pañcaghaṭikāḥ pṛthivī jayamāpnuyāt||
vāruṇe vāyumāropya vakāreṇa samanvitam|
smaraṇ nārāyaṇaṃ devaṃ caturvbāhuṃ(+) śucistritam||
śuddha sphaṭikasaṃkāśaṃ potavāsasamacyutam|
dhārayet pañcaghaṭikāḥ sarvvarogaiḥ pramucyate|
vahnāvanilamāropya rephākṣara samanvitam||
---------------------------------------
P.230
tryakṣaṃ varapradaṃ rudraṃ taruṇādityasannibham|
bhasmodghalita sarvvāṅgaṃ suprasannamanusmaraṇ||
dhārayed ghaṭikāḥ pañcavahninā'saunadahyate|
mārutaṃ marutāṃ sthāne varṇadeva samanvite||
dhārayet pañcaghaṭikā vāyuścavyomago bhavet|
ākāśe vāyumāropya hakāropari śaṅkharam||
vindurūpaṃ mahādevaṃ vyomākāraṃ sadāśivam|
śuddha sphaṭika saṃkāśaṃ vālendu dhṛtaśekharam||
pañcavakttrayutaṃ śāntaṃ daśavāhuṃ trilocanam|
sarvvāyudhodyatakaraṃ sarvvābharaṇa bhūṣitam|
umārddhadehaṃ varadaṃ sarvvakāraṇa kāraṇam|
manasā cintayan yastu muhūrtta mapidhārayet||
sa eva mu(yu)kta stāntrikai rathavāyogaśikṣitaiḥ|
etaduktaṃ bhavatyatra gārgi! brahmavidāmbare! |
brahmādi kāryyarupāṇi sve sve saṃhṛtyakāraṇe|
tasvin sadāśive prāṇaṃ cittaṃvā nijakāraṇe||
yuktacittaḥ sadātmānaṃ yuyoja parameśvare|
asminnarthe vadantyanye yogino brahmavidvarāḥ|
praṇavenatu kāryyaṇi sve sve saṃhṛtya kāraṇe||
asmin sadāśive prāṇaṃ cittaṃ vā nijakāraṇe|
praṇavasya tu nādānte paramānanda vigrahe||
"ṛtaṃ satyaṃ paraṃ vrahma puruṣaṃ kṛṣṇapiṅgalam (1) 
cetasā saṃprapaśyanti santaḥ saṃsāra-bheṣajam||

P. 231
tvaṃ tasmāt praṇavenaiva prāṇāyāmaistribhistribhiḥ|
brahmādi-kāryyarūpāṇi sve sve saṃhṛtyakāraṇe||
viśuddha-cetasā paśyanādānte parameśvaram|
asminnarthe vadantyanye yogino brahmabidvarāḥ||
bhiṣagvarā varārohe yogeṣu pariniṣṭhitāḥ|
śarīraṃ tāvadevedaṃ (hi) pañcabhūtātmakaṃ khalu||
bhavettatra varārohe vāta-pitta-kaphātmakam|
vātātmakānāṃ sarvveṣāṃ yogeṣuniyatātmanām||
prāṇasaṃyamanenaiva śoṣayanti kalevaram|
pittātmakānāmacirānnaśuṣyati kalevaram||
kaphātmakānāṃ kāryyantu samprūrṇamacirād bhavet|
dhāraṇaṃ kurvvatastvagnau sarvve naśyanti vātajāḥ|
pārthivāṃśe jalāṃśe ca dhārarṇaṃ kurvvataḥ sadā|
tridoṣajanitā rogā vinaśyanti na saṃśayaḥ||
asminnarthe tathāvrūtā maśvinau ca bhiṣagvarau|
prāṇasaṃthamanenaiva tridoṣa śamanaṃ nṝṇām||"
dattātreyaḥ,--
"bhavyānarthānanarthān vai jñātvā rahasi yajataḥ (+)
pañcadhā dhāraṇaṃ kuryyāt tattadbhūtabhayāpaham||
nābheradho gudasyorddhaṃ ghaṭikāḥ pañca dhārayet|
vāyuṃ tatobhaveṃt pṛthvī dhāraṇañcedbhayāpaham||
pṛthivīsambhavo mṛtyurnabhavettasya yoginaḥ|
nābhisthāne tatovāyuṃ dhārayet pañca nāḍa़ikāḥ||

P.232
tato jalādbhayaṃ nāsti jale mṛtyurnayoginaḥ|
nābhyurddhvaṃ(+) maṇḍale vāyuṃ dhārayet pañcanāḍa़ikāḥ||
āgneyī dhāraṇāceyaṃ na mṛtyu stasya vahninā|
nadahyate śarīrañca praviṣṭecāgni saṃstare||
nābhibhrūghrāṇamadhye ca prādeśatrayasanmite|
dhārayet pañcaghaṭikā vāyuṃ saiṣāca vāyavī||
dhāraṇenaiva vāyostu bhayaṃ bhavati yoginaḥ|
bhrū madhyādupariṣṭāttu dhārayet pañcanāḍa़ikāḥ||
vāyuṃ yogī prayatnena seya mākāśa dhāraṇā|
ākāśa dhāraṇāṃ kurvvan mṛtyuṃ jayati śāśvattam||
evañca dhāraṇāḥ pañca kuryyādyogī vicakṣaṇaḥ|
tatodṛḍha़ śarīrasya mṛtyu stasya na vidyate||
evaṃ pañcavidhābhūta-dhāraṇā yaḥ samabhyaset|
brahmaṇaḥ pralayenāpi tasyamṛtyurnavidyate"||
mārkaṇḍeyapurāṇe,--
"yogayuktaḥ sadāyogī laghā(+)hārojitendriyaḥ|
sūkṣmāstu dhāraṇāḥ sapta bhūrādyā mūrddhiva dhārayet||
dharitrīṃ dhārayed yogī tat saukṣmyaṃ pratipadyate|
ātmānaṃ manyate corbbīṃ tadgandhañca jahāti saḥ||
tathaivāśūrasaṃsūkṣmaṃ tadbadrūpañca cetasi|
sparśaṃ vāyostathā tadvaddhimrata stasyadhāraṇām||
vyomnaḥ sūkṣmāṃ prabṛttiñca śabdaṃ tadvajjahātica|
manasā sarbba bhūtānāṃ manasyāviśate yadā||

P.233
"mānasīṃ dhāraṇāṃ vibhranmanaḥ saukṣmañca jāyate|
tadbadvuddhimaśeṣāṇāṃ satvānāmetya yogavit||
parityajanti saṃprāpya vuddhi saukṣmamanuttamam|
parityajantisūkṣmāṇi saptatvetāni yogavit||
samyag vijñāyayo'larka ! tasyāvṛttirnavidyate|
etāsāṃ-dhāraṇānāntu saptānāṃ saukṣmamātmavān||
dṛṣṭvā dṛṣṭvā tataḥ siddhiṃ tyaktavā tyaktvāparāmbrajet|
yasmin yasmincakurute bhūte rāgaṃ mahīyate||
tasmin tatmin samāsaktiṃ saṃprāpya savinaśyati|
tasmādviditvā sūkṣmāṇi saṃsaktāni parasparam||
parityajati yo dehī saparaṃ prāpnuyāt padam|
etānyeva tu bandhāya sapta sūkṣmāṇi (+) pārthiva||
bhūrādīnāṃ virāgo'tra sadbhavāttasya muktaye|
gandhādiṣu samāsakta mityetadakhilaṃ jagat||
punarāvṛttaye bhūpa sabrahmamaramānuṣam|
saptaitā dhāraṇāyogī mamatītya yadicchati||
tasmiṃstasmiṃllayaṃ sūkṣme yāti bhūte nareśvara|
devānāṃ sugaṇānāñca gandharbboraga sakṣasāṃ||
deheṣu laya māpnoti saṅgaṃ nāpnoti ca kvicit|
tathā –
prāgnābhyāṃ hṛdaye cātra tṛtīyāca tathorasi|
kaṇṭhe mukhe nāsikāgre netra bhramadhya mūrddhaṣu||
kiñcittasmāt parasmin sā dhāraṇā paramānmutā|(+)
daśaitā dhāraṇāṃ prāpya prāghnotyakṣara sāmyatām"||

P.234
athadhyānam, tallakṣaṇa māha patañcaliḥ –
tatra pratyaikatānatā dhyānamāha dhyānamiti, tatra yaddhāraṇāyā mālambanīkṛtaṃ vastu tasmin pratyayaikatānatā pratyayasya cittavṛtte-rvijātīya pratyaya tiraskāreṇa sajātīha pratyaya pravāhīkaraṇaṃ yat taddhvayāna mityucyate|
śiva gītāyāṃ –
"deśāvisthitimālambya vuddhe ryāvṛtti santatiḥ|
kṛtyantarairasaṃsprṛṣṭā taddhyānaṃ sūrayo viduḥ|"
viṣṇupurāṇe –
"taddhṛti pratyayaikāgra santati ryānyaniṣpṛhā|
taddhyānaṃ prathamaiḥ ṣaḍbhi raṅgairniṣpādyate nṛpa"||
skandapurāṇe—
"dhyai" cintāyāṃ smṛto dhātuścintātattveṣu niścalā|
yātaddhyānamiti proktaṃ saguṇaṃ nirguṇaṃ dvidhā||
saguṇaṃ sarvva bhedena nirguṇaṃ kevalaṃ matam|
samantraṃ saguṇaṃ viddhi nirguṇuṃ mantra varjitam||
śabdādīnāñca tanmātrā yāvat karmmādiṣu sthitāḥ|
tāvadeva smṛtaṃ dhyānaṃ syāt samādhirataḥ param||
nāśvamedhena tat puṇyaṃ naca bairājasūyataḥ|
tat puṇyameka dhyānena labhet yogīsthirāsanaḥ"||

P.235
mārkaṇḍeya purāṇe—
tyaktasaṅgojitakrodho laghvāhārau jitendriyaḥ|
pidhāya vuddhyādvārāṇi mano dhyāne niveśayet||
"śūnye vātha prakāśeṣu guhāsuca vaneṣuca|
nityayuktaḥ sadāyogī dhyānaṃ samyagupakramet"||
"vedāḥ śreṣṭhāḥ sarvvayajña kriyāṣu
yajñāj japyaṃ jñānamāhuśca japyāt"
"jñānāddhyānaṃ sarvvarāgavyapetaṃ
tasmin siddhe śāśvatasyopalavdhiḥ,
samāhitobrahmaparo'pramādī
śuci stanvaikāntaratirjitendriyaḥ|
saḥ prāpnuyāt vyomamimaṃ mahātmā, 
vimukti māpnoti tataśca yogataḥ"||
yājñavalkya—
"dhyānaṃ tataḥ pravakṣyāmi śrṛṇugārgi varānane|
dhyānamevahi jantūnāṃ kāraṇaṃ bandha mokṣayoḥ||
dhyānamātmasvarūpasya vedanaṃ manasākhalu|
saguṇaṃ nirguṇaṃ tacca saguṇaṃ vahuvidā smṛtam||
sūpapannaṃ cottamāniteṣvāhurvaidikā dvijāḥ|
trauṇisukhyatamānyeṣā meka mevahinirguṇaṃ||
marmmasthānāni nāḍa़īnāṃ saṃsthānañca pṛthak pṛthak|
vāyūnāṃ sthānakarmmāṇi jñātvā kurvvātmavedanam||
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
P.236
ekaṃ jyotirmmayaṃ suddhvaṃ sarvvāṅga vyomavaddṛḍha़m|
avyakta mamalaṃ nityaṃ hyādimadhyānta varjitam||
sthala sūkṣya (+) manākāśa masaṃsparśamacākṣuṣam|
"narasaṃ naca gandhāḍhyamaprameyamanaupamam|
ānanda majaraṃ nityaṃ sadasat sarvvakāraṇam||
sarvvāghāraṃ jagadrūpa mamūrtta majamavyayam|
adṛśyaṃ dṛśya madhyasyaṃ vahisthaṃ sarvvatomukham||
sarvvadṛk sarvataḥ pādaṃ sarvaspṛk sarvataḥ śiraḥ|
vrahma brahmamayohaṃsyāmititattvasya vedanat||
tadetannirguṇaṃ dhyāna miti yogavidoviduḥ|
athavā paramātmanāṃ paramānanda vigraham||
gūrūpa deśādvijñāya puruṣaṃ kṛṣṇapiṅgalam|
brahma brahmapure cāsmin (+) daharāñe khamadhyame|| 
cetasā saṃprapaśyanti santaḥ saṃsāra bheṣajam|
hṛt padmāṣṭa dalopete kanda madhyāt samuttyite||
dvādaśāṅgulanālesmiṃ ścaturaṅgula vanmukhe||
prāṇāyāmairvikaśite kesarānvita karṇike|
iṣṭa devaṃ samālocya manasāṃ sarva kāmadam||
sohamātmeti vijñānaṃ saguṇaṃ dhyānamucyate|
hṛtsaroruha madhye'smin prakṛtyādyātmakarpike||
aṣṭaiśvaryya dalopete vidyākesara saṃyute|
jñānanāle vṛhat kande prāṇāyāma prabodhite||
viśvārcciṣaṃ mahāvarhniṃ jvalantaṃ viśvatomukham|
vaiśvānaraṃ jagadyoniṃ śikhātapanta mīśvaram||

P.237
"pratapantaṃ svakaṃ dehaṃ āpādatalamastakam|
nirvvāta dīṃpavatta'smin dīpitaṃ havyavāhanam||
dṛṣṭvā tasya śikhā madhye paramātmāna mavyayam|
nīlatoyadamadhyasthavidyullekheva bhāsvaram||
nīvāra śūkavadrūṣaṃ dīpābhaṃ sarvakāraṇam|
jñātvā vaiśvānaraṃ devaṃ sohamātseti yāmatiḥ||
suguṇa dhyāneṣuttamaṃ dhyānaṃ yogavido viduḥ|
vaiśvānaratvaṃ saṃprāpya muktiṃ tenaiva gacchati||
athavā maṇḍale paśye-dādityasya mahādyuteḥ|
ātmānaṃ jagatohetuṃ pūruṣaṃ hemarūpiṇam||
hiraṇya śmaśrukeśañca hirakmayanakhaṃ harim|
rūpapeśalavaddakt" sṛṣṭisthityantakāriṇam|
padmāsanasthitaṃ saumyaṃ pravuddhāñ nibhānanam|
padmodara dalauṣṭhābhaṃ sarvalokābhayapradam|
jānantaṃ sarvadā sarvaṃ nandayantañca dhārmmikān|
bhāsayantaṃ jagat sarvaṃ dṛṣṭvālokaika sākṣiṇam||
sohamasmīti yāvuddhiḥ sācadhyāne praśasyate|
eṣa evattumokṣasya mahāmāṃrgastapodhane||
dhyānenānena saureṇa muktiṃ yāsyanti sūrayaḥ|
bhruvormmadhye'ntarātmānaṃ bhārūpaṃ sarvakāraṇam|||
sthāṇuvammūrddha paryyantaṃ madhyadehāt samutthitam|
"jagatkāraṇamavyaktaṃ jvalantamamitaujasam||
manasālokya si'haṃsyāmityetaddhvayānamuttamam|
athavābaddha paryyaṅkaḥ śithilīkṛta vigrahaḥ|
śiva eva svayaṃ bhūtvā nāsāgrāropitekṣaṇaḥ||

P.238
mivvikāraṃ śivaṃ śāntaṃ paramātmānamalavyayam|
bhārūpamamṛtaṃ dhyāyedbhruvormaṃdhye varānan|
soha'meveti yāvuddhiḥ sācadhyāne praśasyate|
atha vāṣṭadalopete sakarṇikesarānvite||
unnidrahṛdayāmbhoje somamaṇḍalamadhyame|
svātmānamarbhakākāraṃ bhoktṛrūpiṇamīśvaram|
sudhārasaṃ vimuñcadbhiḥ śaśirasmibhirāvṛtam||
ṣoḍa़śacchadasaṃyuktaṃ śiraḥpadmādadhomukhāt|
nirgatāmṛta dhārābhiḥ sahasrābhiḥ samantataḥ||
plāvitaṃ (+) puruṣantatra cintayitvā samāhitaḥ|
tenāmṛtarasenaiva sāṅgopāṅgakalevaram|
ahameva paraṃ brahma paramātmāhamavyayaḥ||
evaṃ yadvedanaṃ tatra saguṇadhyānamucyate|
evaṃ dhyānāmṛtaṃ karvvan ṣanmāsānmṛtyujidbhavet||
vatsarānmukta evasyājjīvanneva na saṃśayaḥ|
jīvadhmuktasyanakkāpi duḥkhāvāptiḥ kathañcana||
kim punarnityamuktasya muktareva hi durlabhā"|
śrutauśca, --
"hratpuṇḍarīkaṃ virajaṃ viśuddham,
vicintyamadhye viśadaṃ viśokam|
acintyamavyakta manantarūpam,
śivaṃ praśāntaṃ amṛtaṃ brahmayonim||

P.239
tathādi madhyāntavihīna mekam,
vibhuṃ cidānandamarūpamadbhūtam|
umāsahāyaṃ parameśvaraṃ prabhum,
trilocanaṃ nīlakaṇṭhaṃ praśāntam||

dhyātvāmunirgacchati bhūtayonim,
samasta sākṣiṃ tamasaḥ parastāt
tatastutaṃ paśyati niṣkalaṃ dhyāyamānaḥ,
`dṛśyate tvagryayāvuddhyā sūkṣmayā sūkṣmadarśibhiḥ’|
`manasai vaitadāptavyam’ ityādikāḥ||
dattātreyaḥ—
"samabhyasettatodhyānaṃ ghaṭikāḥ ṣaṣṭhimevaca|
vāyuṃ nirūdhya yāṃ kāñciddevatāmiṣṭadāyinīm||
naguṇadhyāna metattu hyaṇimādihuṇapradam|
nirguṇaṃ khamiva dhyātvā mokṣamārgaṃ prapadyate"||
yogiyājñavalkyaḥ, --
"dhyānena sadṛśaṃ nāsti śodhanaṃ pāpakarmmaṇām|
śvapākeṣvapi bhuñjāno dhyāyī naivatu lipyate||
"dhyānameva paraṃ vrahma dhyānameva parantapaḥ|
dhyānameva paraṃ śaucaṃ taddhyānaṃ paramaṃ padam||
sarvvapāpa prasakto'pi dhyāyennimiṣamacyutam|
tadātapasvī bhavati paṅkti pāvana pāvanaḥ"||
P.240
mahābhārate, --
sarvvācāra vihīno'pi yuktovā sarvvapātakaiḥ|
sarvaṃ tudati tatpāpaṃ bhāvayaṃ śchivamātmanā||
śāstrāntare, --
"pātakeṣuca sarveṣu pātakeṣu mahatsuca|
praviśya rajanī pādaṃ brahmadhyānaṃ samācaret||"
bāśiṣṭhe –
ātmapūjāprakaraṇe dhyānasvarūpakathanānantaram|
īśvara uvāca,--
dhyāne'sya para evātmā dhyānamasya mahārccanam|
vinātene tarenāyaṃ ātmālabhyata evano||
dhyānenānena sumate nimeṣāṃstu trayodaśa|
mūḍha़o'pi pūjayitveśaṃ gopradāna phalaṃ labhet||
pūjayitvānimeṣāṇāṃ śatameka miti prabhum|
aśvamedha sahasrasya phalamāpnoti mānavaḥ||
dhyānavalyupahāreṇa srayamātmānamātmanā|
ghaṭikāṃ pūjayed yastu rājasūyaṃ labheta saḥ||
madhyāhna pūjanāditthaṃ rājasūyaikalakṣabhāk|
divasaṃ pūjayitvaiṃvaṃ paredhāmnivasennaraḥ||
eṣo'sauparamoyoga iyaṃ vai paramā kriyā|

P.241
ātmagītāyām, --
"sākṣāt karttumaśakto'pi cintayenmāmaśaṅkitaḥ|(+)
kālenānubhavāruḍha़o bhavet sakalito dhruvam||
yathāgādhanidhirlābhenopāyaḥ khananaṃ vinā|
mallobhe'pi tathā svātmacintāṃ muktvānacāparaḥ||
dehaṃ hyupalamākṛṣya buddhikuddālakāt punaḥ|
khātvāmanobhuvaṃ bhūyo gṛhṇīyānmāṃ nidhiṃ pumān"||

śāstrāntareca, --
anubhūte rabhāveṃ'pi brahmāsmītyeva cintyatām|
apyasat prāpyate dhyānānnityāptaṃ brahma kiṃ punaḥ||
anātmavuddhi śaithilyaṃ phalaṃ dhyānāddine dine|
paśyannapi naceddhyāyet ko'parohyanyathāvadet||
upekṣya tat tīrthayātrāṃ japādīneva kurvatām|
piṇḍaṃ samutsṛjya karaṃleḍha़īti nyāyaāpatet||

atha samādhiḥ|
tatra patañcaliḥ –
"tadevārtha mātranirbhāsaṃsvarupaśūnya mivasamādhiḥ| "
yaddhyānāvalambanīkṛtaṃ vastu tadeva paramārthamātra nirbhāsaṃ
svasvarupenaiva niḥsandigdhaṃ sarvātmanā paripūrṇarūpam,
ataeva sarvaviṣaya vihīnatvāt svarupaśūnyamiva yasyāṃ
bhāvanāyāṃ bhāsatai sa bhāvanāviśeṣaḥ samādhirityucyate|
samādhidvaya sāmānyalakṣaṇa metat| viśeṣa lakṣaṇaṃ prathama pari-

P.242
ccheda eva vistareṇoktvaṃdraṣṭavyam| anyetu (1)samāgādhīyate ekāgrīkriyate vikṣepān parityajya yatra manaḥ samādhi rityāhuḥ"|
yājñavalkyaḥ, –
"samādhim adhunā vakṣye bhavapāśavināśanam|
bhavapāśanibaddhasya yathāvacchrotum arhasi||
samādhistu samāvasthā jīvātmaparamātmanoḥ|
brahmaṇyevasthitiryā sā samādhiḥ pratyagātmanaḥ|
dhyāyed yathā yathātmānaṃ syāt samādhis tathā tathā|
dhyatvaivātmani saṃsthāto nānyathātmā yato bhavet||
evamevahi sarvatraṃ yat prayatnastu yonaraḥ|
tadātmā bhinna evātra samādhiṃ samavāpnuyāt||"

kūrmmapurāṇe—
"ekākāraḥ samādhiḥ syāddeśīlambana varjitaḥ|
pratyayohyarthamātreṇa yoga sādhana muttamam||"

viṣṇupurāṇe –
tasyaiva kalpanāhīnaṃ svarupa grahaṇāya sat|
manasā dhyāna niṣpādyaṃ samādhiḥ so'bhidhīyate||
naca dhyāna saṃprajñāta samādhyorabhedaḥ śaṅkanīyaḥ
vahu prayatna sādhye'pi dhyānebhavet pratyayāntara sañcāraḥ
saṃprajñāta samādhautu sarvathaivanāstyeva pratyayāntarāvakāśa iti tayorbhedaḥ|

P.243
sureśvarācārthyāḥ –
"brahmaviṣṇu śivādīnāṃ cintādhyānaṃ pravakṣyate|
dhyānādaspandanaṃ vuddheḥ samādhirabhidhīte"||

śaivadharmme –
samādhistu samāvasthā jīvātma-paramātmanoḥ|
nistaraṅgapadaprāptiḥ paramānandarupiṇī||
"niḥśvāsocchrāsa yuktāvā niḥspando spandalocanaḥ|
śivadhyāyī sulonaśca sa samādhistha ucyate||

"sarityato niviṣṭāmbu yathā'bhinnaṃ layaṃ vrajet|
tathātmā'bhinna evātra samādhiṃ samavāvruyāt||
na śrṛṇoti yadā kiñcinna paśyati na jighrati|
naca sparśaṃ vijānāti sa samādhistha ucyate||"

skanda purāṇe....
"yat samatvaṃ dvayo ratra jīvātma paramātmanoḥ|
sanaṣṭa sarvamaṅkalpaḥ samādhirabhidhīyate||
nātmānaṃ na paraṃ vetti na śītaṃ noṣṇamevaca|
samādhiyukto yogīndro na sukhaṃ na mukhetaram||
kalpate naivakālena lipyate naiva karmmaṇā|
bhidyate naiva śastrādyairyogī yuktaḥ samādhinā||
yathājale jalaṃ kṣiptaṃ jalamevattu tad bhavet|
kṣīre kṣīraṃ tathā yogī tatra tanmayatāṃ vrajet||"

P.244
dattātraiyaḥ –
nirguṇadhyānasampannaḥ samādhiñca tato'bhyaset|
dinena dvādaśenaiva sasādhiṃ samavāpnuyāt||
vāyuṃ niruddhya medhāvī jīvanmukto bhaved dhruvam|
samādhiḥ samatāvasthā jīvātma paramātmanoḥ||
yadi syadeha mutsraṣṭuṃ iccheccedutsṛjet svayam|
pare brahmaṇi līyeta tyaktvākarmma śubhāsubham||
atha nocet samutsraṣṭuṃ khaśarīraṃ yadi prayam|
sarbalokeṣu bicaret aṇimādi guṇānvitaḥ||
evañcoktarītyā sāṅgaṃ yogamanutiṣṭhato yoginaḥ sāttvika-rājasatāmasā antarāyā bhavanti| sarve'pite tucchā, -- iti matvā yoginā dveyā eva|

tathāca dattātreyaḥ –
"prathamābhyāsakāle'pi vighnāstu vividhā mune|
ālasyaṃ prathamo vighnaḥ dvitīyastu prakathyate||
pūrvokta dhūrtta goṣṭhīca tṛtīyā mantra sādhanam|
caturtho dhātuvādaḥ syāt iti yogavido viduḥ||
kandarpasya yathārupaṃ tathā tasyaiva-yoginaḥ|
tadrūpavaraṇā (varākā) nāryyaḥ kāṅkṣante tasya saṃgamaṃ||
evaṃ hi vahavo vighnāḥ mṛgatṛṇṇā samā mune|
sthirasyāsya prajāyante tānjñātvā santyajet sudhīḥ||" iti

P.245
mārkaṇḍeya purāṇe, --
"upasargāḥ pravarttante dṛṣṭehyātmani yoginaḥ|
ye tāṃstu saṃpravakṣyāmi samāsena nibodhame||
kāmyāḥ kriyā stathākāmān mānuṣyānabhikāṅkṣati|
striyodāna phalaṃ vidyāṃ māyāṃ kūpyaṃ dhanaṃ vasu||
devatva mamareśatvaṃ rasāyana vayaḥkriyām|
meru pratapanaṃ yajñaṃ jalāgnyāveśanantathā||
śrāddhānāṃ śaktidānāṃñca phalāni niyamāstathā|
tathopavāsāt pūrttācca devateṣvarccanādapi||
tebhyastebhyaśca karmmabhya upasṛṣṭo'bhivācchati|
vittamittyaṃ pravarttitaṃ yatnāt yogī nivarttayet|
brahyasaṅgī manaḥ kurvvan upasargaiḥ pravarttate (pramucyate)
upasargrairjitairebhi rupasargāstataḥ punaḥ|
yoginaḥ saṃpravarttante sattvarājasa tāmasāḥ||
prātibhaḥ śrābaṇo devau bhramāvarttau tathāparau|
pañcaite yogināṃ yogavighnāya kaṭukodayāḥ|
vedārthā kāvyaśāstrārthāḥ vighnaśilpānyaśeṣataḥ||
pratibhānti yadasyeti prātibhaḥ satuyoginaḥ|
śabdārthā nakhilān vetti śabdaṃ gṛhnāti caivayat|
yojanānāṃ sahasrebhyaḥ śrāvaṇaṃ so'bhidhīyate||
aṣṭauyadātu dṛśyante samantāddevayonayaḥ|
upasargaṃ tamapyāhu daivamunmattavadvudhāḥ||
bhrāmyate yannirālambaṃ manodoṣeṇa yoginaḥ|
samastādhāra vibhraṃśād bhramaḥ saparikīrttitaḥ||

P.246
āvartta dravatoyasya jñānāvartto yadākulaḥ||
cittaṃ nāśayate yatra upasargaḥ sa ucyate||
etairnāśita yogāstu sakalā devayonayaḥ|
upasargai mahāghorai rābarttante punaḥ punaḥ||
prāvarttya kevalaṃ śuddhaṃ yogo tasmānmanomayam|
cintayet paramaṃ brahma kṛtvā tatpravaṇaṃ manaḥ| iti|

yeca vādhyakarmmaṇyatā yogaphalavatva-saṃśaya-prasādālasya-viṣaya-vaimukhyābhāvabhrānti samādhibhūmyalābhacittāpratiṣṭhādayovighnāścittavikṣepa śabditā ste'pi brahmānusandhānenaiva nivarttante||

taduktaṃ , pātañjale, --

"īśvarapraṇidhānādvā" ityādinā yoganirupaṇānantaraṃ `tasyavācakaḥ praṇavaḥ’ "tajjapastadartha bhāvanam" `tataḥ pratyak cetanādhigamo'ntarāyā bhāvaśce ti sūtraiḥ|

naca tadartha bhāvanasyāpi saṃyamarupatvena tatrāpi vighnāvakāśaḥ sarvvapāpatulānalarupe bhagavajjane vighnāvakāśā bhāvāt|

tathāca, viṣṇupurāṇe, --

"yathāgni ruddhataśikhaḥ rukṣaṃ dahati sānilaḥ|
tathācittasthito viṣṇu ryogināṃ sarvvakilviṣamiti"||
"tathāprāguktānāṃ vikṣepākhya vighnānāṃ sarvveṣāmapi vikṣepa saha jammanāñca duḥkhadairmmanasyāṅgakampaśvāsanidrādrīnāṃ nirāsāya yathābhimata dhyānameva tanuyādityeva kṣyuktam| tat pratiṣedhārthameka tattvāghyāsra (+) iti sūtreṇa"||

P.247
vaśiṣṭaḥ –

"ete brahmaṇ madādoṣāḥ saṃsāra vyādhihetavaḥ|
manāgapi na lumpanti cittamekasamāhitam||
adhikṛta hṛdākāśāḥ kāmakrodha vikārajāḥ|
cintā na parihiṃsanti cittamekasamāhitam||

ye durarthā durārambhā durguṇā durudāhṛtāḥ|
duṣkramāste na kṛntanti cittamekasamāhitam||
parisphurati yasyāntarnityaṃ satya camatkṛttiḥ|
svabrahmāṇḍamivāsvaṇḍaṃ digīśāḥ pālayantitam"||

ādi purāṇe –
"samyag darśanasmpanno nābhibhūyate karmmabhiḥ|
yogavid yogayuktātmā paraṃ nirbbāṇa mṛcchati"||
śrutiśca –
tarhi ya evaṃ vedāhaṃbrahmāsmīti sa idaṃ sarvvaṃ bhavati tasyaha na devāścānābhūtyā deśataḥ ātmāhyaiṣāṃ sabhavatītyādikā iti|

"kiñcamokṣādarbbāg jāyamānāḥ siddhya stāapi vighnarupā eva mantavyāḥ| siddhyāsaktamanaso yogino mokṣo durllabho yataḥ|

tathāca skanda purāṇe –
"antarāyā bhavantīha yogino yoga hānidāḥ|
śruyate dūragāvārttā dūrasthaṃ dṛśyate punaḥ||

vistarasya bhiyānoktāḥ saṃnnibaddhāhyanekaśaḥ|

iti sarvvaśāstra siddhāntaḥ|

"jaṣmīṣadhi mantratapaḥ samādhijāḥ siddhayaḥ" iti yakṣādīnāmākāśagamanalakṣaṇākapilādīnāṃ-janmasamanantaramevopajāyamānajñānādi lakṣaṇā jammasiddhiḥ|

P.251
devyubāca—
"yatkiñcit kalpanāṃ jālaṃ tatramokṣāya śaṅkara|

"satyametattvayoktaṃ te vadāmiśrṛṇu sundari!
dvividhāḥ siddhayoloke kalpitā'kalpitāḥ śive!||
rasauṣadhi kriyākāla-mantrayantrādi sādhanāt|
sidhyanti siddhayoyāstu kalpitāstāḥ prakīrttitāḥ||
ā......tālpavīryyāstāḥ siddhayaḥ sādhanodbhavāḥ|
sādhanena vināpyete jāyante svataeva hi||
svātmayogaikaniṣṭhetu svātantryādīśvaraistataḥ|

cirakālāt prajāyante vāsanā rahiteṣuca||
tāsubhāvyo mahāyogaḥ paramātmapade'kṣaye|
vinā sūryyaṃ sadādīptiḥ yogasiddhasya lakṣaṇam||
yathā kāśīṃ samuddiśya() gacchadbhiḥ pathikaiḥ pathi|
nānātīrthāni dṛśyante tathāmokṣetu siddhayaḥ||
svayameva prajāyante lābhālābhavivarjjite|
siddhibhirlakṣayet siddho jīvanmuktistathaivaca||
alaukika guṇastasya kadācidṛśyate dhruvam|
ityetat kathitandevi! yogasiddhasya lakṣaṇam||

siddhibhiḥ parihīnantat naraṃ baddhantu lakṣayet|
ajaro'marāpiṇḍī yo jīvanmuktaḥ sa evahi||

mānasollāse –
"citte niścalatāṃ yāte prāṇe madhye layaṃ gate|

P.253
tathāṇimādi lakṣaṇaṃ –
"aṇimā mahimā caiva laghimā garimā tathā|
prāptiḥ prākāmyamaiśitvaṃ vaśitvaṃ cāṣṭasiddhayaḥ||"
anantamaṇuṣu prāptiḥ svātmatvena praveśanam|
aṇimā saṃjñamaisvaryyaṃ vyāptasya paramātamanaḥ||
brahāmāṇḍādi śivāṇḍāyāḥ ṣaṭtriṃśat tattvasaṃhateḥ|
vahiśca vyāpyavṛttitva maiśvaryyaṃ mahimāhṇayam|
mahāmeru samāṅgasya samuddharaṇa karmmaṇi||
atyalpa tūlatulyatvaṃ laghimānaṃ vidurvadhāḥ|
paramāṇu samāṅgasya samuddharaṇa karmmaṇi||
gaurave merutulyatvaṃ garimāṇaṃ vidurvudhāḥ|
pātāla vāsinaḥ puṃso brahmalokāvalokanam||

ākāśa gamanādīnā manyāsāṃ siddhisampadām||
svecchāmātreṇa saṃsiddhiḥ prākāmyamabhidhīyate|

prākāmya midamaiśvaryyamiti kecit pracakṣate||
svacchāmātreṇu lokānāṃ sṛṣṭisthityantakarttṛtā|
sūryyādīnāṃ niyoktṛtva mīśitvamabhidhīyate||
salokapālāḥ sarvve'pi lokāśca vaśavarttinaḥ|
tadaiśvaryyaṃ vaśitvākhyaṃ sulabhaṃ śivayoginām||
sarvvātmabhāva sāmrājyo nirantariti cetasām|

yastvevaṃ vrāhmaṇo vetti tasya devāvaśesthitāḥ|

P.255
vāhupramāṇaṃ sthiradṛk ślathāṅgā,

P.256
jagadvyāpāra nirmmukta stathā yogīlayaṅgataḥ|

sagyak puṣṭiṃ prakurbbanti dhātūnāṃ samavāyavaḥ|

ahorātra dvayenāpi layenāntaśca yoginaḥ|
