P.259
dvādaśāha layenāpi bhūcaratvaṃ hi siddhyati||
nimeṣārddha pramāṇena paryyaṭatyeva bhūtalam||
tatastrodaśāhena layenāpi mahadbhūtām|
yogīndraḥ khecarīsiddhiṃ labhate cintanādapi||
catarddaśa dināntañca layastho yadi tiṣṭhati|
aṇimākhyā'sya siddhiḥ sthādaṇutvaṃ prāpyate yayā||
ātmanaivātmanālīno yogī ṣāḍa़śa vāsaram|
labhate mahimā siddhiṃ sa mahārūpadhṛg yathā||
aṣṭādaśa dināntañca layastho yadi tiṣṭhati|
garimākhyāṃ labhet siddhiṃ yayā bhūbhāradhṛgbhavet||
yasya viṃśad vāsarānto layastiṣṭhati niścalaḥ|
laghimākhyā bhavet siddhi ryayāṇutvasya bhāradhṛk||
dvāviṃśati dināni syāt svalakṣe yo layaṃ gataḥ|
"prāptisirddhirbhavettasya prāpayed yo jagat sthitam|
pare layaṃ gato yogī caturviṃśati vāsarān||
tayā prākāmyasiddhiḥ syāt īpsitaṃ labhate yayā|
yasyaivāstaṃ gataṃ cittaṃ ṣaḍa़viṃśati dināni vai|
labhate jagadīśitvaṃ yena viśvagururbhavet|
aṣṭāviṃśatyaharyasya laya stiṣṭhet sthirāsane||
vaśitvasiddhiḥ prāptiḥ syāt yayā vai vaśyavajjagata|
gantumicchanti ye kecit parabrahma pade layam||
bhavanti siddhayaḥ sarbbā steṣāṃ vidhvaṃsakārikāḥ|
māsa mekaṃ layo yasya lagnastiṣṭhedakhaṇḍitaḥ||
najāgartti sayogīndro yāvanmokṣaṃ sagacchati|
navamāsa layenāpi pṛthvītattvaṃ sagacchati||
p.260
pṛthvītatvetu saṃsiddhe yogīndro vajjasannibhaḥ|
sārddhasambatsarenāpi layasthasyāpiyoginaḥ||
sambatsara trayeṇāpi layasyasyāpi yoginaḥ|
toyatattvasyasiddhviḥ syādvāyutattvamayobhavet||
tejastattavasyasiddhviḥ syāttejastattvamayo bhavet|
ṣaḍa़bhiḥ samvatsarairyogītvakhaṇḍalaya saṃsthitaḥ||
vāyutattvasya siddhviḥ syāt vāyutattamayo bhavet|
tathā dvādaśabhirvarṣai rlayasthasya nirantaram||
vyomatattvasyasiddhviḥ syādvyomatattvamayo bhavet|
caturviṃśatirbhirvarṣairlayasthasya nirantam||
śaktitattvasyasiddhisptyācchakti(+) tattvamayohi saḥ|
braṣṇāṇḍaṃ sakalaṃ paśyet prāṇisthamivamauktikam||
"ātmakāyasvarūpañca nidhāyātha yathāsthitam|
kāyasthodṛśyate loke tattvacaryyāṃ samācaret||
tattvacaryyāṃ karotveva śaktitattva kṣamāyaca|
itthaṃ kramavivṛddhena layābhyāsena yoginaḥ||
bhuñjate paramānandaṃ bhūśūṇḍādi mahātmavat|
brahma-viṣṇu maheśānāṃ pralayeṣvapi yoginaḥ||
bhuñjate paramānandaṃ bhuśuṇḍādi mahātmavat||"
atra (+) yogānanda-nirūpaṇānantaraṃ siddhikramamāha,

bhagavān patañjaliḥ –
"vaiśeṣikalayedravyaguṇakarmmaṇāmarthasaṃjñāvaddhāraṇā dhyānasamādhīnā meva viṣayāṇāṃ vyavahāra 
lāghavāya saṃyama iti-saṃjñāṃ kṛtvā tasya saṃyamasya phalamupayogayogañca sūtrābhyāṃ pradarśayet|
p.261
tadyathā—
"tajjayāt prajñālokaḥ" "tasyabhūmiṣu viniyogaḥ" etadarthastu—tasyasaṃyamasya jayādabhyāsena sātmāpādanāt prajñājñātavyaviveka rupāyā ālokaḥ prasarobhavati| prajñāntarānabhibhūtatve sati nirmmale pravāhe avasthā lakṣaṇo hyālokaḥ bhavatītyarthaḥ|
tasya saṃyamasya bhūmiṣu sthūlasūkṣmāvalambanabhedena sthitāsu citavṛttiṣu viniyogaḥkāryyaḥ| adharāmadhārañcittabhūmiṃ jitāṃ kṛtvā uttarasyāmuttarasyāñcittabhūmau saṃyamaḥ kāryyaḥ| nahyaptātmīkṛtādharabhūmiruttaramyāṃ bhūmau saṃyamaṃ kurvvāṇaḥ phalabhāgyayavati| yathoktasya saṃyamasya viṣaya-pariśuddhiṃ karttuṃ pariṇamatrayaṃ sūtreṇādarśayat| vyutthāna-nirodha-saṃskārayorabhibhavaprādurbhāvau nirodhakṣaṇa cittānvayo nirodhapariṇāmaḥ"| vyutthānaṃ kṣiptamūḍha़-vikṣiptākhya bhūmitrayaṃ nirodhaḥ prakṛṣṭa sattusyāṅgatayā(+) cetasaḥ pariṇāmaḥ| tābhyāṃ vyutthāna nirodhābhyāṃ janitau yau saṃskārau tayoryathākramābhibhavonyagmūtatayā kāryyāntara karaṇāsāmarthyeṇāvasthānaṃ prādurbhāva varttamāne'dhvani abhivyaktarupatayā'vasthānam| nirodhāvasara varttinaścittasva dharmmiṇo'nyā dvayorvyatthāna nirodhākhyayovarasthayoḥ kharupeṇāvasthānaṃ sanirodha pariṇāmaḥ prathamaḥ|
ayamāśayaḥ –
yadā byutthāna saṃskārarupodharmmastirobhavati nirodha saṃskārarupaścāvirbhavati dharmmirupanayāvittamubhayānvayitvenāvasthitaṃ pratīyate sanirodha pariṇām śabdenavyavahriyate|
P.262
yadyapicahatvādguṇavṛttasya cetasoniścalatvaṃ nāsti tathāpi evambhūtāvariṇāmasya sthairyyamucyate| etat pariṇāma phalaṃ tu cittasya parihṛta vikṣepatayāsadṛśa pravāha pariṇāmatvam
tathā –
"sarvvorthekāgratayoḥ kṣayodayau citrasya samādhi pariṇāmaḥ"| (artho'sya) (1) sarvvārthatārupasya vikṣepasyātyanta tiraskārāt anutpattiratīte'dhvani praveśalakṣaṇaḥ kṣayonatunyagbhāvamātram| ekālamvana sadṛśa pariṇāmatvalakṣaṇasyaikāgratvasyodbhavo'bhivyaktiḥ|
cittastrodrikta sattvasyānvayitayā'vasthānaṃ- bartta māne'dhvani prakaṭatamiti yāvat samādhi pariṇāmo dvitīyaḥ|
asyaiva parākāṣṭhaṃkāgratāpariṇāmaḥ| sayathā "śāntoditautulyapratyayau cittasyaikāgratā pariṇāmaḥ samāhitasyaiva cittasya ekapratyayo vṛttiviśeṣaḥ śānto'tītamadhvānaṃpraviṣṭaḥ| aparastu udito varttamāne'dhvanisphuritaḥ| dvāvapi samāhitacittatvena tulyau ekarupālambanatvena sadṛśau pratyayau| ubhayatrāpisamāhitasyaiva citrasyānvayitvenāvasthānaṃ yat samādhirekāgratāṃ pariṇāma ityucyate etadeva traividhya manyatrātideṣṭuṃ sūtram –
"etena bhūtendriyeṣu dharmmalakṣaṇāvasthāpariṇāmāvyākhyātāḥ" etena trividhoktvena citra(+)pariṇāmena bhūteṣu sthūlasūkṣmeṣu indriyeṣu vuddhvikarmmāntaḥkaraṇabhedenāvasthiteṣu dharmmalakṣaṇāvasthā bhedena trividhaḥ pariṇāmovyākhyāto'vagantavyaḥ| pariṇāmonāmā'vasthitasya dharmmiṇaḥ pūrvadharmma nibṛttau dharmmāntarāpattiḥ|
P.263
atra dharmma pariṇāmo yathā –
mṛdādi lakṣaṇasya dharmmiṇaḥ piṇḍarupādi dharmmaparityāgena ghaṭadharmmāntara svīkāraḥ|
tathā tasyaiva ghaṭasyānāgatādhvaparityāgena varttamānādhvasvīkāraḥ tatparityāgena vātītādhvā parigraha lakṣaṇaḥ pariṇāmaḥ|
avasvā pariṇāmo yathā - - tasyaiva ghaṭasya prathama dvitīyayoḥ kṣaṇayoranvayitvenāvasthāgatam| yata --"ma(+)laṃguṇavṛttaṃ nāpariṇamamānaṃ kṣaṇamapyāste"|
atha saṃyama balenotpadyamānāḥ siddhīrupadarśayati, -- pariṇāmatrayaṃ saṃyamādatītānāgata jñānam| uktasya dharmmalakṣaṇā'vasthākhya pariṇāmatrayasyāsmin dharmmaṇi, ayaṃ dharmmaḥ, idaṃ lakṣaṇam, iyamavamthā anāgatādhvanaḥ sametya varttamāne'dhvani khabyāpāraṃ vidhāyātītamadhvānaṃ praviśatau tyevaṃ parihṛtavikṣepatayā saṃyamakaraṇādatītānāgataṃ sarbbaṃ jānāti|
kharityādi kṛta vikṣepa pratibandhasya saṃyame nāśe sati cittasya sahajasarbbārthagrahaṇasya arthābhivyakteḥ| tathā gartyarthajñānānāṃ bhinnānāmapītaretarādhyāsaḥ| kaḥ śabdaḥ? ko'rthaḥ? kiṃ jñānamiti praśne gaurityevaṃ rupe uttaradānāt prasiddhagośabdā ttodartha-jñānānāṃ gaurityevamekarupa pratibhāsaḥ| 
tena baddhaikarupatāpādānāt saṃkīrnānāṃ yogavibhāgau gaurityayaṃ śabdovācakaḥ sāsnādimān piṇḍovācyaḥ, jñānañca tat prakāśakaṃ ityevaṃ rupaḥ| tasya saṃyamanena sarvva bhūtarutajñānamabhidhāya dharmmādharmma lakṣaṇasya saṃskrārasya musvarāgāduditasya paracittasya kāyarūpasya nāstyasmin kāye rupamiti bhāvyamānasya śīghraciravipākalakṣaṇa
p.264
karmmaṇe maitryādīnāṃ hastyādibalasya sūryyasya tadvadvarṇasya dhruvasya saṃyamāt|
yathākramaṃ pūrvvajāti jñāna paracittaviṣaya viśeṣa niścaya-grāhyaśaktistambhapūrvvakānta(+)rddhāna niyatadeśakālābacchinnaśarīrāntaḥkaraṇa yogajñāna sarbbabhitratvādi prāptibalābhirbhāva bhuvanajñāna-tārā byūhākhyajyotiścakrajñāna tārāgati jñānāni bhavantītyapavarnitam(1)| tathā-- "pravṛttyālokanyāsāt sūkṣmabyavahitaviprakṛṣṭajñānam" ityanena pravṛttau biṣayavatī lakṣaṇāyāṃ jyotiṣmatī lakṣaṇāyāṃ ca prāguktāyāṃ yo'sāvalokaḥ sāttvikaḥ prasarastasya niścaleṣu viṣayeṣu sannyāsāt tattadvidhānāṃ bhāvanābhiḥ sāntaḥkaraṇendriyeṣu prakṛṣṭaśaktitāmāpanneṣu sūkṣmasya paramāṇvādervyavahitasya bhūmyantagata nidhānā(nyānā) deviprakṛṣṭasya meru pārśvavarttino rasāyanāderjñānaṃ bhavati ityevaṃ vāhyāḥ siddhīḥ pradarśyāntaryyaścanābhicakracihnādhastātsthita-gartta-kūpakaṇṭha kupākhyadeśa tadadhaḥsthita kūrmmanāḍa़ī brahmarandhra rupamūrddha jyotiḥ saṃyamebhyo yathākramanāḍa़ī sanniveśātmaka kāyagatavyūhajñāna kṣutpipāsā nibṛttisiddhadarśana lakṣaṇāḥ siddhayaḥ pratipāditāḥ|
tathā "sattvapuruṣayoratyantāsaṅkīrṇayoḥ pratyayāviśeṣo bhogaḥ parārthāt svārthasaṃyamāt puruṣajñānamityanena sattvapuruṣayoḥ prakāśasusvātmakabhoktvra(+)dhiṣṭhānarupayorjvaḍa़ājaḍa़tvenātyantabhinnayoryaḥ pratyayāviśeṣo bhogenā pratimāsanāt sattvasyevakarttṛtā pratyayena yā sukhaduḥkhasambit sambhogaḥ sacasattasya svārthernairapekṣyeṇa parārthaḥ puruṣārthaḥ, tasmādanyo yaḥ svārthaḥ puruṣakharupamātrāvalambanaḥ parityaktvā'ṅkārasattve yā cicchāyā saṃkrāntiḥ| tatrakṛtasaṃyamasya puruṣasya jñānamutpadyate| tatra jñāne
P.265
utpanne tadevarupaṃ ālambanajñānaṃ satvaniṣṭhaṃ puruṣo jānāti| na punaḥ puruṣo jñātā jñānasya viṣayabhāva māpadyate| jāḍyāpatteriti puruṣasākṣātkāra phalaṃ siddhyantaramuktvā tataḥ "prātibha śrāvaṇavedanādarśa svāda vārttā jāyante" ityanena sūkṣmādyartha phalakaṃ prātibhaṃ jñānaṃ, divyaśabdajñāna phalakam, śrāvaṇasaṃjñam, divyasparśajñāna phalakam vedanā saṃjñam tvāca-jñānamsa divyarūpajñānaphalakam, ādarśa saṃjñaṃ cākṣuṣa jñānam, divyarasa jñānaphalakaṃ āsvādana kalākhyaṃ rasanajñānama divyagandha jñānaphalaṃ bhūtisaṃjñakaṃ ghrāṇendriyajanyajñānam, atyanta dūravarttī-śabdagrahaṇarūpavārttākhyajñānarūpa mityuktvā "tena samādhā vupasargā vyatthānesiddhaya" iti phalaviśeṣa viṣayavibhāgañcābhidhāya "bandhakāraṇa śaithilyāt pracārasambedanāccittasay paraśarīrāveśa" ityanena vyāpakībhūtātmamanasorniyata karmmavaśādeva śarīrāntargatayoreva sukhaṃ dehomanastva yasya bhoktetyeva mahamidaṃ mameti bhogyabhoktṛbhāvena sambedanamupajāyate| saca śarīra bandha ityucyate|
tadyathā,--
samādhi vaśādbandhakāraṇaṃ dharmmādharmmākhyaṃkarmmaṃ śithilaṃ bhavati| tāneva māpadyate cittasya| yo'sau pracāro hṛdaya pradeśāt indriyadbāreṇa viṣayābhimukhyena prasaraḥ| tasya saṃbedana mittham, cittavahānāḍa़ī anayā cittaṃ vahati itthaṃ rasa prāṇādi vahābhyo nāḍa़ībhyo vilakṣaṇeti svapara-śarīrayoryadā śarīrasya sañcāraṃ jānāti tadā parakīyaṃ śarīraṃmṛtaṃ jīvavaccharīraṃ vā cittasañcāradvāreṇa praviśati|
P.266
cittaṃ paraśarīraṃ praviśadindriyānuvarttante| madhukara rājamiva makṣikāḥ| tataḥ vara śarīrapraviṣṭo yogī svaśarīra vattvena vyavaharati| vyāpaka-cittapuruṣa bhogasaṅkocanimittasya karmmaṇaḥ samādhinākṣiptatvāt svātantryāt sarvvabhoga niṣpattiriti siddhyantaramabhidhāya "udānajayāj jalapaṅkakaṇḍhakādyasaṅgam" "samāna jayāt dīpti"rddeśadeśibhāvalakṣaṇā, "śrotrākāśasambandha saṃyamāt" sūkṣmavyavahita śabdadigrāhaka divyaśrotralāmam, kāyākāśasambandhasaṃyamāllakṣatulādi mayabhāvalābhañca| ākāśagamanañcopavarṇya "vahirakalpitāvṛttirvvidehā" "tataḥ prakāśāvaraṇakṣayaḥ"(1) ityanena śarīrāhaṅkāreṇa vā manovṛttiḥ sākalpitākhyā|(+)| yadā punaḥ śarīrāhaṅkāra bhāvaṃ parityajya svātantryeṇa manovṛttiḥ sākalpitā mahāvidehākhyā, tatsaṃyamāccitramalakṣayo bhavatīti evasiddhyantaramuktam|
evaṃ samādhyupayoginīrantaḥkaraṇa vahiḥkaraṇa lakṣaṇendriya bhavāḥ prāṇādi vāyujayabhavāśca siddhī yoginaścattaudāryyāya samāśvāsotpattaye pratipādyasabīja nirbīja samādhisiddhaye vividhopāyāḥ satraiḥ, pradarśitāḥ tāni vyākhyāsahitāni pradarśyante|

yathā,--
"sthūlasvarūpasūkṣmānvayārthasaṃyamādbhūtajayaḥ" sthūlabhūtānāṃ paridṛśyaviśiṣṭākāravadbhūtapañcānāṃ yathākramaṃ sleha kārkaśyoṣṇitā preṇāvakāśadānalakṣaṇaṃ svarūpaṃ sūkṣmāṇikāraṇabhūtāni, tanmānāni anvayito guṇāḥ prakāśa pravṛtti-sthitayaḥ| arthavattavañca teṣve vaguṇeṣu bhogāpavargasampādanākhyā śaktiḥ|
P.267
iti pañcāvasthā vibhinnabhūteṣu pratyavasthaṃkrameṇa saṃyamaṃ kurvvan bhūtajayī bhavati| tadyathā, --
prathamaṃ sthūlarūpe saṃyamaṃ vidhāya tadanusvarūpe ityevaṃ krameṇa kṛtasaṃyamasya saṃkalpitānuvighāyinyovatsānuvidhāyinyogāvaiva bhūtaprakṛtayo bhavanti|
tasyaiva bhūtajayasya phalamāha,--
"tato'ṇimādiprādurbhāvaḥ kāyasampattaddharmmānabhighātaśca" "aṇimā parapamāṇurūpatāprāptiḥ (pattiḥ) tatovajrādīnāmapyantaḥ praviśati| mahimā mahatvaprāptiḥ, (yatomahān bhavati)| laghimātūla-piṇḍavallaghutva prāptiḥ, garimā merurūpatāpattiḥ, aṅgulyagreṇa candrādi sparśayogyatā prāptiḥ|
prākāmya bhicchānābhighātaḥ śarīrāntaḥ karaṇayoḥ|
īśitvaṃ sarvvatra prabhaviṣṇutā| vaśitvaṃ – sarvvāṇyeva bhūtāni anuvāsitvāttaduktanātikrāmanti|
yatra kāmāvaśāyitvaṃ yasmin viṣaye kāmaicchā tasmin viṣaye yogino'vasāyobhavati taṃ viṣayaṃ svīkāradvāreṇābhilāṣaparyyantaṃ jayatītyarthaḥ|
etesamādhyupayogino'ṇimādayo'ṣṭauguṇāmahāsiddhayaityucyante | (1)|
kāyasampadaśca lakṣaṇaṃ sūtreṇāha, --
"rūpalāvaṇya valavajra saṃhananatvāni kāyasampat"| (2)
vajra saṃhananaṃ vajravat kaṭhinā saṃhatirasya saṃhatirasya śarīre bhavati|
P.268
śeṣaṃ ṣpaṣṭam| tasya valayasya dharmmā rūpādayaḥ teṣāmagni vāyvādinā nāśo na bhavati|
atha bhūtajayamabhidhāya prāptabhūmikasyendriyajayamāha||
"grahaṇasvarupāsmitānvayārthavatva-saṃyamādindriyajayaḥ"|
grahaṇaṃ indriyābhimukhī vṛttri;, svarupaṃ sāmānyena prakāśatvam, asmitāiṅkārānugamaḥ| anvayārthavatve pūrvvamukte eṣāmindriyāṇāmavasthā pañcakena pūrvvavat saṃyamaṃ kṛtvendriyajayī bhavati|
tasya phalamāha—
"tato manojavitvaṃ vikaraṇa bhāvaḥ pradhāna jayaśca"|
śarīrasya manovadttamagatilābhaḥ kāyanirapokṣāṇāṃ indriyāṇāṃ vṛttilābhaḥ sarvvavaśitvaṃ ceti tritayaṃ phalaṃ etāḥ siddhayo jitendriyasya prādurbhavanti| etāśca madhupratīkākhyāḥ, yathā madhuna ekadeśe'pi svadattre mudaṃ dhatte evametāḥ pratyekamiti|
athāntaḥkaraṇa jayamāha,--
"sattvapuruṣānyathākhyātimātrasyasarvvabhāvādhiṣṭhātṛtvaṃ sarvvajñātṛtvañca"| asmin śuddhe sāttvike pariṇāse kṛtasaṃyamanasya sattvapuruṣayorutpadyatevivekakhyātiḥ guṇānāṃ kartṛtvābhimānaḥ śithilībhāvarupā| tanmāhātmyāt tatraiva sthitasya yoginaḥ sarvvabhāvādhiṣṭhātṛtvaṃ sarvvajñātṛtvaṃ ca samādherbhavati|
sarvveṣāṃ guṇa pariṇāmāṇāṃ bhāvānāṃ svāmivadākramaṇaṃ sarvvādhiṣṭhā-
P.269
tṛtvam| teṣāmeva śāntoditā'vyapadeśa dharmmatvena sthitānāṃ yathāvad vivekajajñānaṃ sarvvajñātṛtvam| eṣācāsmin śānte'syāṃ vaśīkārasaṃjñāyāṃ prāptāyāṃ viśokonāma siddhirucyate|
krameṇa bhūmikāntaramāha, --
"tadvairāgyādapidoṣa bīja kṣaye kaivalyam" (1),
viśokāyāmapi vairāgyaṃ yadotpadyate yoginaḥ
tadā tasmāddoṣāṇāṃ rāgādīnāṃ yadbījaṃ avidyādiḥ|
tasya kṣaye nirmmūlane kaivalyaṃ ātyantikī duḥkhanivṛttriḥ
puruṣasya guṇānāṃ adhikāra parisamāptiḥ svarupapratiṣṭhatvam|
asminneva samādhausthityupāyamāha|
"sthāṇupanimantraṇe saṅgasmayākaraṇaṃ punaraniṣṭaprasaṅgāt" (2)
catvāriyoginaḥ sthānāni bhavanti|
tatrābhyāsavān pravṛttimātrajyotiḥ prathamaḥ|
pradhama kalpa iti cocyate|(+)
ṛtambhara prajño dvitīyo madhu bhūmika ityucyate|
bhūtendriyajayau tṛtīyaḥ prajñojyoti riti|
atikrānta bhāvanaścaturthaḥ, tasya caturthasya samādhyāvirbhūta saptavighaprāptabhūmi-prajñasyāntyāṃ madhumatī saṃjñikāṃ bhūmikāṃ sākṣāt kurvvataḥ sthānino devo upanimantrayitāro bhavanti|
divyastrī rasāyanādikaṃ ṭokayanti| tasminnupanimantraṇe'nena saṅgo na karttavyaḥ| nāsmin smayaśca| saṅgati karaṇe puna rviṣaya-
P.270
bhoge niyata smayakaraṇe kṛtakṛtya bhivātmānaṃ manyamāno na samādhimutsahate| ataḥsaṅgasmayayoḥ tena varjjanaṃ karttavyam|
asyāmeva phalabhūtāyāṃ vivekakhyātau|
pūrvvokta saṃyama vyatirikta mupāyāntaramāha, --
"kṣaṇastatkramayoḥ sambandha saṃyamādvivekajaṃ jñānam" |(1)
kṣaṇaḥ sarvvāntyaḥ kālāvayavo yasyakalāḥ
pravibhaktuṃ na śakyante| tathā vidhānāṃ kālakṣaṇānāṃ yaḥ kramaḥ paurbbāparyyeṇa pariṇāmaḥ| tatra saṃyamāt prāguktaṃ vivekajaṃjñānamutpadyate|
ayamarthaḥ—
ayaṃ kālakṣaṇo'muṣmāt kālakṣaṇāduttaro'yamasmāt (n) pūrvva ityevaṃ vidhe krame kṛtasaṃyamasyā'ntaḥ sūkṣme'pi kramakrame yadā bhavati sākṣātkāraḥ tadā'nyadapi sūkṣmaṃ mahadādi sākṣāt karotīti viveka jñānotpattiḥ|
asyaiva saṃyamasya viṣaya vivekopāyamāha | --
"jāti lakṣaṇa deśairanyathā vyavacchedāttulyayostataḥ pratipattiḥ"(2)
yadā (thā) arthānāṃ bhedahetavo jāti lakṣaṇadeśā bhavanti|
yathā gauriti karvvurāruṇeti jātilakṣaṇe bhedake tula pariṇāmayordaśo bhedakaḥ|
yatra punarbhedo'vadhārayitūṃ na śakyate, yathā-ekadeśasthitayo śuklayoḥ pārthivayoḥ paramāṇvoḥ|
P.271
tathāvidheviṣaye kṛtasaṃyamasya yadā bhedajñānamupajāyate tadā tasyābhyāsāt sūkṣmāṇyapi tattvāni vuhvyādīni bhedena pratipādyante|
uktasya vivekajñānasya saṃjñāviṣayaṃ khebhāvaṃcākhyātuṃ "tārakaṃ sarvvaviṣayaṃ sarvvathā viṣayamakramañceti vivekajaṃ jñānam"| (1) uktasaṃyamavalāt dattāyāṃ bhūmikāyāṃ unpannajñānaṃ tārayati agādhasaṃsāropādhe ryogina miti tārakam|
tasya viṣayamāha, --
sarvvāṇi tattvāni mahadādīni viṣayo'syeti sarvvaviṣayam|
khebhāvāntaramāha, --
akramañceti-niḥśeṣanānā'vasthā pariṇatadvitryeka bhāvagraheṇāsyakramovidyata ityakramaṃ sarvvaṃkaratalāmalakavat yugapatpaśyatītyarthaḥ| tasmācca tārakākhyādviveka jñānāt kiṃ bhavatītyāha—
"sattvapuruṣayoḥ śuddhi sāmye kaivalyamiti" (2)|
sāmyaṃ sattvasya sarvathā karttṛtvābhimāna nivṛttyā svakāraṇe'nupraveśaḥ śuddhiḥ, puruṣasya śuddhirupacaritabhogābhāva iti dvayoḥ samānāyāṃ śuddhau mokṣo bhavati|
evañcābhyāsakrameṇa rājyādimokṣaparyyantāsu jāyamānāsu siddhvaṣu
P.272
yoginā na kvāpi prakāśanīyā naca vismayo'bhimāno vā karttavya statkaraṇe siddhirnaṣṭābhavet|
athaca dattātreyaḥ --
"pravṛtti lakṣaṇākhyānāt yogino visprayātta(sta) thā|
vijñānaṃ bilayaṃ yāti tena gopyāḥ pravṛttayaḥ||"
yogatattva prakāśe, --
"saṃdarśayennalokānāṃ kha sāmarthyaṃ kadācana|
kadāciddarśayet prītyā bhaktiyuktāya vā punaḥ||
"yathā mūko yathā mūḍha़o yathā vai vadhirojanaḥ|
tathā varttate lokeṣu svasāmarthyasya guptaye||
nocecchiṣyāhi vahavo bhaveryu rnaca saṃśayaḥ|
tat karmmakaraṇa vyagrasyābhyāso vismṛto bhavet|
abhyāsena vihīnastu tatolaukikamācaret||
laukikeca samāsakto bhaved bhraṣṭo na saṃśayaḥ| "
evaṃ karmmāpi na prakāśanīyan prakāśane niṣphalambhabhate|(+)
tathāhaṭha pradīpikāyām, --
"haṭhavidyāparaṃ gopyā yoginā siddhimicchatā|
bhaved vīryyavatī guptā nirviryyāt prakāśite"ti||
atha siddha yogena vā kālacihnāni sarvāvaśyaṃ draṣṭavyāni yāni tāni likhyante|
P.2 73
atra siddhayogena jīvammuktikāmena kālavañcanārtham| asiddha yogena tu maraṇakāle yogasaṃsprṛtiḥ punarjjammāntare yogaprāptaye|
tathāca gītāyām, --
"yaṃ yaṃ vāpi smaran bhāvaṃ tyajatyante kalevaram|
tantamevaiti kaunteye! sadātadbhāva bhāvatiḥ||
eṣā brāhmīsthitiḥ pārtha! naināṃ prāpya vimuhyati|
sthitvo'syāmantakāle'pi brahmanirbbāṇa mṛcchati|| " (1)
kālavañcana vidhistu prasaṅgāt pūrvamevokta;|

taccihnāni yathā skanda purāṇe, --
"vāma nāsāpuṭe yasya pāyurvāti divāniśam|
akhaṇḍamekaṃ tasyāyu rnaśyatyabdatrayeṇahi||
dvyahorātraṃ tryahorātram ravirvahati santatam|
ādyekavarṣaṃ tasyeha jīvitāvadhirucyate|
vahennāsāṣuṭayuge daśāhāni nirantaram|
vāyuścet prati saṃkrāntiṃ tadā jīvedṛtu trayam||
nāsāvartmadbrayaṃ hitvā mātariśvā mukhādbraman|
śaṃseddhiyā tato'rvāk ca prayāṇaṃ tasya khādhvani||
sūryye saptamarāṃśisthe janmarkṣasthe niśākare|
pauṣṇāḥ sa kālo neṣṭavyo yadā yāmye ravirvahet||
akasmāttasya tatkāle mṛtyuḥ sannihito bhavet||
cintanīyaḥ prayatnena sakālo mṛtyu bhīruṇā||(+)
P.274
akasamādvīkṣate yastu puruṣaṃ kṛṣṇapiṅgalam|
asminnevakṣaṇerupaṃ sajīvedvatsaradbrayam|
yasyavīryyaṃ malaṃ mūtraṃ kṣutaṃ mūtraṃ malantuvā|
ihaikadā yatettacce dvarṣaṃ tasyāyuriṣyate||
indranīlanibhaṃ vyomni nāgabṛndaṃ ya ikṣate|
itastataḥ pracālitaṃ ṣaṇmāsantu sa jīvati||
arundhatī bhavejihvā dhruvo nāsāgre mucyate|
viṣṇoḥ padāni bhrūmadhyaṃ netraṃ glaurmātṛ maṇḍalam||
arundhatīṃ dhravañcaiva viṇṇoḥ trīṇipadāni ca|
āsannamṛtyurnekṣeta caturthaṃ mātṛmaṇḍalam||
"vettinīlādi varṇyasya vaṭa(+)mlādirasasyaca|
akasmādanyathā bhāvaṃ ṣaṇmāsena sa mṛtyubhāk||
ṣaṇmāsa mṛtyormarttasya kaṇṭhauṣṭha rasanā radāḥ|
śuṣyanti satataṃ tadvat sajihvāstālu pañcamāḥ||
retaḥ karaja netrāntaṃ nīlimānaṃ bhajantacet|
tarhi kīnāśa nagarīṃ ṣaṣṭhe māsi vrajennaraḥ||
drutamāruhya saraṭaṃ trivarṇa yasya mastakam|
prayāti yāti tasyāyuḥ ṣaṇmāse parisaṃkṣayam||
susnātasyāpi yasyāśu hṛdayaṃ pariśuṣyati|
caraṇaucakarau cāpi trimāsaṃ tasyajīvitam||
jāyāprakampate yasya dehabandhe'pi niścale|
kṛtāntadūtā vadhnanti caturthe māsi taṃ naram||
matirbhrasyeccacedvāṇī dhanuraindraṃ nirīkṣyate|
rātrau candra dvayañcaiva divādau ca divākarau||
P.275
divā satārakañcandraṃ rātrau vyoma vitārakam|
yugapat ca caturddikṣuśākraṃ (vyaktaṃ) kodaṇḍamaṇḍalam|
bhūruhe bhūdharāgrevā gandharva nagarālayam|
divā piśāca nṛtyañca hyete pañcatva hetavaḥ||
sarvaṣveteṣu cihneṣu yadyekamapivīkṣyate|
tadā māsāvadhiṃ mṛtyuṃ pratīkṣeta nacādhikam||
prohyate bhakṣyate yohi piśācāsura vāyasaiḥ|
bhūtaiḥ pretaiḥ śvabhirgṛghrai rgomāyu khara śūkaraiḥ||
śarabhaiḥ karabhaiḥ kīrai śyenairaśvatarairvakaiḥ|
khaple sa jīvitaṃ tyaktvā varṣānte yama mīkṣyate||
gandhapuṣpākṣataiḥ śoṇaiḥ khāntanuṃ bhūṣitāṃ naraḥ|
yaḥ paśyet khapnasamaye so'ṣṭo māsāṃstu jīvati||
pāṃśurāśīṃścavalmīkaṃ yūpadaṇḍamathāpi vā |
yo'dhirohati vaisvapne sa ṣaṣṭhe māsi naśyati|
rāsabhāruḍha़ mātmānaṃ tailābhyaktañca muṇḍitam|
nīyamānaṃ yamāśāṃ yāṃ yaḥ paśyet satu pūrvvavat||
svamaulau svatanauvāpi yaḥ paśyate svapragonaraḥ|
tṛṇāni śuṣka kāṣṭhāni ṣaṣṭhemāsi vinaśyati||
loha daṇḍadharaṃ kṛṣṇaṃ puruṣaṃ kṛṣṇuvāsasam|
svapnāgre ca sthitaṃ paśyet satrīn māsānnalaṅgayet||
kālīkumārī yaṃsvapne vaghnīyādvāhu pāśakaiḥ|
sa māsena samīkṣeta nagarīṃ śamanoṣitām|
narī yo vānarārūḍha़o yāyāt prācīṃ diśaṃ svapan|
dinaiḥ sa pañcāśair eva paśyet saṃyamanīṃ purīm||
P.276
kṛpāṇo'pi vadānyaḥ syāt vadānyaḥ kṛpaṇo yadi|
prakṛtirvikṛtiśca syāt tadā pañcatvamṛcchati||
etāni kālacihnāni santyanyāni vahūnyapi|
jñātvā'bhyasennaroyoga mathavā kāśikāṃ śrayet||
nākālakañcanopāyaṃ mune hyanyaṃ avaibhyaham||" iti|
mārkaṇḍeya purāṇe,--
"akṣīṇa karmabandhaś ca jñātvā mṛtyum aniścitaḥ|
utkrānti kāle saṃsmṛtya punaryogitva mṛcchati||
tasmādasiddhiyogena siddhayogenavā punaḥ|
jñeyānyariṣṭāni sadā yenotkrāntau na sīdati||
ariṣṭāni mahārāja! śṛṇṛ vakṣyāmi tāni te|
yeṣāmālokanānmṛtyuṃ nijaṃ jānāti yogavit||
asasmivimvaṃ sūryyañca vahṇicaivāṃśumālinam|
dṛṣṭvaikādaśa māsāṃs tu naro varṣaṃ sa jīvati||
vamenmūtraṃ purīpañca yaḥ svarṇaṃ rajatantathā|
pratyakṣa mathavā svapne jīvitaṃ daśamāsikam||
dṛṣṭvā pretapiśācādon gandharva nagarāṇica|
suvarṇavarna bṛkṣāṃśca navamāsaṃ sa jīvati|
sthūlaḥ kṛśaḥ kṛśaḥ sthūlo yo'kasmādeva jāyate|
prakṛteśca nivartteta tasyāyuśvāṣṭhasāsikam||
svaṇḍaṃ yasya padaṃ pārṣṇyāṃ pādasyāgre'thavā bhaveṃt|
pāṃśukarddamayorbhadhye saptamāsān sa jīvati||
P.277
kapotogṛdhrakākolū vāyaso vāpimūrddhani|
kravyādo vā khagastiṣṭhet ṣaṇmāsāyuḥ pradarśakaḥ||
hanyate kāka paṅlibhiḥ pāṃśuvarṣeṇa vānaraḥ|
śuṣvecca ve yasyamarmma snānādvāmāmadhastanam||
tasyāpi pañcabhirmāsai rvighnānmatyumupasthitam|
kha (syā) cchāyāmanyathā dṛṣṭvā caturmāsān sa jīvati||
anabhrevidyutaṃ dṛṣṭvā dakṣiṇāndiśamāsthitām|
udakendradhanurvvāpi jīvitaṃ dvitrimāsikam||
"ghṛte taile tathā darśe tailevāpyātmanaḥ tanum|
yaḥ paśye daśiraskāṃvā māsādūrddhvaṃ na jīvati||
yasyavasti samogandho gātreśavasamo'pivā|
tasyārddha māsikaṃjñeyaṃ yogino nṛpajīvitam||
yasyavaitastya mastiṣka hṛtpadmamavaśuṣyati|
pivataśca jalaṃ cauṣṭau daśāhaṃ so'pi jīvati||
stambhito (+) māruto yasya mabhrma sthānāni kṛntati
nahṛṣyatyamvusaṃsparśā ttasyamṛtyu rūpasthitaḥ||
ṛkṣa-vānara-yānasthaḥ bhramaṇ yo dakṣiṇāṃ diśam|
khapne prayāti tasyāpi namṛtyuḥ kālamicchati||
rakta kṛṣṇāmbaradharā gāyatrī hasati ca yam|
dakṣiṇāśāṃ nayennārī khapne so'pi na jīvati||
nagnaṃ kṣapaṇakaṃ khapne hasamānaṃ mahāvalam|
evaṃ vā vīkṣyabālāṃ taṃ vidhyānmṛtyu mupasthitam||
ābhastakatalādyastu nimagnaḥ paṅkasāgare|
khapne paśyatyathātmānaṃ sa sadyomriyate naraḥ||
P.278
keśāndāvāṃ stathābhasma bhujaṅgaṃ nirjjalāṃ nadīm|
dṛṣṭvākhapne daśāhāttu mṛtyurekādaśe'hani|
karālairvikaṭaiḥ kṛṣṇaiḥ puruṣairudyatāyughaiḥ|
pāṣāṇaistāritaḥ svapne sadyo mṛtyu mavāpnuyāt||
sūryyodaye yasya śivā krośantī yāti sammukham|
viparītyā'parītyāca sasadyo mṛtyubhṛcchati||
yasya vai bhuktamātrasya hṛdayaṃ bādhate kṣudhā|
jāyate dantakṣarmmaśca sa gātāyurasaṃśayam|
dīpādi gandhaṃ noveṃtti trasatyadbhistathāniśi|
nātmānaṃ para netrasthaṃ vīkṣate na sa jīvati||
śakrāyudhaṃ sārddharātre divendu grahaṇantathā|
dṛṣṭvāmanyeta saṃkṣīṇa mātmajīvita mātmavit||
nāsikā bakratāmeti karṇayornnamanonnatiḥ|
netrañca vāmaṃ sravati yasya tasyāyurantagam|
āraktatāmeti mukhaṃ jihlācāpyasitā yadi|
tadā prājñovijānīyāt mṛtyumāsannamātmanaḥ||
uṣṭrarāsabha yānena yaḥ khapne dakṣiṇāṃ diśam|
prayātitaṃ vijānīyāt khapne mṛtyuṃ nareśvara||
pidhāyakarṇo nirdhoṣaṃ na śṛṇotyātmasambhavam|
naśyati cakṣuṣo jryoti ryasyaso'pi na jīvati||
patitasya ca vaigartte yasya dbāraṃ pidhīyate|
nacotiṣṭhati yaḥ khapnāt tadantaṃ tasya jīvitam||
ūrddhvāca dṛṣṭirnaca saṃpratiṣṭhitā,
raktāpunaḥ saṃparivarttamānā|
P.279
mukhasya coṣṇaṃ śiśirāca nābhiḥ
śaṃsanti puṃsātmaparaṃ śarīram||
khapne'gniṃ praviśedyastu naca niṣkramate punaḥ|
jalapraveśādapivā tadantaṃ tasya jīvitam||
yaścābhihanyate dṛṣṭau bhūtairātmāvātho divā|
samṛtyuṃ saptarātrānte naraḥ prāpnotyasaṃśayam||
khavastramamalaṃ śuklaṃ paśyatyathāsitam|
yaḥ pumān mṛtyumāsannaṃ tasyāpihi vinirddriśet||
svabhāvavaiparītyañca prakṛteśca viparyyayaḥ|
kathayanti manuṣyāṇāṃ samāsanno'ya mantakaḥ||
yeṣāṃ vinītaḥ satataṃ ye'syapūjyatamā matāḥ|
tānevacāvajānāti tānevaca vinindati||
devān nārccayate vidvān gurūn viprāṃśca nindati|
mātā pitryorahaṅkāraṃ jāmātṛṇāṃ karotica||
yogināṃ jāti viduṣāṃ anyeṣāñca mahātmanām|
prāptekāleca viduṣāṃ tadvijñeyaṃ vicakṣaṇaḥ|
yogināṃ satataṃ yatnāt ariṣṭānyapa(va) nīyate|
sambatsarāntāni tajjñena phaladāni divāniśam|
vilokyāni sadācaiṣāṃ phalayukteṣu bhīruṇā|
vijñāyakāryyaṃ manasi sacakālonareśvaraḥ||
jñātvā kālaṃcataṃ samyak abhayasthānamāśritaḥ|
yuñjīta yogī kālo'sau yathā tasyāphalo bhavet||
dṛṣṭvā'riṣṭaṃ tathā yogī tyaktvā maraṇajaṃ bhayam|
tat svabhāvaṃ tadālocya kāle yāvad yugāgate||
p.280
tasyabhāge tathaivāhvo ryogaṃ yuñjīta yogavid|
pūrvvāhne vā parāhneca madhyāhṇevā dine dine||
yatra vā rajanī bhāge tadā'riṣṭaṃ nirīkṣitam
yatraitāvad sa yuñjīta yāvat prāptaṃhitaddinam||
tatastyaktvā bhayaṃ sarvvaṃ jitvā taṃ kālamātmavit|
tatraivāvasathe sthitvā yatracasthairyyamātmanaḥ||
yuñjīta yogī nirjjitya trīn guṇān paramātmani|
tanmayaścātmanā bhūtvā cidvṛtti mapi santyajet||
tataḥ paramaṃ nirbbāṇaṃ atīndrimagocaram|
yadvuddhe ryannacāsvyātuṃ śakyate tatsamaśrute"||
brahmayāmale—
ākāśe vigatāmbude niśi sitepakṣepunaḥ pratyaham,
nābhau vā padayorlalāṭa phalake hyekāgracittasthiram|
cchāyādhaḥ prathamaṃ pidhāyanayanaṃ paśye ttatastvambaram,
sadyaḥ paśyati tatrakālapuruṣaṃ kṣīrāvdhi kundopamam|

tasyadarśanamātreṇa sarvva pāpaiḥ pramucyate|
sampūrṇañca yadāpaśye ttadāmṛtyu bhayaṃ kutaḥ?||
samprūrṇalakṣaṇopetaṃ yadā paśyati nirmmalam|
tadā na vidyate kālo yāvat sambatasaro dhravam||
śirohīne bhavenmṛtyuḥ ṣaṇmāsānnātrasaṃśayaḥ|
vāhuhīne bhaved vyādhiḥ syādvāsahodarakṣayaḥ||
p.281
vṛkṣe ca (kṛṣṇe ca) dhananāśaḥ syāt yonicchidre mṛtiḥ striyāḥ|
madhye cchidre śastrapāto durbhikṣaṃ vā bhavithyati||
vivāse ca subhikṣaṃ syāt grīvābhaṅge nṛpakṣayaḥ|
pītavarṇe bhavellābhaḥ kṛṣṇavarṇe'pamṛtyukṛt||
raktavarṇe bhavedrājyaṃ pītabhe cirajīvitam|
tasya darśanamātreṇa punar janma na vidyate||

śvetādivarṇarahitaṃ śabdarūpavivarjjitam|
jñāpayanti paraṃ tattvaṃ manovācāmagocaram||
gopite hi harenmṛtyuṃ gurorapi na darśayet|
darśite tu phalaṃ nāsti gupte paśyaṃstu kālahā||"
iti dik|
nididhyāsanametattu mayā sādhanasaṃyutam|
yathāśāstraṃ yathābodhaṃ saṃkṣepeṇa nirūpitam||
śravaṇaṃ mananañcaiva saviśeṣaṃ savistaram|
vedānteṣveva draṣṭavyaṃ noktaṃ visatarabhītitaḥ||
rahasyaṃ rājayogasya haṭhayogasya yat sthitam|
prakāśitaṃ mayā sarvaṃ prītaye yoganāmiha||

sadhrīcīnairasādhyatvāt likhito na mayā hi saḥ|
uddālakabhuśuṇḍādyairhaṭhayogastu yaḥ kṛtaḥ||
p.282
kāpālikapraṇītāstu itikarttavyatādayaḥ|
likhitā na mayā te tu śrutismṛtivirodhanāḥ|

yenārādhi mahādevo gīrau brahma'thavā hariḥ|
lambodaro ravirvāpi sa yogāt siddhim āpnuyāt||
iti śrīparamahaṃsa-parivrājakācārya-śrī-rāmacandrasadānanda-sarasvatī-śiṣya-śrīmacchivānanda-sarasvatī-viracita-yogacintāmaṇau caturthaparicchedaḥ

samāptaś cāyaṃ granthaḥ|
